=== Elevation | <!-- @dsCard group="Foundations" height=470 page subtitle="Four tiers you can read, eight levels you can feel" -->
--- css
.ladder { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(8, minmax(0, 1fr)); gap: 14px; align-items: start; padding: 40px 4px 8px; }
.rung { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 10px; }
.tile { height: 112px; border-radius: var(--radius-lg); display: grid; place-items: center; font: 600 22px/1 var(--font-sans); color: var(--ink-muted); }
.tile[data-level="-2"], .tile[data-level="-1"] { border: 1px solid var(--line); }
.tile[data-level="0"] { outline: 1px dashed var(--line-control); outline-offset: -1px; }
.step { transition: transform var(--dur-travel-3) var(--ease-rise); }
.meta b { display: block; font: 600 13px/18px var(--font-sans); color: var(--ink); }
.meta span { display: block; font: 400 12px/16px var(--font-mono); color: var(--ink-muted); }
.meta p { margin: 4px 0 0; font: 400 12.5px/17px var(--font-sans); color: var(--ink-muted); }
.note { margin: 18px 4px 0; font: 400 13.5px/20px var(--font-sans); color: var(--ink-muted); max-width: 760px; }
.tiers { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(8, minmax(0, 1fr)); gap: 14px; padding: 8px 4px 0; }
.tier { border-top: 2px solid var(--line-control); padding-top: 6px; font: 600 12.5px/17px var(--font-sans); color: var(--ink); }
.tier small { display: block; font: 400 12px/16px var(--font-sans); color: var(--ink-muted); }
--- jsx
const rungs = [
  [-2, 'Trench', 'trench', 'Tracks: slider, switch, progress'],
  [-1, 'Well', 'well', 'Receptacles: inputs, tab track'],
  [0, 'Ground', 'elev-0', 'The page; badges, ghost, disabled'],
  [1, 'Raised', 'elev-1', 'Cards, buttons, thumbs'],
  [2, 'Lifted', 'elev-2', 'Hover; the primary action'],
  [3, 'Floating', 'elev-3', 'Tooltips, menus, popovers'],
  [4, 'Overlay', 'elev-4', 'Toasts, sheets'],
  [5, 'Modal', 'elev-5', 'Dialogs, one at a time'],
];
const tiers = [['Sunk', 'an opening', 2], ['Ground', 'settled', 1], ['Raised', 'live in the page', 2], ['Floating', 'above the page', 3]];
function App() {
  return <>
    <div className="tiers">{tiers.map(([t, m, n]) => <div key={t} className="tier" style={{ gridColumn: 'span ' + n }}>{t}<small>{m}</small></div>)}</div>
    <div className="ladder">
      {rungs.map(([lvl, name, tok, use]) => (
        <div className="rung" key={lvl}>
          <div className="step" style={{ transform: `translateY(${-lvl * 5}px)` }}>
            <A.Surface level={lvl} interactive={lvl >= 0 && lvl < 5} className="tile">{lvl > 0 ? '+' + lvl : lvl}</A.Surface>
          </div>
          <div className="meta"><b>{name}</b><span>--{tok}</span><p>{use}</p></div>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
    <p className="note">The ground is the record. Live things rise above it; openings sink below it. Meaning lives in the four tiers, because adjacent levels inside a tier can't be told apart at a glance. The levels are for motion: hover a tile and it climbs one rung; press it and it returns to the ground.</p>
  </>;
}

=== Motion | <!-- @dsCard group="Foundations" height=400 page subtitle="Duration is distance travelled" -->
--- css
.lanes { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr)); gap: 20px; padding: 8px 4px; }
.lane { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 14px; }
.pad { height: 170px; border-radius: var(--radius-lg); background: var(--surface-well); box-shadow: var(--well); border: 1px solid var(--line); display: grid; place-items: end center; padding: 18px; overflow: hidden; }
.puck { width: 64%; height: 64px; border-radius: var(--radius-md); }
.lane-meta { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; }
.lane-meta b { font: 600 14px/20px var(--font-sans); }
.lane-meta span { font: 400 12px/16px var(--font-mono); color: var(--ink-muted); }
.curves { display: flex; gap: 24px; padding: 18px 4px 0; flex-wrap: wrap; }
.curve { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; font: 400 12px/16px var(--font-mono); color: var(--ink-muted); }
.curve svg { overflow: visible; }
.curve path { fill: none; stroke: var(--accent-ink); stroke-width: 2; stroke-linecap: round; }
.curve rect { fill: none; stroke: var(--line); }
--- jsx
const lanes = [[1, 'dur-travel-1', '140ms', 'Hover lift'], [3, 'dur-travel-3', '220ms', 'Menu, tooltip'], [5, 'dur-travel-5', '300ms', 'Dialog']];
const curves = [['ease-rise', [0.2, 0.9, 0.25, 1]], ['ease-sink', [0.45, 0, 0.8, 0.35]], ['ease-settle', [0.3, 1.3, 0.45, 1]], ['ease-glide', [0.65, 0, 0.35, 1]]];
function Lane({ lvl, tok, ms, use, up }) {
  const pose = up
    ? { transform: `translateY(${-lvl * 14}px)`, boxShadow: `var(--elev-${lvl})`, background: lvl >= 3 ? 'var(--surface-floating)' : 'var(--surface-raised)', transition: `transform var(--${tok}) var(--ease-rise), box-shadow var(--${tok}) var(--ease-rise)` }
    : { transform: 'none', boxShadow: 'var(--elev-0)', background: 'var(--surface-raised)', transition: `transform calc(var(--${tok}) * .7) var(--ease-sink), box-shadow calc(var(--${tok}) * .7) var(--ease-sink)` };
  return (
    <div className="lane">
      <div className="pad"><div className="puck" style={pose} /></div>
      <div className="lane-meta"><b>{lvl} level{lvl > 1 ? 's' : ''} · {use}</b><span>{ms}</span></div>
    </div>
  );
}
function App() {
  const [up, setUp] = React.useState(false);
  React.useEffect(() => { const t = setInterval(() => setUp((u) => !u), 1400); return () => clearInterval(t); }, []);
  return <>
    <div className="lanes">{lanes.map(([l, t, m, u]) => <Lane key={l} lvl={l} tok={t} ms={m} use={u} up={up} />)}</div>
    <div className="curves">
      {curves.map(([name, [a, b, c, d]]) => (
        <div className="curve" key={name}>
          <svg width="36" height="36" viewBox="0 0 36 36"><rect x="0.5" y="0.5" width="35" height="35" rx="6" /><path d={`M4 32 C ${4 + a * 28} ${32 - b * 28}, ${4 + c * 28} ${32 - d * 28}, 32 4`} /></svg>
          {name}
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  </>;
}

// A custom scheme: warm paper grounds and green ink laid over daylight. checkScheme() passes it.
const paper = { name: 'paper', base: 'daylight', tokens: {
  'surface-trench': '#e5e1d2', 'surface-well': '#edeadf', 'surface-ground': '#f5f3ea', 'surface-raised': '#fffdf7',
  'surface-lifted': '#fffdf8', 'surface-floating': '#fffefa', 'surface-overlay': '#fffefb', 'surface-modal': '#fffefc',
  ink: '#1f3329', 'ink-muted': '#4b5d53', 'ink-faint': '#949b8e', line: '#e2ded0', 'line-control': '#76806f',
  accent: '#284d39', 'on-accent': '#ffffff', 'accent-ink': '#284d39', 'accent-soft': '#e1e9dc', positive: '#2c6e3f',
} };
const list = [['daylight'], ['nightfall'], ['daylight', '#0f8a6b'], [paper]];
function App() {
  return (
    <>
      <div className="schemes">
      {list.map(([s, accent], i) => (
        <A.AltitudeProvider key={i} scheme={s} accent={accent} className="shell"><div className="scheme">
          <h4>{typeof s === 'string' ? s[0].toUpperCase() + s.slice(1) : 'Custom'}</h4>
          <small>{accent ? 'accent="' + accent + '"' : typeof s === 'string' ? 'scheme="' + s + '"' : 'scheme={paper}'}</small>
          <A.Button variant="primary" size="sm">Publish</A.Button>
          <A.Input size="sm" placeholder="Search" />
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 10, alignItems: 'center' }}><A.Switch defaultChecked aria-label="Enabled" /><A.Badge tone="accent">Beta</A.Badge></div>
          <div className="series">{[1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6].map((n) => <span key={n} style={{ background: `var(--data-${n})` }} />)}<small>{accent ? 'data-1…6, led by the accent' : 'data-1…6'}</small></div>
        </div></A.AltitudeProvider>
      ))}
      </div>
      {/* Density (#245): 'auto' follows the pointer; 'compact' pins the mouse sizes, 'comfortable' the touch sizes. */}
      <div className="density">
        {['compact', 'comfortable'].map((d) => (
          <A.AltitudeProvider key={d} density={d} className="shell"><div className="scheme">
            <h4>{d === 'compact' ? 'Compact' : 'Comfortable'}</h4>
            <small>density="{d}"</small>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}><A.Button variant="primary">Save</A.Button><A.Button>Cancel</A.Button></div>
            <A.Input placeholder="Search" />
            <A.Tabs defaultValue="a"><A.TabsList aria-label={'Sections, ' + d}><A.TabsTrigger value="a">General</A.TabsTrigger><A.TabsTrigger value="b">Members</A.TabsTrigger></A.TabsList></A.Tabs>
          </div></A.AltitudeProvider>
        ))}
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

        <A.Button variant="danger">Delete project</A.Button>

        <A.Button variant="primary" size="lg">Large</A.Button>
        <A.Button square aria-label="Close"><A.Icons.X /></A.Button>

        <A.Button disabled>Disabled</A.Button>
      </div>
      <p className="cap">Primary rests at level 2, secondary at 1, ghost at 0. Hover climbs one level; pressing returns to the ground. Click Save: a loading button stays pressed.</p>

function App() {

  const [email, setEmail] = React.useState('');
  return (
    <div className="col">
      <div className="grid">
        <A.Input placeholder="Email address" value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} />
        <A.Input defaultValue="mara@northwind.dev" />
        <A.Input placeholder="Search projects" leading={<svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.75" strokeLinecap="round"><circle cx="11" cy="11" r="7"/><path d="M20 20l-3.5-3.5"/></svg>} trailing={<A.Kbd>/</A.Kbd>} />
        <A.Input defaultValue="not-an-email" invalid />
        <div style={{ gridColumn: '1 / -1' }}><A.Textarea value={note} onChange={(e) => setNote(e.target.value)} maxLength={80} placeholder="Leave a note for your team…" /></div>
      </div>
      <p className="cap">An empty field shows its inside wall below the top edge, and deepens as your pointer comes near. Type and the floor rises flush with the page. Invalid drops it to trench depth.</p>
    </div>
  );
}

=== Field | <!-- @dsCard group="Inputs" height=230 subtitle="Label, hint, and an error that emerges" -->

--- jsx
function App() {
  const [v, setV] = React.useState('acme');
  const err = v.length < 5 ? 'Use at least 5 characters.' : null;
  return (
    <div className="grid">
      <A.Field label="Workspace name" hint="Shown to everyone you invite.">
        <A.Input defaultValue="Northwind Studio" />
      </A.Field>
      <A.Field label="URL slug" required error={err}>
        <A.Input value={v} onChange={(e) => setV(e.target.value)} />
      </A.Field>
    </div>
  );
}

=== Checkbox | <!-- @dsCard group="Inputs" height=230 subtitle="A one-position switch" -->
--- css
.family { display: flex; gap: 22px; align-items: center; padding-top: 4px; }
--- jsx
function App() {
  const [a, setA] = React.useState(true);
  const [b, setB] = React.useState(false);
  return (
    <div className="col">
      <A.Checkbox label="Select all" checked={a && b} indeterminate={a !== b} onCheckedChange={(c) => { setA(c); setB(c); }} />
      <div className="col" style={{ paddingLeft: 34 }}>
        <A.Checkbox label="Weekly digest" description="A summary every Monday morning." checked={a} onCheckedChange={setA} />
        <A.Checkbox label="Product updates" checked={b} onCheckedChange={setB} />
      </div>
      <A.Checkbox label="Disabled" disabled defaultChecked />
      <div className="family"><span className="cap">Same build as</span><A.Switch defaultChecked aria-label="Switch" /><A.Switch aria-label="Switch off" /></div>
    </div>
  );
}

=== Badge | <!-- @dsCard group="Display" height=200 subtitle="Indicator plates set into the page" -->

function App() {
  const [deploying, setDeploying] = React.useState(true);
  const [n, setN] = React.useState(3);
  React.useEffect(() => { const t = setInterval(() => { setDeploying((d) => !d); setN((x) => (x % 5) + 1); }, 2400); return () => clearInterval(t); }, []);
  return (
    <div className="col">
      <div className="row">
        <A.Badge lamp>Draft</A.Badge>
        <A.Badge tone="accent">Beta</A.Badge>
        <A.Badge tone="positive">Operational</A.Badge>
        <A.Badge tone="caution">Degraded</A.Badge>
        <A.Badge tone="critical">Outage</A.Badge>
        <A.Badge tone={deploying ? 'accent' : 'positive'} lamp={deploying ? 'pulse' : true}>{deploying ? 'Deploying' : 'Live'}</A.Badge>
      </div>
      <div className="row">
        <A.Badge tone="positive" variant="tinted">Operational</A.Badge>
        <A.Badge tone="critical" variant="tinted">Outage</A.Badge>
        <A.Badge variant="outline">v2.4.0</A.Badge>
        <span className="anchor"><A.Button>Inbox</A.Button><A.Badge variant="count" tone="critical">{n}</A.Badge></span>
      </div>
      <p className="cap">Labels are stamped into the record; status wears a lamp that relights when it changes. A count is the one badge that floats, because it wants you.</p>
    </div>
  );
}

const USER = { name: 'Mara Quist', role: 'Platform engineer, Lisbon', bio: 'Merged “Faster cold starts” into main. Cold starts are down 38% across all regions.' };
// Stands in for useQuery or useSWR: undefined until the data arrives.
function useLoaded(value, ms) {
  const [data, setData] = React.useState(undefined);
  React.useEffect(() => { const t = setTimeout(() => setData(value), ms); return () => clearTimeout(t); }, []);
  return data;
}
function Member() {
  const user = useLoaded(USER, 1200);
  const status = useLoaded('On call', 1800);

    <A.Card className="card">
      <A.CardHeader>
        <div className="who">
          <A.Avatar name={user?.name} size={44} status="active" />
          <div className="id">
            <A.CardTitle>{user?.name}</A.CardTitle>
            <A.CardDescription skeleton={{ lines: 1, width: '70%' }}>{user?.role}</A.CardDescription>
          </div>
          <A.Badge tone="positive">{status}</A.Badge>
        </div>
      </A.CardHeader>
      <A.CardContent skeleton={{ lines: 2 }}>{user?.bio}</A.CardContent>
    </A.Card>
  );
}
function App() {
  const [round, setRound] = React.useState(0);
  React.useEffect(() => { const t = setTimeout(() => setRound((r) => r + 1), 4400); return () => clearTimeout(t); }, [round]);
  return (
    <div className="col">
      <Member key={round} />
      <p className="cap">No skeleton code: while user is undefined, each part imprints itself at its own size, and rises out of it when the data arrives.</p>
    </div>
  );
}

=== LoadingBreath | <!-- @dsCard group="Patterns" height=700 subtitle="Experimental: imprints breathe while they wait, then rise out" -->
--- css

--- jsx
const later = (value, ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(() => r(value), ms));
// Stands in for useQuery or useSWR: undefined until the data arrives.
function useLoaded(value, ms) {
  const [data, setData] = React.useState(undefined);
  React.useEffect(() => { const t = setTimeout(() => setData(value), ms); return () => clearTimeout(t); }, []);
  return data;
}
// Suspends until a promised detail lands, so the card's content imprints only that line.
const Detail = ({ value }) => A.readPromised(value);
function EnvCard({ env }) {
  const e = A.readPromised(env);
  return (
    <A.Card>
      <A.CardHeader>
        <div className="top"><A.CardTitle>{e.name}</A.CardTitle><A.Badge tone={e.tone}>{e.status}</A.Badge></div>
      </A.CardHeader>
      <A.CardContent skeleton={{ lines: 1 }}><Detail value={e.commit} /></A.CardContent>
    </A.Card>
  );
}
function Event({ event }) {
  const [who, text] = A.readPromised(event);
  return <div className="row"><A.Avatar name={who} size={26} /><span><b>{who}</b> {text}</span></div>;
}
const EVENTS = [['Mara Osei', 'promoted a41c9e2 to production'], ['Tomás Nieto', 'started a deploy of c7d2f10 to staging'], ['Aiko Kato', 'deploy of e09b3a4 failed at the build step']];
const OLDER = [['Priya Raman', 'rolled back staging to 9f1e2b7'], ['Mara Osei', 'added an environment variable to preview']];
function View() {
  // G1: the whole card comes from one request.
  const [production] = React.useState(() => later({ name: 'Production', tone: 'positive', status: 'Operational', commit: 'a41c9e2 · Fix invoice rounding' }, 1300));
  // G2: the card is known; its status and commit are still coming, and land together.
  const status = useLoaded('Deploying', 900);
  const [commit] = React.useState(() => later('c7d2f10 · Add bulk export to orders', 1700));
  // G3: a summary first, then the detail inside it.
  const [preview] = React.useState(() => later({ name: 'Preview', tone: 'critical', status: 'Failed', commit: later('e09b3a4 · Try the new pricing table', 1500) }, 700));
  const [feed] = React.useState(() => EVENTS.map((e) => later(e, 1500)));
  const [older, setOlder] = React.useState([]);
  return (
    <div className="col">
      <span className="label">Environments</span>
      <div className="envs">
        <A.Await settle="raised" imprint={<A.Skeleton height={103} radius="var(--radius-lg)" />}><EnvCard env={production} /></A.Await>
        <A.Card>
          <A.CardHeader>
            <div className="top"><A.CardTitle>Staging</A.CardTitle><A.Badge tone="caution">{status}</A.Badge></div>
          </A.CardHeader>
          <A.CardContent skeleton={{ lines: 1 }}><Detail value={commit} /></A.CardContent>
        </A.Card>
        <A.Await settle="raised" imprint={<A.Skeleton height={103} radius="var(--radius-lg)" />}><EnvCard env={preview} /></A.Await>
      </div>
      <span className="label">Recent activity</span>
      <div className="feed">
        <A.RevealGroup>
          {feed.map((e, i) => <A.Await key={i} imprint={<span className="alt-skeleton row-imprint" />}><Event event={e} /></A.Await>)}
        </A.RevealGroup>
        {older.map((e, i) => <A.Await key={'o' + i} late imprint={<span className="alt-skeleton row-imprint" />}><Event event={e} /></A.Await>)}
      </div>
      <div className="bar"><A.Button onClick={() => setOlder((o) => [...o, ...OLDER.map((e) => later(e, 1200))])} disabled={older.length > 0}>Load older events</A.Button></div>
    </div>
  );
}
function App() {
  const [round, setRound] = React.useState(0);
  return (
    <A.AltitudeProvider loading="breath">
      <div className="col">
        <View key={round} />
        <div className="bar"><A.Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setRound((r) => r + 1)}>Load the view again</A.Button></div>
        <p className="cap">Experimental (#343). Each imprint breathes in depth on one page clock, a slow swell down the view. Cards from one request rise a level past their rest and settle; a card's parts land together; rows fill past the ground; older events arrive on a plate.</p>
      </div>
    </A.AltitudeProvider>
  );
}

=== CommentThread | <!-- @dsCard group="Display" height=600 subtitle="Comments on a record, with mentions, posting and resolving" -->
--- css
.ct { max-width: 560px; }
--- jsx
const people = [{ id: 'maya', name: 'Maya Chen' }, { id: 'theo', name: 'Theo Marsh' }, { id: 'ada', name: 'Ada Obi' }];
const ago = (m) => new Date(Date.now() - m * 60000);
function App() {
  const [comments, setComments] = React.useState([
    { id: 'c1', author: { name: 'Theo Marsh' }, body: 'Can we ship this before the release freeze on Friday?', createdAt: ago(90) },
    { id: 'c2', author: { name: 'Maya Chen' }, body: '@Theo Marsh yes, if QA signs off by Thursday.', mentions: ['Theo Marsh'], createdAt: ago(40) },
  ]);
  const [resolved, setResolved] = React.useState(false);
  const [failNext, setFailNext] = React.useState(false);
  const post = (body, mentions) => {
    const id = 'n' + Date.now(), fail = failNext;
    setComments((xs) => [...xs, { id, author: { name: 'You' }, body, mentions: mentions.map((m) => m.name), createdAt: new Date(), status: 'pending', mine: true }]);
    setTimeout(() => setComments((xs) => xs.map((x) => (x.id === id ? { ...x, status: fail ? 'failed' : undefined } : x))), 900);
  };
  return (
    <div className="col ct">
      <A.CommentThread comments={comments} resolved={resolved ? { by: 'You' } : false} onPost={post}
        onResolve={() => setResolved(true)} onReopen={() => setResolved(false)}
        onRetry={(id) => setComments((xs) => xs.map((x) => (x.id === id ? { ...x, status: undefined } : x)))}
        onDelete={(id) => setComments((xs) => xs.map((x) => (x.id === id ? { ...x, deleted: true } : x)))}
        onUndoDelete={(id) => setComments((xs) => xs.map((x) => (x.id === id ? { ...x, deleted: false } : x)))}
        mentionable={(q) => people.filter((p) => p.name.toLowerCase().includes(q.toLowerCase()))} typing={resolved ? [] : ['Ada Obi']} />
      <A.Switch label="Make the next post fail" checked={failNext} onCheckedChange={setFailNext} />
    </div>
  );
}

    { id: 'n4', title: 'Nightly sync failed for Salesforce', body: 'The token expired. Reconnect to resume.', createdAt: ago(60 * 27), read: true, href: '#/integrations' },
  ]);
  const markRead = (ids) => setItems((xs) => xs.map((x) => (ids.includes(x.id) ? { ...x, read: true } : x)));
  return (
    <div className="col">
      <div className="nc">
        <A.Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => setItems((xs) => [{ id: 'n' + Date.now(), title: 'Ada assigned you “Billing page”', createdAt: new Date(), read: false, href: '#/issues/212' }, ...xs])}>Simulate a new notification</A.Button>
        <A.NotificationCenter items={items} onMarkRead={markRead} onMarkAllRead={() => setItems((xs) => xs.map((x) => ({ ...x, read: true })))} preferencesHref="#/settings/notifications" />
      </div>
      <p className="cap">Unread is a dot and a bolder title, never a height. A new notification arrives lifted and settles, and is announced even while the panel is closed.</p>
    </div>
  );
}

=== Dialog | <!-- @dsCard group="Overlays" height=620 subtitle="A focused decision; the background can stay in place" -->
--- css
.dialog-demo { max-width: 720px; margin: auto; display: grid; gap: 20px; }
.dialog-demo h1 { font: 600 24px/1.2 var(--font-sans); margin: 0; }
.dialog-demo h2 { margin: 0; font: 600 19px/1.4 var(--font-sans); }
.dialog-file { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; gap: 12px; padding: 16px 0; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line); }
.dialog-file > div { min-width: 0; overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
.dialog-file p { margin: 4px 0 0; }
.dialog-demo details { border-top: 1px solid var(--line); padding-top: 16px; }

.dialog-demo select { padding: 8px; max-width: 100%; font: inherit; color: var(--ink); background: var(--surface-well); border: 1px solid var(--line-control); border-radius: var(--radius-md); }
.dialog-target { overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
@media (max-width: 420px) { .dialog-file { align-items: flex-start; flex-direction: column; } }
--- jsx
const dialogFiles = [
  { id: 'file-brand', name: 'Brand guidelines.pdf', folder: 'Brand', revision: 1 },
  { id: 'file-brand-draft', name: 'Brand guidelines draft.pdf', folder: 'Brand', revision: 1 },
  { id: 'file-launch', name: 'Launch checklist.md', folder: 'Launch', revision: 1 },
];
function ModalFrameFixture() {
  const [open, setOpen] = React.useState(false), [sheet, setSheet] = React.useState(false);
  React.useEffect(() => { window.altitudeModalFrameFixture = { setOpen, setSheet }; return () => { delete window.altitudeModalFrameFixture; }; }, []);
  return <A.AltitudeProvider><p>Independent document fixture</p><A.Button data-frame-trigger onClick={() => setOpen(true)}>Open frame palette</A.Button>
    <A.CommandPalette open={open} onOpenChange={setOpen} items={[{ items: [{ label: 'Frame command' }] }]} />
    <A.Sheet open={sheet} onOpenChange={setSheet}><A.SheetHeader><A.SheetTitle>Frame sheet</A.SheetTitle></A.SheetHeader><A.Input aria-label="Frame input" /></A.Sheet>
  </A.AltitudeProvider>;
}
function App() {
  const altitude = A.useAltitude();
  const releaseLegacy = React.useRef(null);
  const [rows, setRows] = React.useState(dialogFiles);
  const server = React.useRef(dialogFiles.map(file => ({ ...file })));
  const heading = React.useRef(null);
  const [target, setTarget] = React.useState(null);
  const [open, setOpen] = React.useState(false);
  const opened = React.useRef(false); opened.current = open;
  const [background, setBackground] = React.useState('stationary');
  const [fixture, setFixture] = React.useState(new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('fixture') || 'success-100');
  const [operation, setOperation] = React.useState(null);
  const [outcome, setOutcome] = React.useState('');
  const [present, setPresent] = React.useState(true);
  const session = React.useRef(0), sequence = React.useRef(0), pending = React.useRef(new Set());
  const failUsed = React.useRef(false), accepted = React.useRef([]);
  const invocation = React.useRef(null), mounted = React.useRef(false), epoch = React.useRef(0);
  const [frame, setFrame] = React.useState(false);
  const frameRoot = React.useRef(null);
  const [mode, setMode] = React.useState('');
  const [sheet, setSheet] = React.useState(false), [nonmodal, setNonmodal] = React.useState(false);
  const [palette, setPalette] = React.useState(false), [competing, setCompeting] = React.useState(false);
  const [controls, setControls] = React.useState(true), [inserted, setInserted] = React.useState(false);
  const [nestedPresent, setNestedPresent] = React.useState(true);
  const [nested, setNested] = React.useState(false), [toastActions, setToastActions] = React.useState(0);
  const latest = React.useRef({});
  React.useEffect(() => { mounted.current = true; return () => { mounted.current = false; epoch.current++; }; }, []);
  const begin = (file, invoker) => {
    if (pending.current.has(file.id)) { setOutcome(`Deletion is already in progress for “${file.name}”.`); return; }
    session.current++; invocation.current = invoker || document.activeElement;
    setTarget({ ...file, session: session.current }); setOperation(null); setOpen(true); setPresent(true);
  };
  const finish = (captured, id, result, ownEpoch) => {
    pending.current.delete(captured.id);
    if (!mounted.current || ownEpoch !== epoch.current) return;
    const matching = session.current === captured.session;
    if (result === 'success') {
      const restoreRemoved = !opened.current && document.activeElement === invocation.current && matching;
      setRows(server.current.map(file => ({ ...file })));
      setOutcome(`Deleted “${captured.name}”.`);
      if (matching) { setOperation({ id, session: captured.session, state: 'done' }); setOpen(false); }
      if (restoreRemoved) requestAnimationFrame(() => heading.current?.focus({ preventScroll: true }));
    } else {
      const message = result === 'conflict' ? 'This file changed. Close this dialog and review the latest file before deleting.' : 'The file was not deleted. Try again.';
      if (matching && opened.current) setOperation({ id, session: captured.session, state: result, message, current: server.current.find(file => file.id === captured.id) });
      else setOutcome(`“${captured.name}”: ${message}`);
    }
  };
  const confirm = async () => {
    if (!target || pending.current.has(target.id)) return;
    const captured = { ...target }, id = `delete-${++sequence.current}`, ownEpoch = epoch.current, behavior = fixture;
    pending.current.add(captured.id); setOperation({ id, session: captured.session, state: 'pending' });
    const request = { id: captured.id, expectedRevision: captured.revision, operationId: id, outcome: 'pending' };
    accepted.current.push(request);
    const reject = behavior === 'fail-once' && !failUsed.current;
    if (reject) failUsed.current = true;
    if (behavior === 'unmount') setTimeout(() => { if (epoch.current === ownEpoch && mounted.current) { setPresent(false); setOpen(false); } }, 50);
    if (behavior === 'interrupted-after-save') {
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 100));
      if (ownEpoch !== epoch.current) return;
      const record = server.current.find(file => file.id === captured.id);
      if (record?.revision === captured.revision) server.current = server.current.filter(file => file.id !== captured.id);
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 1100));
    } else await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, behavior === 'success-1200' || reject || behavior === 'conflict' ? 1200 : 100));
    if (ownEpoch !== epoch.current) return;
    if (behavior === 'conflict') { server.current = server.current.map(file => file.id === captured.id ? { ...file, name: 'Brand guidelines revised.pdf', revision: file.revision + 1 } : file); setRows(server.current); }
    const current = server.current.find(file => file.id === captured.id);
    let result = !current || current.revision !== captured.revision ? 'conflict' : reject || behavior === 'interrupted-before-save' ? 'failure' : 'success';
    if (result === 'success') server.current = server.current.filter(file => file.id !== captured.id);
    if (behavior.startsWith('interrupted')) {
      if (mounted.current && session.current === captured.session && opened.current) setOperation({ id, session: captured.session, state: 'checking' });
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 200));
      result = server.current.some(file => file.id === captured.id) ? 'failure' : 'success';
    }
    request.outcome = result;
    finish(captured, id, result, ownEpoch);
  };
  const reset = () => {
    epoch.current++; sequence.current = 0; failUsed.current = false; accepted.current = []; pending.current.clear(); server.current = dialogFiles.map(file => ({ ...file }));
    setRows(server.current); setOpen(false); setOutcome(''); setOperation(null); setPresent(true); setMode(''); setSheet(false); setNonmodal(false); setPalette(false); setCompeting(false); setNested(false);
  };
  latest.current = { legacyVote: () => { releaseLegacy.current = altitude.pushLayer(); }, legacyRelease: () => releaseLegacy.current?.(), begin, reset, setFrame, setMode, setSheet, setNonmodal, setPalette, setCompeting, setNested, setNestedPresent, setControls, setInserted, setPresent, setBackground, setFixture,
    close: () => setOpen(false), confirm, openFirst: () => begin(server.current[0]),
    changeTarget: () => { server.current = server.current.map(file => file.id === target?.id ? { ...file, name: file.name.replace('.pdf', ' revised.pdf'), revision: file.revision + 1 } : file); setRows(server.current); },
    notify: () => A.toast({ title: 'Background update', duration: 1200, action: { label: 'Undo background change', onClick: () => setToastActions(n => n + 1) } }),
    snapshot: () => ({ rows, operation, requests: sequence.current, accepted: accepted.current, toastActions, session: session.current, open }) };
  React.useEffect(() => {
    window.altitudeModalFixture = new Proxy({}, { get: (_, key) => latest.current[key] });
    return () => { delete window.altitudeModalFixture; };
  }, []);
  const busy = operation?.session === target?.session && ['pending', 'checking'].includes(operation?.state);
  const failed = operation?.session === target?.session && ['failure', 'conflict'].includes(operation?.state);
  return <div className="dialog-demo">
    <header><p className="cap">Files / Archived</p><h1 ref={heading} tabIndex={-1}>Archived files</h1></header>
    <p className="body">Archived files can be restored. Permanently deleted files cannot.</p>
    <section aria-label="Archived files">{rows.map(file => <div className="dialog-file" key={file.id}>
      <div><strong>{file.name}</strong><p className="cap">{file.folder}</p></div>
      <A.Button data-file={file.id} onClick={event => begin(file, event.currentTarget)}>Delete permanently</A.Button>
    </div>)}</section>
    <p role="status" data-outcome>{outcome}</p>
    {present && <A.Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={setOpen} background={background} size="sm" title="Delete this file permanently?"
      description={`“${target?.name || ''}” will be removed. You won’t be able to restore it.`} focusSelector="[data-cancel]" returnFocus={() => heading.current}
      footer={<><A.Button data-cancel variant="ghost" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>{busy ? 'Close' : 'Cancel'}</A.Button>
        <A.Button data-confirm variant="danger" disabled={busy || operation?.state === 'conflict'} onClick={confirm}>{busy ? operation?.state === 'checking' ? 'Checking deletion…' : 'Deleting…' : failed ? 'Try deleting again' : 'Delete permanently'}</A.Button></>}>
      <p className="body dialog-target">{target?.name}<br /><span className="cap">{target?.folder}</span></p>
      {busy && <p role="status">Deletion is in progress. Closing this dialog won’t cancel it.</p>}
      {failed && <A.Alert tone="critical" title="File not deleted">{operation.message}{operation.state === 'conflict' && <p>Latest file: {operation.current?.name || 'No longer available'}</p>}</A.Alert>}
    </A.Dialog>}
    <details><summary>Demo controls</summary>
      <div className="row"><label>Background <select aria-label="Dialog background" value={background} onChange={event => setBackground(event.target.value)}><option value="stationary">Stationary</option><option value="recede">Default recession</option></select></label>
      <label>Service fixture <select aria-label="Service fixture" value={fixture} onChange={event => setFixture(event.target.value)}><option value="success-100">Success · 100 ms</option><option value="success-1200">Slow success · 1,200 ms</option><option value="fail-once">Fail once, then retry</option><option value="conflict">Record changes before save</option><option value="interrupted-after-save">Connection interrupted after save</option><option value="interrupted-before-save">Connection interrupted before save</option><option value="unmount">Unmount presentation during write</option></select></label>
      <A.Button onClick={reset}>Reset fixtures</A.Button></div>
      <div className="row"><A.Button onClick={() => { setControls(true); setMode('title'); }}>Read a structured dialog</A.Button><A.Button onClick={() => setMode('field')}>Edit in a dialog</A.Button><A.Button onClick={() => setMode('panel')}>Panel-only dialog</A.Button><A.Button onClick={() => setSheet(true)}>Open modal sheet</A.Button><A.Button onClick={() => setNonmodal(true)}>Open nonmodal sheet</A.Button><A.Button onClick={() => setPalette(true)}>Open command palette</A.Button><A.Button onClick={() => setNested(true)}>Open nested-provider dialog</A.Button></div>
      <p className="cap">Requests: {sequence.current}. Accepted operation log: <output data-operation-log>{JSON.stringify(accepted.current)}</output></p>
      <p className="cap">Deterministic fictional data. Pending close, failure, conflict, unmount and ownership cases are exercised by the canonical regression harness. <a href="https://github.com/richlaconte/altitude/blob/main/build/previews.txt">Example source</a> · <a href="../../ActionsInPlace.md">Actions in place recipe</a></p>
    <button data-background-control onClick={() => setOutcome('Background action')}>Background control</button>
    {inserted && <button data-late-background>New background control</button>}
      {frame && <iframe srcDoc="<!doctype html><html><head></head><body></body></html>" title="Independent document" style={{ width: '100%', height: 360 }} onLoad={event => {
        const doc = event.currentTarget.contentDocument;
        for (const style of document.querySelectorAll('style')) doc.head.append(style.cloneNode(true));
        frameRoot.current = ReactDOM.createRoot(doc.body); frameRoot.current.render(<ModalFrameFixture />);
      }} />}
    </details>
    <A.Dialog open={!!mode} onOpenChange={value => { if (!value) setMode(''); }} title="Modal focus fixture" initialFocus={mode === 'title' ? 'title' : mode === 'panel' ? false : 'first-field'} dismissible={mode !== 'panel'} background={background}
      footer={mode !== 'panel' && controls ? <><button data-first>First control</button><button data-last>Last control</button></> : null}>
      {mode === 'field' && controls && <A.Input aria-label="Fixture field" />}<button hidden>Hidden control</button><button disabled>Disabled control</button>
    </A.Dialog>
    <A.Dialog open={competing} onOpenChange={setCompeting} title="Competing summit" />
    <A.Sheet open={sheet} onOpenChange={setSheet}><A.SheetHeader><A.SheetTitle>File details</A.SheetTitle></A.SheetHeader><A.Button data-sheet-dialog onClick={() => setMode('title')}>Read details in dialog</A.Button></A.Sheet>
    <A.Sheet open={nonmodal} onOpenChange={setNonmodal} modal={false}><A.SheetHeader><A.SheetTitle>File filters</A.SheetTitle></A.SheetHeader><A.Button data-nonmodal-close onClick={() => setNonmodal(false)}>Close filters</A.Button></A.Sheet>
    <A.CommandPalette open={palette} onOpenChange={setPalette} items={[{ items: [{ label: 'Read file details', onSelect: () => setMode('title') }] }]} />
    {nestedPresent && <A.AltitudeProvider scheme="nightfall" recede={false}><div data-nested-background hidden>Nested workspace</div><A.Dialog open={nested} onOpenChange={setNested} title="Nested dialog" initialFocus="title" /></A.AltitudeProvider>}
    <A.Toaster />
  </div>;
}

const msgs = [

const names = ['Atlas', 'Beacon', 'Cinder', 'Delta', 'Ember', 'Fable', 'Garnet', 'Harbor'];
function App() {
  const [rev, setRev] = React.useState(3);
  const [status, setStatus] = React.useState('committed');
  const [q, setQ] = React.useState('ar');
  const save = (fail) => { setStatus('pending'); setTimeout(() => setStatus(fail ? 'failed' : 'committed'), 1800); };
  return (
    <div className="grid">
      <div className="cell">
        <h4>Recency · afterglow</h4>
        <p className="cap">Changed content arrives lifted and tinted, then settles to the ground over 8s. No badge needed to say what just changed.</p>
        <A.Surface level={1} className="card" settle={rev}><div><b>Northwind · production</b><br /><span className="sub">build #{rev}</span></div><A.Button size="sm" onClick={() => setRev(rev + 1)}>Deploy</A.Button></A.Surface>
      </div>
      <div className="cell">
        <h4>Commitment · pending, landed, failed</h4>
        <p className="cap">An optimistic write hovers above the ground until the server confirms, then lands. A failure drops into a trench.</p>
        <A.Surface level={1} className="card" status={status}><div><b>Rename to “Northwind 2”</b><br /><span className="sub">{status}</span></div>
          <span style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 6 }}><A.Button size="sm" onClick={() => save(false)}>Save</A.Button><A.Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => save(true)}>Save (fail)</A.Button></span></A.Surface>
      </div>
      <div className="cell">
        <h4>Possession · held by someone</h4>
        <p className="cap">A collaborator editing a card is holding it: it's lifted out of the page, wearing their colour and name.</p>
        <A.Surface level={1} className="card" heldBy={{ name: 'Mara', presence: 1 }}><div><b>Pricing page copy</b><br /><span className="sub">editing now</span></div></A.Surface>
      </div>
      <div className="cell">
        <h4>Relevance · focus+context</h4>
        <A.Input size="sm" value={q} onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)} placeholder="Filter" />
        <div className="tiles">{names.map((n) => <A.Surface key={n} level={1} className="tile" relevance={!q ? undefined : n.toLowerCase().includes(q.toLowerCase()) ? 'match' : 'miss'}>{n}</A.Surface>)}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

=== Contrast | <!-- @dsCard group="Foundations" height=300 page subtitle="Elevation for people who can't see shadows" -->

function Ladder() {
  return <>
    <div className="row5">{[-2, -1, 0, 1, 2, 3, 5].map((l) => <A.Surface key={l} level={l} className="t">{l}</A.Surface>)}</div>
    <div className="ctl"><A.Button variant="primary" size="sm">Publish</A.Button><A.Button size="sm">Preview</A.Button><A.Input size="sm" placeholder="Search" style={{ width: 104 }} /><A.Switch defaultChecked aria-label="On" /></div>
  </>;
}
function App() {
  return (
    <div className="pair">
      <div className="half"><h4>Default</h4><Ladder /></div>

=== ChartContainer | <!-- @dsCard group="Charts" height=430 subtitle="The frame every chart shares: scales, axes, the ground line and colour slots" -->

--- jsx
// Until the chart types land, these marks are drawn by hand with useChart(): the same scales the types will use.
const usd = new Intl.NumberFormat('en', { style: 'currency', currency: 'USD', notation: 'compact' });
const months = [
  { month: '2026-01-01', revenue: 8200, costs: 5100 }, { month: '2026-02-01', revenue: 8900, costs: 5000 },
  { month: '2026-03-01', revenue: 9400, costs: 5300 }, { month: '2026-04-01', revenue: 9100, costs: 5200 },
  { month: '2026-05-01', revenue: 10600, costs: 5400 }, { month: '2026-06-01', revenue: 11800, costs: 5300 },
];
const regions = [
  { region: 'North', q2: 42, q3: 51 }, { region: 'South', q2: 35, q3: 31 }, { region: 'East', q2: 28, q3: 37 }, { region: 'West', q2: 19, q3: 24 },
];
// encode() draws a series by its slot: colour, and a dash, marker or hatch whenever patterns are on (forced colours, print).
function Lines() {
  const { series, data, xOf, y, encode, patterns, strokeWidth } = A.useChart();
  return series.map((s) => {
    const e = encode(s.key);
    return <g key={s.key}>
      <polyline fill="none" stroke={e.stroke} strokeDasharray={e.dash} strokeWidth={strokeWidth} strokeLinejoin="round" strokeLinecap="round"
        points={data.map((d) => `${xOf(d)},${y(d[s.key])}`).join(' ')} />
      {patterns && data.map((d, i) => <A.ChartMarker key={i} shape={e.marker} x={xOf(d)} y={y(d[s.key])} color={e.stroke} />)}
    </g>;
  });
}
function Bars() {
  const { series, data, x, y, xKey, ground, alpha, encode, gap } = A.useChart();
  const w = (x.bandwidth - gap * (series.length - 1)) / series.length;
  return data.map((d) => series.map((s, i) => {
    const v = d[s.key], top = Math.min(y(v), ground), e = encode(s.key);
    return <rect key={d[xKey] + s.key} x={x(d[xKey]) + i * (w + gap)} y={top} width={w} height={Math.abs(y(v) - ground)}
      fill={e.fill} stroke={e.edge} strokeWidth={e.edge ? 1 : undefined} opacity={alpha(d, s.key)} />;
  }));
}
function App() {
  return (
    <div className="charts">
      <A.Card>
        <A.CardHeader><A.CardTitle id="rc">Revenue and costs</A.CardTitle><A.CardDescription>Jan to Jun 2026</A.CardDescription></A.CardHeader>
        <A.CardContent>
          <A.ChartContainer aria-labelledby="rc" data={months} x="month" aspect={1.7} summary="Revenue rose 44% while costs held flat."
            config={{ revenue: { label: 'Revenue', format: usd.format }, costs: { label: 'Costs', format: usd.format } }}>
            <Lines />
          </A.ChartContainer>
        </A.CardContent>
      </A.Card>
      <A.Card>
        <A.CardHeader><A.CardTitle id="dr">Deals closed by region</A.CardTitle><A.CardDescription>Q2 and Q3</A.CardDescription></A.CardHeader>
        <A.CardContent>
          <A.ChartContainer aria-labelledby="dr" data={regions} x="region" aspect={1.7}
            config={{ q2: { label: 'Q2' }, q3: { label: 'Q3' } }}>
            <Bars />
          </A.ChartContainer>
        </A.CardContent>
      </A.Card>
    </div>
  );
}

=== ChartMotion | <!-- @dsCard group="Charts" height=430 subtitle="Data that changes glides at one level; a rescale comes first" -->

// Every chart moves by ChartContainer's one engine. This BarChart cycles through the changes it handles.
const steps = [
  ['Q2', [['North', 42], ['South', 35], ['East', 28], ['West', 19]]],
  ['Q3: values change', [['North', 51], ['South', 31], ['East', 37], ['West', 24]]],
  ['Q4: the axis rescales first', [['North', 88], ['South', 64], ['East', 71], ['West', 40]]],
  ['Q4, by value: bars are carried', [['North', 88], ['East', 71], ['South', 64], ['West', 40]]],
  ['A region enters', [['North', 88], ['East', 71], ['South', 64], ['West', 40], ['Central', 22]]],
  ['…and leaves', [['North', 88], ['East', 71], ['South', 64], ['West', 40]]],
];
function App() {
  const [i, setI] = React.useState(0);
  React.useEffect(() => { const t = setInterval(() => setI((n) => (n + 1) % steps.length), 2400); return () => clearInterval(t); }, []);
  const data = React.useMemo(() => steps[i][1].map(([region, deals]) => ({ region, deals })), [i]);
  return (
    <div className="cm">
      <A.Card>
        <A.CardHeader><A.CardTitle id="cm-t">Deals closed by region</A.CardTitle><A.CardDescription>{steps[i][0]}</A.CardDescription></A.CardHeader>
        <A.CardContent>
          <A.ChartContainer aria-labelledby="cm-t" aspect={2} config={{ deals: { label: 'Deals' } }}><A.BarChart data={data} x="region" /></A.ChartContainer>
        </A.CardContent>
      </A.Card>
      <ol>{steps.map(([label], n) => <li key={n} data-on={n === i ? '' : undefined}>{label}</li>)}</ol>
    </div>
  );
}

          { key: 'errors', header: 'Error rate, 14 days', cell: (r) => {

            return <span className="row">{r.errors[r.errors.length - 1]}%<A.Sparkline data={r.errors} tone={rising ? 'critical' : 'neutral'} format={(v) => `${v}%`} aria-label={`${r.name} error rate`} />{rising && <A.Badge tone="critical">Rising</A.Badge>}</span>;
          } },
        ]} />
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

=== SyncIndicator | <!-- @dsCard group="Display" height=440 subtitle="Whether your work has reached the server, said in words on a flush plate" -->
--- css
.si { display: grid; grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) 220px; gap: 20px; align-items: start; }
.si .bar { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; margin-bottom: 8px; font: 600 15px/20px var(--font-sans); }
.si .change { padding: 8px 10px; border-radius: var(--radius-md); font: 400 13px/18px var(--font-sans); }
@media (max-width: 720px) { .si { grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr); } }
--- jsx
function App() {
  const [text, setText] = React.useState('Q4 goals: grow self-serve revenue 20% and halve time to first value.');
  const [state, setState] = React.useState('saved');
  const [at, setAt] = React.useState(() => new Date(Date.now() - 120000));
  const [offline, setOffline] = React.useState(false);
  const [waiting, setWaiting] = React.useState([]);
  const timer = React.useRef();
  const edit = (v) => {
    setText(v);
    if (offline) { setWaiting((w) => [...w.slice(-2), 'Edited the goals paragraph']); setState('offline'); return; }
    setState('saving'); clearTimeout(timer.current);
    timer.current = setTimeout(() => { setState('saved'); setAt(new Date()); }, 700);
  };
  return (
    <div className="si">
      <div>
        <div className="bar"><span>Q4 planning</span>
          <A.SyncIndicator state={state} pending={waiting.length} lastSyncedAt={at} conflicts={1} onRetry={() => { setState('saving'); setTimeout(() => { setState('saved'); setAt(new Date()); }, 700); }}
            onOpenConflicts={() => setState('saved')}
            queue={waiting.length ? waiting.map((w, i) => <A.Surface key={i} status="pending" className="change">{w}</A.Surface>) : undefined} />
        </div>
        <A.Textarea value={text} onChange={(e) => edit(e.target.value)} aria-label="Q4 goals" />
      </div>
      <div className="col">
        <A.Switch label="Offline" checked={offline} onCheckedChange={(on) => { setOffline(on); if (!on) { setWaiting([]); setState('saving'); setTimeout(() => { setState('saved'); setAt(new Date()); }, 700); } }} />
        <A.Button size="sm" onClick={() => setState('failed')}>Make a save fail</A.Button>
        <A.Button size="sm" onClick={() => setState('conflict')}>Cause a conflict</A.Button>
        <p className="cap">Type to save. Go offline and the plate counts what's waiting; press it to see those changes, each hovering until it lands.</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

=== Heatmap | <!-- @dsCard group="Charts" height=980 subtitle="A value per cell across two dimensions: flat steps of one ramp, each named in words" -->
--- css
.hm { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr)); gap: 16px; align-items: start; }
.hm > .wide { grid-column: 1 / -1; }
@media (max-width: 720px) { .hm { grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr); } }
--- jsx
const days = ['Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat', 'Sun'];
// Requests per hour: a working-day hump, quieter weekends, and a Tuesday-afternoon peak.
const load = days.flatMap((weekday, w) => Array.from({ length: 24 }, (_, hour) => {
  const day = Math.max(0, Math.sin(((hour - 6) / 15) * Math.PI));
  const base = (w < 5 ? 300 : 110) * day + 20 + ((hour * 7 + w * 13) % 17);
  return { weekday, hour, requests: Math.round(base + (w === 1 && hour >= 13 && hour <= 15 ? 160 : 0)) };
}));
// Commits per day for a year, with quiet weekends, a holiday gap and a few busy weeks.
const start = Date.UTC(2025, 9, 27);
const commits = Array.from({ length: 365 }, (_, i) => {
  const t = start + i * 864e5, wd = new Date(t).getUTCDay(), r = (i * 37 + 11) % 23;
  const busy = (i > 120 && i < 140) || (i > 300 && i < 312);
  const n = wd === 0 || wd === 6 ? (r < 18 ? 0 : r - 17) : i > 56 && i < 68 ? 0 : busy ? 6 + (r % 9) : r < 5 ? 0 : Math.round(r / 3);
  return { day: new Date(t).toISOString().slice(0, 10), count: n };
});
const services = ['Checkout', 'Search', 'Accounts', 'Media', 'Payments'];
const regions = ['US', 'EU', 'APAC', 'LATAM'];
// Error rate against last week, in points: below zero is better.
const change = services.flatMap((service, s) => regions.map((region, r) => ({ service, region, delta: s === 3 && r === 3 ? null : Math.round((Math.sin(s * 2.1 + r * 1.3) * 1.6 + (s === 4 && r === 1 ? 1.8 : 0)) * 10) / 10 })));
const pts = (v) => (v > 0 ? '+' : v < 0 ? '−' : '') + Math.abs(v).toFixed(1) + ' pts';
function App() {
  const [cell, setCell] = React.useState(null);
  return (
    <div className="hm">
      <A.Card className="wide">
        <A.CardHeader><A.CardTitle id="h1">Commits</A.CardTitle><A.CardDescription>A year, a cell per day: zero is the lightest step, and each step says its range</A.CardDescription></A.CardHeader>
        <A.CardContent>
          <A.ChartContainer aria-labelledby="h1" config={{ count: { label: 'Commits' } }}>
            <A.Heatmap variant="calendar" data={commits} date="day" value="count" bins={[0, 1, 4, 10]} />
          </A.ChartContainer>
        </A.CardContent>
      </A.Card>
      <A.Card>
        <A.CardHeader><A.CardTitle id="h2">Requests by hour</A.CardTitle><A.CardDescription>Load by weekday and hour, with the table view</A.CardDescription></A.CardHeader>
        <A.CardContent>
          <A.ChartContainer aria-labelledby="h2" views config={{ requests: { label: 'Requests' } }} xLabel="Weekday">
            <A.Heatmap data={load} x="hour" y="weekday" value="requests" xFormat={(h) => String(h).padStart(2, '0')} />
          </A.ChartContainer>
        </A.CardContent>
      </A.Card>
      <A.Card>
        <A.CardHeader><A.CardTitle id="h3">Error rate against last week</A.CardTitle><A.CardDescription>{cell ? `${cell.service} in ${cell.region}: select it again to clear` : 'scale="diverging": better below zero. Select a cell'}</A.CardDescription></A.CardHeader>
        <A.CardContent>
          <A.ChartContainer aria-labelledby="h3" config={{ delta: { label: 'Change', format: pts } }}>
            <A.Heatmap data={change} x="region" y="service" value="delta" scale="diverging" selected={cell} onSelect={setCell} />
          </A.ChartContainer>
        </A.CardContent>
      </A.Card>
    </div>
  );
}

const svc = ['Checkout', 'Search', 'Media'];
// p95 latency against payload per endpoint: latency climbs with payload, Media's big payloads most of all.
const endpoints = Array.from({ length: 30 }, (_, i) => {
  const service = svc[i % 3], kb = Math.round(8 + ((i * 53) % 97) * (service === 'Media' ? 4.1 : 2.2));
  const ms = Math.round(70 + kb * (service === 'Media' ? 0.62 : 0.48) + ((i * 37) % 41) - 20 + (i === 13 ? 190 : 0));
  return { name: `${service} ${['list', 'get', 'create', 'update', 'search', 'export', 'upload', 'sync', 'batch', 'stream'][Math.floor(i / 3)]}`, service, kb, ms };
});
const kb = (v) => `${Math.round(v)} KB`, ms = (v) => `${Math.round(v)} ms`;
const usd = new Intl.NumberFormat('en', { style: 'currency', currency: 'USD', notation: 'compact' });
const month = (m) => Array.from({ length: 16 }, (_, i) => {
  const seats = 20 + ((i * 29) % 140) + m * (i % 4) * 6;
  return { id: 'c' + i, name: ['Acme', 'Globex', 'Initech', 'Umbrella', 'Hooli', 'Vandelay', 'Stark', 'Wayne', 'Wonka', 'Tyrell', 'Cyberdyne', 'Soylent', 'Aperture', 'Massive', 'Oscorp', 'Gringotts'][i], seats, spend: Math.round(seats * (38 + ((i * 17) % 23)) + m * 300) };
});
function App() {
  const [m, setM] = React.useState(0);
  const [picked, setPicked] = React.useState(null);
  const customers = React.useMemo(() => month(m), [m]);
  return (
    <div className="sc">
      <A.Card>
        <A.CardHeader><A.CardTitle id="sc1">Latency against payload</A.CardTitle><A.CardDescription>p95 per endpoint, by service, with the trend</A.CardDescription></A.CardHeader>
        <A.CardContent>
          <A.ChartContainer aria-labelledby="sc1" aspect={1.5} config={{ kb: { label: 'Payload', format: kb }, ms: { label: 'p95', format: ms } }}>
            <A.ScatterChart data={endpoints} x="kb" y="ms" group="service" id="name" name="name" label={(d) => (d.ms > 250 && d.kb < 300 ? d.name : null)} trend>
              <A.ChartLegend content={<A.ChartLegendContent toggle label="Services" />} />
            </A.ScatterChart>
          </A.ChartContainer>
        </A.CardContent>
      </A.Card>
      <A.Card>
        <A.CardHeader><A.CardTitle id="sc2">Spend against seats</A.CardTitle><A.CardDescription>{picked ? `${picked.name} selected: the rest recede` : 'Per customer. Select one; dots glide by id when the month changes'}</A.CardDescription></A.CardHeader>
        <A.CardContent>
          <A.ChartContainer aria-labelledby="sc2" aspect={1.5} views config={{ seats: { label: 'Seats' }, spend: { label: 'Spend', format: usd.format } }}>
            <A.ScatterChart data={customers} x="seats" y="spend" id="id" name="name" selected={picked ? picked.id : null} onSelect={setPicked} />
          </A.ChartContainer>
          <div className="sc-foot"><A.Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => setM((n) => (n + 1) % 3)}>Next month</A.Button><span>Month {m + 1} of 3</span></div>
        </A.CardContent>
      </A.Card>
    </div>
  );
}

=== ChartLive | <!-- @dsCard group="Charts" height=640 subtitle="Live data: changes leave a fading trace, a window glides, the plot holds still while you read" -->
--- css
.cl { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr)); gap: 16px; }
@media (max-width: 720px) { .cl { grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr); } }
--- jsx
const t0 = Date.UTC(2026, 9, 28, 12, 0);
const wave = (i) => +(1.3 + 0.45 * Math.sin(i / 2.2) + (i % 9 === 8 ? 0.95 : 0)).toFixed(2);
const pct = (v) => `${v.toFixed(2)}%`;
const names = ['Ingest', 'Render', 'Email', 'Export', 'Billing'];
const start = names.map((queue, i) => ({ queue, depth: [42, 18, 64, 27, 9][i] }));
function App() {
  const [rates, setRates] = React.useState(() => Array.from({ length: 16 }, (_, i) => ({ t: t0 + i * 60e3, rate: wave(i) })));
  const [n, setN] = React.useState(16);
  const [queues, setQueues] = React.useState(start);
  React.useEffect(() => {
    const id = setInterval(() => setN((k) => k + 1), 2000);
    return () => clearInterval(id);
  }, []);
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (n === 16) return;
    setRates((r) => [...r.slice(-40), { t: t0 + (n - 1) * 60e3, rate: wave(n - 1) }]);
  }, [n]);
  const bump = () => setQueues((q) => q.map((r, i) => (i === 2 ? { ...r, depth: r.depth + 11 } : r)));
  const reload = () => setQueues((q) => q.map((r, i) => ({ ...r, depth: r.depth === [30, 25, 51, 36, 14][i] ? r.depth + 3 : [30, 25, 51, 36, 14][i] })));
  return (
    <div className="cl">
      <A.Card>
        <A.CardHeader><A.CardTitle id="lv1">Error rate</A.CardTitle><A.CardDescription>The last 15 minutes, a point a minute</A.CardDescription></A.CardHeader>
        <A.CardContent>
          <A.ChartContainer aria-labelledby="lv1" aspect={1.9} config={{ rate: { label: 'Errors', format: pct } }} live pausable
            announce={(d, prev) => { const a = prev?.at(-1)?.rate, b = d.at(-1)?.rate; return a !== undefined && b !== undefined && a < 2 && b >= 2 ? `Error rate crossed 2%: ${pct(b)}` : null; }}>
            <A.LineChart data={rates} x="t" window={{ minutes: 15 }}><A.ChartReference y={2} label="Alert 2%" tone="caution" /></A.LineChart>
          </A.ChartContainer>
        </A.CardContent>
      </A.Card>
      <A.Card>
        <A.CardHeader><A.CardTitle id="lv2">Queue depth</A.CardTitle><A.CardDescription>One change glows; a whole refresh is one change</A.CardDescription></A.CardHeader>
        <A.CardContent>
          <A.ChartContainer aria-labelledby="lv2" aspect={1.9} config={{ depth: { label: 'Jobs waiting' } }} live>
            <A.BarChart data={queues} x="queue" />
          </A.ChartContainer>
          <div className="row" style={{ marginTop: 12 }}>
            <A.Button size="sm" onClick={bump}>Add jobs to Email</A.Button>
            <A.Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={reload}>Refresh all</A.Button>
          </div>
        </A.CardContent>
      </A.Card>
    </div>
  );
}

function App() {
  const [said, setSaid] = React.useState('');
  const [deploying, setDeploying] = React.useState(false);
  const deploy = (where) => { setSaid(`Deploying to ${where}…`); setDeploying(true); setTimeout(() => { setDeploying(false); setSaid(`Deployed to ${where}.`); }, 1600); };
  return (
    <div className="col">
      <div className="row">
        <A.SplitButton label="Save changes" menuLabel="More save options" onClick={() => setSaid('Saved.')}
          items={[{ label: 'Save as draft', onSelect: () => setSaid('Saved as a draft.') }, { label: 'Save and close', onSelect: () => setSaid('Saved and closed.') }]} />
        <A.SplitButton variant="secondary" label="Merge" menuLabel="More merge options" onClick={() => setSaid('Merged.')}
          items={[{ label: 'Squash and merge', onSelect: () => setSaid('Squashed and merged.') }, { label: 'Rebase and merge', onSelect: () => setSaid('Rebased and merged.') }]} />
        <A.SplitButton variant="secondary" label="Deploy" menuLabel="More deploy options" loading={deploying} onClick={() => deploy('staging')}
          items={[{ label: 'Deploy to staging', onSelect: () => deploy('staging') }, { label: 'Deploy to production', onSelect: () => deploy('production'), tone: 'critical' }]} />
      </div>
      <p className="cap" role="status">{said || 'Each face lifts on its own. Alt+Down on an action opens its menu. While Deploy is loading, its chevron still works.'}</p>
    </div>
  );
}

=== Checklist | <!-- @dsCard group="Inputs" height=560 subtitle="A list you write into: Enter for the next item, Tab to nest, check it off" -->
--- css
.cl-demo { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(280px, 1fr)); gap: 32px; max-width: 760px; }
.cl-demo h3 { margin: 0 0 8px; font: 600 15px/22px var(--font-sans); color: var(--ink); }
.cl-demo p { margin: 0 0 8px; color: var(--ink-muted); font: 400 12.5px/18px var(--font-sans); }
--- jsx
// A tiny store that applies the Checklist's ops to nested items, and reverses them for Undo.
const clStrip = (list, id) => { let found = null; const out = list.filter(x => { if (x.id === id) { found = x; return false; } return true; }).map(x => { if (found || !x.children) return x; const [kids, hit] = clStrip(x.children, id); if (hit) found = hit; return hit ? { ...x, children: kids } : x; }); return [out, found]; };
const clInsert = (list, parentId, index, node) => parentId === null ? [...list.slice(0, index), node, ...list.slice(index)]
  : list.map(x => x.id === parentId ? { ...x, children: [...(x.children || []).slice(0, index), node, ...(x.children || []).slice(index)] } : x.children ? { ...x, children: clInsert(x.children, parentId, index, node) } : x);
const clUpdate = (list, id, patch) => list.map(x => x.id === id ? { ...x, ...patch } : x.children ? { ...x, children: clUpdate(x.children, id, patch) } : x);
const clApply = (list, op) => {
  if (op.type === 'add') return clInsert(list, op.parentId, op.index, { id: op.id, text: op.text, done: op.done });
  if (op.type === 'edit') return clUpdate(list, op.id, { text: op.text });
  if (op.type === 'toggle') return clUpdate(list, op.id, { done: op.done });
  const [rest, node] = clStrip(list, op.id);
  if (op.type === 'remove') return rest;
  return clInsert(rest, op.parentId, op.index, node);
};
function useChecklist(seed, refuse) {
  const [items, setItems] = React.useState(seed);
  const removedNodes = React.useRef(new Map());
  const onChange = op => {
    if (refuse && refuse(op)) return { ok: false, error: refuse(op) };
    if (op.type === 'remove') removedNodes.current.set(op.id, clStrip(items, op.id)[1]);
    setItems(list => clApply(list, op));
  };
  const onUndo = op => setItems(list => {
    if (op.type === 'add') return clStrip(list, op.id)[0];
    if (op.type === 'remove') return clInsert(list, op.parentId, op.index, removedNodes.current.get(op.id));
    const [rest, node] = clStrip(list, op.id); return clInsert(rest, op.from.parentId, op.from.index, node);
  });
  return { items, onChange, onUndo };
}
const CL_LAUNCH = [
  { id: 'venue', text: 'Book the venue', done: true },
  { id: 'invites', text: 'Send invitations', done: false, children: [
    { id: 'list', text: 'Confirm the guest list', done: true }, { id: 'design', text: 'Design the card', done: false } ] },
  { id: 'catering', text: 'Choose catering', done: false },
];
const CL_PACK = [{ id: 'p1', text: 'Passport', done: false }, { id: 'p2', text: 'Charger', done: true }, { id: 'p3', text: 'Rain jacket', done: false }];
function App() {
  const launch = useChecklist(CL_LAUNCH, op => op.type === 'edit' && /tbd/i.test(op.text) ? 'Write the task, not “TBD”.' : undefined);
  const pack = useChecklist(CL_PACK);
  const props = { getKey: t => t.id, getText: t => t.text, isDone: t => t.done, getChildren: t => t.children };
  return <div className="cl-demo">
    <section><h3 id="cl-launch">Launch tasks</h3><p>Nested: Tab and Shift+Tab indent and outdent. Backspace on an empty line removes it; Ctrl+Z (⌘Z) brings it back. Writing “TBD” is refused.</p>
      <A.Checklist aria-labelledby="cl-launch" nested {...props} {...launch} /></section>
    <section><h3 id="cl-pack">Packing</h3><p>Flat, done items last.</p>
      <A.Checklist aria-labelledby="cl-pack" sortDone {...props} {...pack} placeholder="Add something to pack" /></section>
  </div>;
}

const Body = () => <><svg width="22" height="22" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.75" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round"><path d="M12 16V4M7 9l5-5 5 5M5 20h14" /></svg><span>Drop files or <u>browse</u></span></>;

    <div className="three">
      <div><div className="lab">idle · flush</div><A.DropZone state="idle"><Body /></A.DropZone></div>
      <div><div className="lab">armed · a file is in flight</div><A.DropZone state="armed"><Body /></A.DropZone></div>
      <div><div className="lab">over · ready to receive</div><A.DropZone state="over"><Body /></A.DropZone></div>

    </div>

// A stand-in uploader: files upload in steps, then the server processes them; one fails until retried.
const start = [
  { id: 'a', name: 'Q3 board deck.pdf', size: 2400000, state: 'uploading', progress: 0 },
  { id: 'b', name: 'Customer list.csv', size: 480000, state: 'uploading', progress: 0 },
  { id: 'c', name: 'Brand guidelines.pdf', size: 18200000, state: 'uploading', progress: 0, fails: true },
];
function Queue() {
  const [items, setItems] = React.useState(start);
  React.useEffect(() => {
    const t = setInterval(() => setItems((xs) => xs.map((x) => {
      if (x.state === 'uploading') { const p = Math.min(100, x.progress + (x.id === 'b' ? 34 : 13)); if (x.fails && p > 40) return { ...x, state: 'failed', error: 'The connection dropped' }; return p >= 100 ? { ...x, state: 'processing', progress: 100 } : { ...x, progress: p }; }
      if (x.state === 'processing') return Math.random() < 0.35 ? { ...x, state: 'done' } : x;
      return x;
    })), 450);
    return () => clearInterval(t);
  }, []);
  return <A.DropZone multiple queue={items} onFiles={(fs) => setItems((xs) => [...xs, ...fs.map((f, i) => ({ id: f.name + Date.now() + i, name: f.name, size: f.size, state: 'uploading', progress: 0 }))])}
    onRetry={(id) => setItems((xs) => xs.map((x) => (x.id === id ? { ...x, state: 'uploading', progress: 40, fails: false, error: undefined } : x)))}
    onCancel={(id) => setItems((xs) => xs.filter((x) => x.id !== id))} onRemove={(id) => setItems((xs) => xs.filter((x) => x.id !== id))}><Body /></A.DropZone>;
}

function App() {

=== Tree | <!-- @dsCard group="Navigation" height=620 subtitle="A hierarchy seen at once and changed in place: rename, add, carry into a folder" -->
--- css
.tree-demo { display: grid; grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 320px) minmax(0, 1fr); gap: 24px; max-width: 760px; }

@media (max-width: 560px) { .tree-demo { grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr); } }
--- jsx
const TREE_SEED = [
  { id: 'brand', title: 'Brand', children: [
    { id: 'logo', title: 'Logo usage' }, { id: 'colour', title: 'Colour' }, { id: 'type', title: 'Typography' } ] },
  { id: 'product', title: 'Product', children: [
    { id: 'roadmap', title: 'Roadmap' },
    { id: 'specs', title: 'Specs', children: [{ id: 'search', title: 'Search' }, { id: 'billing', title: 'Billing' }] } ] },
  { id: 'archive', title: 'Archive', children: [] },
  { id: 'handbook', title: 'Team handbook' },
];
const treeWait = ms => new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
// Pure helpers over the nested pages: find, remove, insert.
const treeFind = (list, id, parent = null) => { for (const [i, p] of list.entries()) { if (p.id === id) return { page: p, parent, index: i }; const hit = p.children && treeFind(p.children, id, p.id); if (hit) return hit; } return null; };
const treeRemove = (list, id) => list.filter(p => p.id !== id).map(p => p.children ? { ...p, children: treeRemove(p.children, id) } : p);
const treeInsert = (list, parentId, index, page) => parentId === null ? [...list.slice(0, index), page, ...list.slice(index)]
  : list.map(p => p.id === parentId ? { ...p, children: [...(p.children || []).slice(0, index), page, ...(p.children || []).slice(index)] } : p.children ? { ...p, children: treeInsert(p.children, parentId, index, page) } : p);
const treeRename = (list, id, title) => list.map(p => p.id === id ? { ...p, title } : p.children ? { ...p, children: treeRename(p.children, id, title) } : p);
const FolderIcon = () => <svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.4"><path d="M2 4.5h4l1.5 1.5H14v6.5H2z" /></svg>;
const PageIcon = () => <svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.4"><path d="M4 2h5l3 3v9H4z" /><path d="M9 2v3h3" /></svg>;

function App() {
  const [pages, setPages] = React.useState(TREE_SEED);
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = React.useState(['brand', 'product']);
  const [selected, setSelected] = React.useState('roadmap');
  const tree = React.useRef(null);
  const seq = React.useRef(0);
  const current = selected && treeFind(pages, selected);
  return <div className="tree-demo">
    <div>
      <div className="tree-demo-tools">
        <A.Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => tree.current.startCreate(current && current.page.children ? current.page.id : current ? current.parent : null)}>New page</A.Button>
        <A.Button size="sm" variant="ghost" disabled={!current} onClick={() => tree.current.startRename(selected)}>Rename</A.Button>
      </div>
      <A.Tree ref={tree} aria-label="Pages" items={pages} getKey={p => p.id} getLabel={p => p.title} getChildren={p => p.children} hasChildren={p => !!p.children}
        expanded={expanded} onExpandedChange={setExpanded} selected={selected} onSelectedChange={setSelected}
        renderItem={p => <><span className="tree-demo-icon" aria-hidden="true">{p.children ? <FolderIcon /> : <PageIcon />}</span>{p.title}</>}
        onRename={async (page, title) => { await treeWait(250); if (title.length > 40) return { ok: false, error: 'Use 40 characters or fewer.' }; setPages(list => treeRename(list, page.id, title.trim())); return { ok: true }; }}
        canDrop={({ to }) => to.parentId === null || !!treeFind(pages, to.parentId).page.children}
        onMove={async ({ id, to }) => {
          await treeWait(250);
          // The demo service refuses to move anything out of Archive.
          if (treeFind(pages, id).parent === 'archive' && to.parentId !== 'archive') return { ok: false, error: 'Archived pages can’t be moved out of Archive.' };
          setPages(list => { const page = treeFind(list, id).page; return treeInsert(treeRemove(list, id), to.parentId, to.index, page); });
          return { ok: true };
        }}
        onCreate={async ({ parentId, index, name }) => { await treeWait(250); const page = { id: 'new-' + ++seq.current, title: name }; setPages(list => treeInsert(list, parentId, index, page)); return { ok: true, value: page }; }}
        onRemove={page => setPages(list => treeRemove(list, page.id))} />
    </div>
    <div className="tree-demo-panel" aria-live="polite">
      <h3>{current ? current.page.title : 'No page selected'}</h3>
      <p>F2 renames. Ctrl+Enter adds a page after, Ctrl+Shift+Enter one inside. Space picks a page up: arrows move it, → puts it inside the page above, Space drops. Or drag it onto a folder.</p>
    </div>
  </div>;
}

function App() {
  const [code, setCode] = React.useState('');
  const [state, setState] = React.useState('idle');
  const verify = (c) => { setState('verifying'); setTimeout(() => setState(c === '123456' ? 'ok' : 'wrong'), 900); };
  const hint = state === 'verifying' ? 'Checking code…' : state === 'ok' ? 'Verified.' : 'We sent it to j•••@acme.com. Try 123456, or any other code to see a rejection.';
  return (
    <div className="col">
      <A.Field label="Verification code" hint={hint} error={state === 'wrong' ? 'That code didn\'t work. Check the latest email and try again.' : undefined}>
        <A.InputOTP maxLength={6} value={code} verifying={state === 'verifying'}
          onChange={(c) => { setCode(c); if (state === 'wrong') setState('idle'); }} onComplete={verify}>
          <A.InputOTPGroup>{[0, 1, 2].map((i) => <A.InputOTPSlot key={i} index={i} />)}</A.InputOTPGroup>
          <A.InputOTPSeparator />
          <A.InputOTPGroup>{[3, 4, 5].map((i) => <A.InputOTPSlot key={i} index={i} />)}</A.InputOTPGroup>
        </A.InputOTP>
      </A.Field>
      <A.Field label="Backup code" hint="8 letters or digits.">
        <A.InputOTP maxLength={8} pattern="alphanumeric" defaultValue="K7Q2" />
      </A.Field>
    </div>
  );
}

          ]} />
      </A.Field>

        <A.CardHeader><A.CardTitle>API key created</A.CardTitle><A.CardDescription>Production · created just now</A.CardDescription></A.CardHeader>
        <A.CardContent>
          <A.SecretField label="Secret key" value="ak_live_EXAMPLE0000demo4f2a" once onAcknowledge={() => setSaved(true)} />
        </A.CardContent>
        <A.CardFooter><A.Button variant="primary" disabled={!saved}>Done</A.Button></A.CardFooter>
      </A.Card>
      <A.Card>
        <A.CardHeader><A.CardTitle>Webhook signing secret</A.CardTitle><A.CardDescription>Used to verify deliveries to /hooks/billing</A.CardDescription></A.CardHeader>
        <A.CardContent>
          <div className="col">
            <A.SecretField label="Signing secret" noun="secret" prefix="whk_" maskedTail="9d1c"
              onReveal={() => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(() => r('whk_EXAMPLE0000demo9d1c'), 400))}
              onRotate={() => setSaid('A new secret would be created; the old one keeps working for 24 hours.')} onRevoke={() => setSaid('Secret revoked.')} />
            <p className="cap" role="status">{said}</p>
          </div>
        </A.CardContent>

  );

=== SaveBar | <!-- @dsCard group="Inputs" height=460 subtitle="Unsaved changes on a page, held above it until saved or discarded" -->
