const INLINE_RECORDS = Object.freeze([
  Object.freeze({ id: 'task-launch', name: 'Prepare launch checklist', revision: 1 }),
  Object.freeze({ id: 'file-brand', name: 'Brand guidelines.pdf', revision: 1 }),
  Object.freeze({ id: 'file-brand-draft', name: 'Brand guidelines draft.pdf', revision: 1 }),
  Object.freeze({ id: 'file-launch', name: 'Launch checklist.md', revision: 1 }),
]);
const INLINE_FIXTURES = ['success-100', 'success-1200', 'fail-once', 'conflict', 'interrupted-after-save'];
const inlineWait = ms => new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
const INLINE_STATUSES = [{ value: 'todo', label: 'To do' }, { value: 'progress', label: 'In progress' }, { value: 'review', label: 'In review' }, { value: 'blocked', label: 'Blocked' }];
const INLINE_TONES = { todo: 'neutral', progress: 'accent', review: 'caution', blocked: 'critical' };
const INLINE_PEOPLE = [{ value: 'mara', label: 'Mara Quist' }, { value: 'ines', label: 'Inés Ortega' }, { value: 'tobi', label: 'Tobi Adeyemi' }, { value: 'wen', label: 'Li Wen' }];
const inlineLabel = (list, value) => list.find(option => option.value === value)?.label ?? 'Not set';

// Task properties: the same session with a different editor. Status and assignee save on choose; the estimate waits for Save.
function InlineTaskProperties() {
  const [task, setTask] = React.useState({ status: 'progress', assignee: 'mara', estimate: 3, description: 'Collect sign-off from design, support and legal.\nShare the final list in #launch by Thursday.' });
  const publish = key => value => setTask(task => ({ ...task, [key]: value }));
  const save = key => async draft => {
    await inlineWait(300);
    if (key === 'status' && draft === 'blocked') return { ok: false, error: 'Only the project lead can block a task. Choose another status.' };
    return { ok: true, value: draft };
  };
  return <>
    <div className="inline-demo-description"><span className="inline-demo-prop-label" aria-hidden="true">Description</span>
      <A.InlineEdit label="Description" value={task.description} onValueChange={publish('description')} onSave={save('description')}
        multiline rows={3} maxRows={8} maxLength={500} /></div>
    <div className="inline-demo-props">
    <div className="inline-demo-prop"><span className="inline-demo-prop-label" aria-hidden="true">Status</span>
      <A.InlineEdit label="Status" value={task.status} onValueChange={publish('status')} onSave={save('status')} commit="on-choose"
        renderValue={status => <A.Badge tone={INLINE_TONES[status]}>{inlineLabel(INLINE_STATUSES, status)}</A.Badge>}
        editor={control => <A.Select {...control} items={INLINE_STATUSES} size="sm" />} /></div>
    <div className="inline-demo-prop"><span className="inline-demo-prop-label" aria-hidden="true">Assignee</span>
      <A.InlineEdit label="Assignee" value={task.assignee} onValueChange={publish('assignee')} onSave={save('assignee')} commit="on-choose"
        renderValue={person => inlineLabel(INLINE_PEOPLE, person)}
        editor={control => <A.Combobox {...control} items={INLINE_PEOPLE} size="sm" placeholder="Type a name" />} /></div>
    <div className="inline-demo-prop"><span className="inline-demo-prop-label" aria-hidden="true">Estimate</span>
      <A.InlineEdit label="Estimate" value={task.estimate} onValueChange={publish('estimate')} onSave={save('estimate')} required
        validate={points => Number.isInteger(points) && points >= 0 && points <= 40 ? undefined : 'Use a whole number from 0 to 40.'}
        renderValue={points => `${points} point${points === 1 ? '' : 's'}`}
        editor={({ value, onValueChange, ...control }) => <A.Input {...control} type="number" inputMode="numeric" min={0} max={40} size="sm"
          value={value ?? ''} onChange={event => onValueChange(event.target.value === '' ? null : Number(event.target.value))} />} /></div>
    </div>
  </>;
}

function InlineExample({ fixture, slowValidation, epoch }) {
  const server = React.useRef(null);
  if (!server.current) server.current = new Map(INLINE_RECORDS.map(record => [record.id, { ...record }]));
  const [records, setRecords] = React.useState(() => INLINE_RECORDS.map(record => ({ ...record })));
  const [log, setLog] = React.useState([]);
  const [visible, setVisible] = React.useState(true);
  const [outsideCount, setOutsideCount] = React.useState(0);
  const [baseline, setBaseline] = React.useState(false);
  const sequence = React.useRef(0), failUsed = React.useRef(false), accepted = React.useRef(new Map());
  const alive = React.useRef(true);
  React.useEffect(() => { alive.current = true; return () => { alive.current = false; }; }, []);
  const record = id => records.find(record => record.id === id);
  const trace = event => { if (alive.current) setLog(log => [...log, event]); };
  const reconcile = id => { if (alive.current) setRecords(records => records.map(record => record.id === id ? { ...server.current.get(id) } : record)); };
  const publish = id => canonicalValue => {
    trace({ event: 'published', id, value: canonicalValue });
    setRecords(records => records.map(record => record.id === id ? { ...server.current.get(id), name: canonicalValue } : record));
  };
  const validate = id => async draft => {
    if (slowValidation) await inlineWait(1200);
    if (!draft.trim()) return 'Enter a name.';
    if ([...server.current.values()].some(record => record.id !== id && record.name === draft.trim())) return 'A file already has this name. Choose another.';
  };
  const save = id => async (draft, { previousValue }) => {
    const expectedRevision = record(id).revision;
    const operationId = `rename-${epoch}-${++sequence.current}`;
    trace({ event: 'requested', id, expectedRevision, operationId, draft });
    const commit = () => {
      if (accepted.current.has(operationId)) return accepted.current.get(operationId);
      const current = server.current.get(id);
      if (current.revision !== expectedRevision || current.name !== previousValue) {
        reconcile(id); trace({ event: 'rejected-stale', id, operationId });
        return { ok: false, error: 'The saved name changed. Review the current name.' };
      }
      const next = { ...current, name: draft.trim(), revision: current.revision + 1 };
      server.current.set(id, next);
      const result = { ok: true, value: next.name };
      accepted.current.set(operationId, result);
      trace({ event: 'committed', id, revision: next.revision, operationId, value: next.name });
      return result;
    };
    if (fixture === 'interrupted-after-save') {
      await inlineWait(100); const result = commit();
      await inlineWait(1100);
      // Simulate a lost acknowledgement, then query/retry the same operation ID.
      // A known committed result is returned as success; no second mutation occurs.
      trace({ event: 'reconciled', id, operationId, revision: server.current.get(id).revision });
      return accepted.current.get(operationId) || result;
    }
    if (fixture === 'conflict') {
      await inlineWait(100);
      const current = server.current.get(id);
      server.current.set(id, { ...current, name: id === 'task-launch' ? 'Prepare updated launch checklist' : 'Brand guidelines revised.pdf', revision: current.revision + 1 });
      reconcile(id); trace({ event: 'external-update', id, revision: current.revision + 1 });
      await inlineWait(1100); return commit();
    }
    if (fixture === 'fail-once' && !failUsed.current) {
      failUsed.current = true; await inlineWait(1200);
      trace({ event: 'rejected', id, operationId });
      return { ok: false, error: 'We couldn’t save this name. Your text is still here. Try again.' };
    }
    await inlineWait(fixture === 'success-1200' ? 1200 : 100);
    return commit();
  };
  const file = record('file-brand');
  const externalUpdate = () => {
    const current = server.current.get('file-brand');
    server.current.set('file-brand', { ...current, name: 'Brand guidelines updated.pdf', revision: current.revision + 1 });
    trace({ event: 'external-update', id: 'file-brand', revision: current.revision + 1 }); reconcile('file-brand');
  };
  return <>
    <div className="inline-demo-scene" data-inline-scene="">
      <section className="inline-demo-task" aria-labelledby="inline-task-title">
        <h2 id="inline-task-title">Launch preparation</h2>
        <A.InlineEdit label="Task title" editLabel="Edit task title" value={record('task-launch').name} onValueChange={publish('task-launch')} onSave={save('task-launch')} validate={validate('task-launch')} required maxLength={100} />
        <p className="inline-demo-meta">Due Friday · Product team</p>
        <InlineTaskProperties />
      </section>
      <section aria-labelledby="inline-files-title">
        <h2 id="inline-files-title">Files</h2>
        <A.Table>
          <A.TableHeader><A.TableRow><A.TableHead>File name</A.TableHead><A.TableHead>Size</A.TableHead></A.TableRow></A.TableHeader>
          <A.TableBody>
            {visible && <A.TableRow><A.TableCell>
              <A.InlineEdit label="File name" editLabel="Rename file" value={file.name} onValueChange={publish('file-brand')} onSave={save('file-brand')} validate={validate('file-brand')} required maxLength={100} />
            </A.TableCell><A.TableCell>2.4 MB</A.TableCell></A.TableRow>}
            <A.TableRow><A.TableCell>{record('file-brand-draft').name}<p className="inline-demo-meta">Draft · Updated yesterday</p></A.TableCell><A.TableCell>1.8 MB</A.TableCell></A.TableRow>
            <A.TableRow><A.TableCell>{record('file-launch').name}</A.TableCell><A.TableCell>8 KB</A.TableCell></A.TableRow>
          </A.TableBody>
        </A.Table>
        <A.Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setOutsideCount(count => count + 1)}>Show file details</A.Button>
        {outsideCount > 0 && <p className="inline-demo-meta">Three fictional files · Details opened {outsideCount} time{outsideCount === 1 ? '' : 's'}.</p>}
      </section>
      <InlineWaysToOpen />
    </div>
    <details className="inline-demo-harness">
      <summary>Comparison and fixture controls</summary>
      <p>These controls belong to the documentation harness. The dialog comparison uses the same filename, validation and save adapter. Reset before each trial.</p>
      <div className="inline-demo-controls">
        <A.Button onClick={externalUpdate}>Apply external filename update</A.Button>
        <A.Button onClick={() => { if (!visible) reconcile('file-brand'); setVisible(visible => !visible); }}>{visible ? 'Remove filename editor' : 'Show filename editor'}</A.Button>
        <A.Button onClick={() => setBaseline(true)}>Open dialog rename baseline</A.Button>
      </div>
      <output className="inline-demo-log" data-inline-log="" aria-label="Fixture operation log">{JSON.stringify(log, null, 2)}</output>
    </details>
    <InlineDialogBaseline open={baseline} onOpenChange={setBaseline} value={file.name} validate={validate('file-brand')} onSave={save('file-brand')} onValueChange={publish('file-brand')} />
  </>;
}

function InlineDialogBaseline({ open, onOpenChange, value, validate, onSave, onValueChange }) {
  const [draft, setDraft] = React.useState(value), [error, setError] = React.useState(''), [busy, setBusy] = React.useState('');
  const baseline = React.useRef(value), generation = React.useRef(0), guard = React.useRef(false), latest = React.useRef(value);
  latest.current = value;
  React.useEffect(() => {
    generation.current++; guard.current = false; setBusy(''); setError('');
    if (open) { baseline.current = value; setDraft(value); }
    return () => { generation.current++; };
  }, [open]);
  const submit = async () => {
    if (guard.current) return;
    if (latest.current !== baseline.current) { setError('The saved value changed. Close and reopen to review it.'); return; }
    if (draft === baseline.current) { onOpenChange(false); return; }
    guard.current = true; const token = generation.current; setError(''); setBusy('Checking value');
    const error = draft.length > 100 ? 'Use 100 characters or fewer.' : await validate(draft);
    if (token !== generation.current) return;
    if (error) { guard.current = false; setBusy(''); setError(error); return; }
    setBusy('Saving changes');
    const result = await onSave(draft, { previousValue: baseline.current });
    if (token !== generation.current) return;
    guard.current = false; setBusy('');
    if (latest.current !== baseline.current) { setError('The value changed while saving. Your save may have completed. Check the current record.'); return; }
    if (!result.ok) { setError(result.error); return; }
    onValueChange(result.value); onOpenChange(false);
  };
  return <A.Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange} title="Rename file" description="Change the name of this file." dismissible={busy !== 'Saving changes'} footer={<>
    <A.Button variant="ghost" disabled={busy === 'Saving changes'} onClick={() => onOpenChange(false)}>Cancel</A.Button>
    <A.Button variant="primary" loading={busy === 'Saving changes'} disabled={busy === 'Checking value'} onClick={submit}>Save changes</A.Button>
  </>}>
    <A.Field label="File name" error={error} required><A.Input value={draft} maxLength={100} readOnly={!!busy} onChange={event => { setDraft(event.target.value); setError(''); }} onKeyDown={event => {
      if (event.nativeEvent.isComposing || event.nativeEvent.keyCode === 229) return;
      if (event.key === 'Enter') { event.preventDefault(); submit(); }
    }} /></A.Field>
    <p role="status">{busy}</p>
  </A.Dialog>;
}

// The value as its own button: a pencil on hover, or the field itself on hover. The seam says so at rest.
function InlineWaysToOpen() {
  const [values, setValues] = React.useState({ project: 'Northwind launch', meeting: 'Quarterly planning review', owner: '' });
  const set = key => next => setValues(all => ({ ...all, [key]: next }));
  const save = draft => ({ ok: true, value: draft.trim() });
  return <section aria-labelledby="inline-ways-title">
    <h2 id="inline-ways-title">Ways to open</h2>
    <div className="inline-demo-props">
      <div className="inline-demo-prop"><span className="inline-demo-prop-label">Pencil on hover, with a seam</span>
        <A.InlineEdit label="Project name" trigger="pencil" hint="seam" value={values.project} onValueChange={set('project')} onSave={save} required /></div>
      <div className="inline-demo-prop"><span className="inline-demo-prop-label">Field on hover, with a seam</span>
        <A.InlineEdit label="Meeting title" trigger="field" hint="seam" value={values.meeting} onValueChange={set('meeting')} onSave={save} required /></div>
      <div className="inline-demo-prop"><span className="inline-demo-prop-label">Field on hover, empty</span>
        <A.InlineEdit label="Owner" trigger="field" hint="seam" emptyLabel="Add an owner" value={values.owner} onValueChange={set('owner')} onSave={save} /></div>
    </div>
  </section>;
}

function App() {
  const [fixture, setFixture] = React.useState('success-100'), [slow, setSlow] = React.useState(false);
  const [mounted, setMounted] = React.useState({ fixture: 'success-100', slow: false, epoch: 0 });
  return <div className="inline-demo">
    <InlineExample key={mounted.epoch} fixture={mounted.fixture} slowValidation={mounted.slow} epoch={mounted.epoch} />
    <details className="inline-demo-harness">
      <summary>Choose the next fixture</summary>
      <p>Select before mounting. Reset restores the same fictional records, revisions and request counters.</p>
      <div className="inline-demo-controls">
        <label>Rename fixture<select value={fixture} onChange={event => setFixture(event.target.value)}>{INLINE_FIXTURES.map(name => <option key={name}>{name}</option>)}</select></label>
        <label><input type="checkbox" checked={slow} onChange={event => setSlow(event.target.checked)} />Delay validation by 1,200 ms</label>
        <A.Button onClick={() => setMounted({ fixture, slow, epoch: mounted.epoch + 1 })}>Reset example</A.Button>
      </div>
    </details>
  </div>;
}

=== EditRecord | group="Patterns" subtitle="Edit a record with validation, real demo persistence, failure and recovery" height=680
--- css
.framework-demo{max-width:640px;margin-inline:auto;display:grid;gap:18px;overflow-wrap:anywhere}.framework-demo h2{font:600 var(--text-heading)/1.3 var(--font-sans);margin:0}.framework-demo p{margin:0}.framework-demo form{display:grid;gap:16px}.framework-actions{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:12px;align-items:center}.framework-record{padding:16px;border:1px solid var(--line-control);border-radius:var(--radius-lg)}.framework-demo [role="status"]{min-height:1.5em}.framework-demo pre{white-space:pre-wrap;overflow-wrap:anywhere}

function App(){
 const [record,setRecord]=React.useState({name:'Northwind studio',email:'team@example.com'});
 const [mode,setMode]=React.useState('success');const [overview,setOverview]=React.useState(false);
 const [discard,setDiscard]=React.useState(null);const heading=React.useRef(null);
 const form=A.useEditRecord({initialValue:record,validationMode:'submit',validate:v=>({...(v.name.trim()?{}:{name:'Enter a workspace name.'}),...(/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(v.email)?{}:{email:'Enter a valid email address.'})}),save:async draft=>{
  await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,mode==='slow'?2200:450));
  if(mode==='failure')return {ok:false,error:'The demo service could not save. Choose “Save succeeds” and try again.'};
  const value={...draft,name:draft.name.trim()};setRecord(value);return {ok:true,value};
 }});
 const leave=async reason=>{if(await form.requestLeave(reason,()=>new Promise(resolve=>setDiscard(()=>resolve))))setOverview(true);};
 const errors=Object.entries(form.errors).map(([key,message])=>({id:'workspace-'+key,label:key==='name'?'Workspace name':'Contact email',message}));
 return <main className="framework-demo">
  <h2 ref={heading} tabIndex={-1}>{overview?'Workspace overview':'Workspace settings'}</h2>
  <p>Edits are saved to this example’s in-memory record. Reloading the page resets it.</p>
  <A.Surface level={0} className="framework-record"><strong>Saved record</strong><p data-saved-name>{record.name}</p><p>{record.email}</p></A.Surface>
  {overview?<A.Button onClick={()=>setOverview(false)}>Edit workspace</A.Button>:<>
   <A.Field label="Demo service"><A.Select value={mode} onValueChange={setMode} items={[{value:'success',label:'Save succeeds'},{value:'slow',label:'Save takes longer'},{value:'failure',label:'Save fails'}]}/></A.Field>
   <form noValidate onSubmit={e=>{e.preventDefault();void form.submit();}}>
    <A.ErrorSummary errors={errors} focusKey={form.submitCount}/>
    <A.Field label="Workspace name" required error={form.errors.name} hint="Use the name your team recognizes."><A.Input id="workspace-name" value={form.draft.name} onChange={e=>form.setField('name',e.target.value)}/></A.Field>
    <A.Field label="Contact email" required error={form.errors.email}><A.Input id="workspace-email" type="email" value={form.draft.email} onChange={e=>form.setField('email',e.target.value)}/></A.Field>
    <div className="framework-actions"><A.Button type="submit" variant="primary" disabled={form.phase==='submitting'}>{form.phase==='submitting'?'Saving changes':'Save changes'}</A.Button><A.Button type="button" onClick={()=>leave('cancel')} disabled={form.phase==='submitting'}>Cancel</A.Button><A.Button type="button" variant="ghost" onClick={()=>leave('back')} disabled={form.phase==='submitting'}>Back to overview</A.Button></div>
   </form>
   <A.WorkflowStatus>{form.status}</A.WorkflowStatus>
  </>}
  <A.Dialog returnFocus={()=>heading.current} open={!!discard} onOpenChange={open=>{if(!open){discard?.(false);setDiscard(null);}}} title="Discard unsaved changes?" description="Your saved workspace stays as it is." footer={<><A.Button onClick={()=>{discard?.(false);setDiscard(null);}}>Keep editing</A.Button><A.Button variant="danger" onClick={()=>{discard?.(true);setDiscard(null);}}>Discard changes</A.Button></>}/>
 </main>;
}

const initialRecords=[{id:'atlas',name:'Atlas planning'},{id:'beacon',name:'Beacon research archive — a longer project title to check wrapping'},{id:'cedar',name:'Cedar release'}];
function App(){
 const [mode,setMode]=React.useState('success');const service=React.useRef(mode);service.current=mode;
 const collection=A.useAsyncCollection({initialQuery:'',isFiltered:q=>q.trim()!=='',getKey:item=>item.id,selection:'clear-on-query',load:async(query)=>{await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,700));if(service.current==='failure')throw Error('Demo read failed');return service.current==='empty'?[]:initialRecords.filter(r=>r.name.toLowerCase().includes(query.toLowerCase()));}});
 return <main className="framework-demo"><h2>Project records</h2><p>This example clears selection when the query changes. Refresh preserves selected identities.</p>
  <A.Field label="Search projects"><A.Input value={collection.query} onChange={e=>collection.setQuery(e.target.value)}/></A.Field>
  <div className="framework-actions"><A.Button onClick={collection.refresh}>Refresh records</A.Button><A.Button onClick={collection.reset}>Clear search</A.Button></div>
  <A.Field label="Demo service"><A.Select value={mode} onValueChange={v=>{service.current=v;setMode(v);collection.refresh();}} items={[{value:'success',label:'Records available'},{value:'failure',label:'Load fails'},{value:'empty',label:'No records created yet'}]}/></A.Field>
  <A.WorkflowStatus>{collection.status}</A.WorkflowStatus>
  {collection.phase==='failed'&&<A.Button onClick={collection.refresh}>Try loading again</A.Button>}
  {collection.phase==='no-results'&&<p>Try another name or clear the search.</p>}
  {collection.phase==='empty'&&<p>Create your first project in your application to begin.</p>}

  <p>{collection.selectedKeys.size} selected</p>
 </main>;
}

=== ConsequentialAction | group="Patterns" subtitle="A clear consequence, real mutation and real undo" height=580
--- css
.framework-demo{max-width:640px;margin-inline:auto;display:grid;gap:18px;overflow-wrap:anywhere}.framework-demo h2{font:600 var(--text-heading)/1.3 var(--font-sans);margin:0}.framework-actions{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:12px;align-items:center}.framework-record{padding:16px;border:1px solid var(--line-control);border-radius:var(--radius-lg)}.framework-demo [role="status"]{min-height:1.5em}
--- jsx
function App(){
 const [present,setPresent]=React.useState(true);const [reject,setReject]=React.useState(false);const failure=React.useRef(false);const heading=React.useRef(null);
 const action=A.useConsequentialAction({target:'Quarterly plan',consequence:'Move Quarterly plan to trash. You can restore it in this example.',requiresConfirmation:true,focusFallback:()=>heading.current,execute:async()=>{await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,650));if(failure.current){failure.current=false;setReject(false);return {ok:false,error:'The demo service could not move this record. Try moving it again.'};}setPresent(false);return {ok:true,value:'quarterly-plan'};},undo:async id=>{await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,450));setPresent(true);return {ok:true};},messages:{pending:'Moving Quarterly plan to trash',succeeded:'Quarterly plan moved to trash',undone:'Quarterly plan restored'}});
 const open=['confirming','pending','failed'].includes(action.phase)&&!action.canUndo;
 return <main className="framework-demo"><h2 ref={heading} tabIndex={-1}>Project files</h2><p>The demo changes actual in-memory records; Undo restores the removed record. Production recovery belongs to your application.</p>
  <A.Switch label="Fail the next move" checked={reject} onCheckedChange={v=>{failure.current=v;setReject(v);}}/>
  {present?<A.Surface level={0} className="framework-record"><strong>Quarterly plan</strong><p>Owned by the planning team.</p><A.Button variant="danger" onClick={action.request}>Move to trash</A.Button></A.Surface>:<p>No files in this view.</p>}
  <A.WorkflowStatus>{action.status}</A.WorkflowStatus>
  {action.canUndo&&<A.Button onClick={()=>void action.undo()}>Restore Quarterly plan</A.Button>}
  <A.Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={value=>{if(!value)action.cancel();}} title="Move Quarterly plan to trash?" description={action.consequence} dismissible={action.phase!=='pending'} returnFocus={()=>heading.current} footer={<><A.Button disabled={action.phase==='pending'} onClick={action.cancel}>Keep file</A.Button><A.Button variant="danger" disabled={action.phase==='pending'} onClick={action.phase==='failed'?action.retry:action.confirm}>{action.phase==='failed'?'Try moving again':action.phase==='pending'?'Moving file':'Move to trash'}</A.Button></>}>
   {action.error&&<p role="alert">{action.error}</p>}
  </A.Dialog>
 </main>;
}

=== ReviewSubmit | group="Patterns" subtitle="Review, correct, retry and keep a truthful receipt" height=620
--- css
.framework-demo{max-width:640px;margin-inline:auto;display:grid;gap:18px;overflow-wrap:anywhere}.framework-demo h2{font:600 var(--text-heading)/1.3 var(--font-sans);margin:0}.framework-demo form{display:grid;gap:16px}.framework-actions{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:12px;align-items:center}.framework-record{padding:16px;border:1px solid var(--line-control);border-radius:var(--radius-lg)}.framework-demo dd{margin-inline-start:0;margin-block-end:14px}.framework-demo [role="status"]{min-height:1.5em}

function App(){
 const [reject,setReject]=React.useState(false);const [received,setReceived]=React.useState(null);const heading=React.useRef(null);const firstField=React.useRef(null);const previousPhase=React.useRef('editing');
 const workflow=A.useReviewSubmit({initialValue:{name:'',notes:''},validate:v=>v.name.trim()?{}:{name:'Enter a request name.'},submit:async draft=>{await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,700));if(reject)return {ok:false,error:'The demo service did not receive this request. Turn off failure and try again.'};const receipt={id:'REQUEST-1042',name:draft.name};setReceived({...draft});return {ok:true,receipt};}});
 React.useEffect(()=>{if(workflow.phase!==previousPhase.current){if(workflow.phase==='editing')firstField.current?.focus();else if(workflow.phase==='review'||workflow.phase==='complete')heading.current?.focus();previousPhase.current=workflow.phase;}},[workflow.phase]);
 return <main className="framework-demo"><h2 ref={heading} tabIndex={-1}>{workflow.phase==='complete'?'Request received':workflow.phase==='review'?'Review your request':'New project request'}</h2><ol aria-label="Request progress"><li aria-current={workflow.phase==='editing'?'step':undefined}>Enter details</li><li aria-current={workflow.phase==='review'||workflow.phase==='submitting'?'step':undefined}>Review and send</li><li aria-current={workflow.phase==='complete'?'step':undefined}>Confirmation</li></ol>
  {workflow.phase!=='complete'&&<A.Switch label="Simulate service failure" checked={reject} onCheckedChange={setReject}/>}
  {workflow.phase==='editing'?<form noValidate onSubmit={e=>{e.preventDefault();workflow.review();}}>
   <A.ErrorSummary errors={Object.entries(workflow.errors).map(([key,message])=>({id:'request-'+key,label:'Request name',message}))} focusKey={workflow.submitCount}/>

   <A.Field label="Notes"><A.Textarea value={workflow.draft.notes} onChange={e=>workflow.setField('notes',e.target.value)}/></A.Field><A.Button variant="primary" type="submit">Review request</A.Button>
  </form>:workflow.phase==='complete'?<A.Surface level={0} className="framework-record"><h3>Confirmation</h3><p>Reference: {workflow.receipt.id}</p><p>{workflow.receipt.name}</p><p>The in-memory demo service received your request. This receipt remains until you reload the example.</p></A.Surface>:<>
   <dl><dt>Request name</dt><dd>{workflow.draft.name}</dd><dt>Notes</dt><dd>{workflow.draft.notes||'No notes provided'}</dd></dl>
   <div className="framework-actions"><A.Button disabled={workflow.phase==='submitting'} onClick={workflow.correct}>Correct details</A.Button><A.Button variant="primary" disabled={workflow.phase==='submitting'} onClick={()=>void workflow.submit()}>{workflow.phase==='submitting'?'Sending request':workflow.error?'Try sending again':'Send request'}</A.Button></div>
  </>}
  <A.WorkflowStatus>{workflow.status}</A.WorkflowStatus>
  <p>Requests received by the demo service: {received?1:0}</p>
 </main>;
}

=== SettingsPattern | <!-- @dsCard group="Patterns" height=1100 subtitle="Sections of rows: apply-now switches, fields saved together, a danger zone" -->
--- css
.st-page{max-width:680px;margin-inline:auto;display:grid;gap:20px;padding-bottom:96px;overflow-wrap:anywhere}.st-h{font:600 21px/28px var(--font-sans);margin:0}.st-nav{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px 16px;font:500 14px/20px var(--font-sans)}.st-nav a{display:inline-block;padding-block:2px;color:var(--ink-muted)}.st-form{display:grid;gap:16px}.st-page [role="status"]:empty{display:none}
--- jsx
const wait = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
function App() {
  const [failNext, setFailNext] = React.useState(false);
  const fail = React.useRef(false); fail.current = failNext;
  const [saved, setSaved] = React.useState({ name: 'Acme', url: 'acme' });
  const general = A.useEditRecord({
    initialValue: saved, validationMode: 'submit',
    validate: (d) => ({ ...(d.name.trim() ? {} : { name: 'Enter a workspace name.' }), ...(/^[a-z0-9-]+$/.test(d.url) ? {} : { url: 'Use lowercase letters, numbers and hyphens.' }) }),
    save: async (draft) => { await wait(800); if (fail.current) return { ok: false, error: 'We couldn’t reach the server. Your changes are still here.' }; setSaved(draft); return { ok: true, value: draft }; },
  });
  const [prefs, setPrefs] = React.useState({ digest: true, mentions: true });
  const [writes, setWrites] = React.useState({ digest: undefined, mentions: undefined });
  const apply = async (key, value) => {
    setPrefs((p) => ({ ...p, [key]: value })); setWrites((w) => ({ ...w, [key]: 'pending' }));
    await wait(900);
    if (fail.current) { setPrefs((p) => ({ ...p, [key]: !value })); setWrites((w) => ({ ...w, [key]: 'failed' })); }
    else setWrites((w) => ({ ...w, [key]: 'committed' }));
  };
  const [deleted, setDeleted] = React.useState(false);
  const [typed, setTyped] = React.useState('');
  const heading = React.useRef(null);
  const remove = A.useConsequentialAction({
    target: 'Acme workspace', consequence: 'Delete Acme, its 12 projects and 1,204 files for all 8 members. This can’t be undone.',
    requiresConfirmation: true, focusFallback: () => heading.current,
    execute: async () => { await wait(900); if (fail.current) return { ok: false, error: 'We couldn’t delete the workspace. Nothing was removed. Try again.' }; setDeleted(true); return { ok: true, value: 'acme' }; },
    messages: { pending: 'Deleting Acme workspace', succeeded: 'Acme workspace deleted' },
  });
  const confirming = ['confirming', 'pending', 'failed'].includes(remove.phase);
  const field = (key, label, hint) => <A.Field label={label} hint={hint} error={general.errors[key]}><A.Input id={'ws-' + key} value={general.draft[key]} onChange={(e) => general.setField(key, e.target.value)} /></A.Field>;
  if (deleted) return <main className="st-page"><h2 ref={heading} tabIndex={-1} className="st-h">Workspace deleted</h2><p>Acme and everything in it were deleted.</p><A.WorkflowStatus>{remove.status}</A.WorkflowStatus></main>;
  return (
    <main className="st-page">
      <h2 ref={heading} tabIndex={-1} className="st-h">Workspace settings</h2>
      <nav aria-label="Settings sections" className="st-nav"><a href="#st-general">General</a><a href="#st-notify">Notifications</a><a href="#st-billing">Billing</a><a href="#st-danger">Danger zone</a></nav>
      <A.Switch label="Make the next change fail" checked={failNext} onCheckedChange={setFailNext} />
      <A.SettingsSection id="st-general" title="General" description="Saved together: change what you need, then save once.">
        <form className="st-form" noValidate onSubmit={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); void general.submit(); }}>
          <A.ErrorSummary errors={Object.entries(general.errors).map(([k, message]) => ({ id: 'ws-' + k, label: k === 'name' ? 'Workspace name' : 'Workspace URL', message }))} focusKey={general.submitCount} />
          {field('name', 'Workspace name', 'Shown to everyone in the workspace.')}
          {field('url', 'Workspace URL', 'altitude.app/' + general.draft.url)}
        </form>
      </A.SettingsSection>
      <A.SettingsSection id="st-notify" title="Notifications" description="Each change applies as soon as you make it.">
        <A.SettingsRow label="Weekly digest" description="A summary of activity every Monday." status={writes.digest}><A.Switch checked={prefs.digest} onCheckedChange={(v) => apply('digest', v)} /></A.SettingsRow>
        <A.SettingsRow label="Mentions" description="Email me when someone mentions me." status={writes.mentions}><A.Switch checked={prefs.mentions} onCheckedChange={(v) => apply('mentions', v)} /></A.SettingsRow>
      </A.SettingsSection>
      <A.SettingsSection id="st-billing" title="Billing" readOnlyReason="Only workspace owners can change billing details. Ask Maya Chen.">
        <A.SettingsRow label="Billing email" description="Invoices and receipts go here."><A.Input defaultValue="finance@acme.dev" /></A.SettingsRow>
      </A.SettingsSection>
      <A.SettingsSection id="st-danger" title="Danger zone" tone="critical">
        <A.SettingsRow label="Delete workspace" description={remove.consequence}><A.Button variant="danger" onClick={remove.request}>Delete workspace</A.Button></A.SettingsRow>
      </A.SettingsSection>
      <A.WorkflowStatus>{general.status || remove.status}</A.WorkflowStatus>
      <A.SaveBar record={general} />
      <A.Dialog open={confirming} background="stationary" dismissible={remove.phase !== 'pending'} onOpenChange={(o) => { if (!o) { remove.cancel(); setTyped(''); } }} returnFocus={() => heading.current}
        title="Delete Acme workspace?" description={remove.consequence}
        footer={<><A.Button disabled={remove.phase === 'pending'} onClick={() => { remove.cancel(); setTyped(''); }}>Keep workspace</A.Button><A.Button variant="danger" disabled={typed !== 'Acme' || remove.phase === 'pending'} onClick={remove.phase === 'failed' ? remove.retry : remove.confirm}>{remove.phase === 'pending' ? 'Deleting workspace' : remove.phase === 'failed' ? 'Try deleting again' : 'Delete workspace'}</A.Button></>}>
        <A.Field label="Type Acme to confirm"><A.Input value={typed} onChange={(e) => setTyped(e.target.value)} autoComplete="off" /></A.Field>
        {remove.error && <p role="alert">{remove.error}</p>}
      </A.Dialog>
    </main>
  );
}

=== MembersPattern | <!-- @dsCard group="Patterns" height=1000 subtitle="Invite by email with a role, change roles in place, remove with what they lose" -->
--- css
.mb-page{max-width:760px;margin-inline:auto;display:grid;gap:20px;overflow-wrap:break-word}.mb-page td:first-child{width:40%}.mb-page .alt-table{min-width:600px}.mb-h{font:600 21px/28px var(--font-sans);margin:0}.mb-sub{font:600 17px/24px var(--font-sans);margin:0}.mb-invite{padding:20px;gap:16px}.mb-roles{display:grid;gap:8px}.mb-legend{font:500 14px/20px var(--font-sans)}.mb-who{display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;min-width:0}.mb-name{font:500 14px/20px var(--font-sans)}.mb-email,.mb-reason,.mb-write,.mb-invited{font:400 13px/18px var(--font-sans);color:var(--ink-muted)}.mb-invited{display:inline-flex;gap:6px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap}.mb-role{display:grid;gap:4px;max-width:200px}.mb-write:empty{display:none}.mb-actions{display:inline-flex;gap:4px}.mb-vh{position:absolute;width:1px;height:1px;overflow:hidden;clip-path:inset(50%)}.mb-page [role="status"]:empty{display:none}
--- jsx
const wait = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
const ROLES = [
  { value: 'admin', label: 'Admin', description: 'Manages members, billing and every project.' },
  { value: 'member', label: 'Member', description: 'Creates and edits projects they can see.' },
  { value: 'viewer', label: 'Viewer', description: 'Sees projects and leaves comments. Can’t edit.' },
];
const roleName = (v) => (v === 'owner' ? 'Owner' : ROLES.find((r) => r.value === v)?.label);
function MemberRow({ person, me, owners, fail, onRole, onRemove, onResend, onRevoke }) {
  const [write, setWrite] = React.useState(/** @type {A.DepthProps['status']} */ (undefined));
  const row = React.useRef(null);
  const remove = A.useConsequentialAction({
    target: person.name, consequence: `${person.name} loses access to ${person.projects} projects and every shared dashboard. Their work stays in the workspace.`,
    requiresConfirmation: true, focusFallback: () => document.getElementById('mb-heading'),
    execute: async () => { await wait(700); onRemove(person); return { ok: true, value: person.id }; },
    messages: { pending: `Removing ${person.name}`, succeeded: `${person.name} was removed` },
  });
  const { style, ...depth } = A.useDepth({ status: write, settle: person.invitedAt ? true : undefined });
  const changeRole = async (role) => {
    const before = person.role; onRole(person.id, role); setWrite('pending');
    await wait(800);
    if (fail.current) { onRole(person.id, before); setWrite('failed'); } else setWrite('committed');
  };
  const onlyOwner = person.role === 'owner' && owners === 1;
  return (
    <A.TableRow ref={row} rowId={person.id} {...depth} style={style}>
      <A.TableCell><div className="mb-who"><A.Avatar name={person.name} size={28} /><div><div className="mb-name">{person.name}{person.id === me && ' (you)'}</div><div className="mb-email">{person.email}</div></div></div></A.TableCell>
      <A.TableCell>
        {person.invited ? <span className="mb-invited"><A.Badge>Invited</A.Badge> {roleName(person.role)} · sent {person.sent}</span>
          : onlyOwner ? <span className="mb-reason">Owner. You’re the only owner, so your role can’t change. Transfer ownership first.</span>
          : <div className="mb-role"><A.Select aria-label={`Role for ${person.name}`} value={person.role} onValueChange={changeRole} items={ROLES.map(({ value, label }) => ({ value, label }))} />
              <span className="mb-write" role="status">{write === 'pending' ? 'Changing role…' : write === 'failed' ? 'Couldn’t change the role. Try again.' : write === 'committed' ? 'Role changed' : ''}</span></div>}
      </A.TableCell>
      <A.TableCell align="end">
        {person.invited ? <div className="mb-actions"><A.Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => onResend(person)}>Resend</A.Button><A.Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => onRevoke(person)}>Revoke</A.Button></div>
          : onlyOwner || person.id === me ? null
          : <A.Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={remove.request}>Remove</A.Button>}
        <A.Dialog open={['confirming', 'pending', 'failed'].includes(remove.phase)} background="stationary" size="sm" dismissible={remove.phase !== 'pending'}
          onOpenChange={(o) => { if (!o) remove.cancel(); }} title={`Remove ${person.name}?`} description={remove.consequence}
          footer={<><A.Button disabled={remove.phase === 'pending'} onClick={remove.cancel}>Keep {person.name.split(' ')[0]}</A.Button><A.Button variant="danger" disabled={remove.phase === 'pending'} onClick={remove.confirm}>{remove.phase === 'pending' ? 'Removing' : `Remove ${person.name.split(' ')[0]}`}</A.Button></>} />
      </A.TableCell>
    </A.TableRow>
  );
}
function App() {
  const me = 'u1';
  const [people, setPeople] = React.useState([
    { id: 'u1', name: 'Maya Chen', email: 'maya@acme.dev', role: 'owner', projects: 14 },
    { id: 'u2', name: 'Theo Marsh', email: 'theo@acme.dev', role: 'admin', projects: 9 },
    { id: 'u3', name: 'Ines Duarte', email: 'ines@acme.dev', role: 'member', projects: 4 },
    { id: 'i1', name: 'sam@acme.dev', email: 'Invitation', role: 'member', invited: true, sent: 'yesterday', projects: 0 },
  ]);
  const [emails, setEmails] = React.useState(/** @type {string[]} */ ([]));
  const [role, setRole] = React.useState('member');
  const [sending, setSending] = React.useState(false);
  const [status, setStatus] = React.useState('');
  const [failNext, setFailNext] = React.useState(false);
  const fail = React.useRef(false); fail.current = failNext;
  const owners = people.filter((p) => p.role === 'owner').length;
  const send = async () => {
    setSending(true); setStatus('Sending invites…');
    await wait(900);
    const known = new Set(people.map((p) => p.name).concat(people.map((p) => p.email)));
    const fresh = emails.filter((e) => !known.has(e)), already = emails.length - fresh.length;
    const at = Date.now();
    setPeople((list) => list.concat(fresh.map((e, i) => ({ id: 'i' + at + i, name: e, email: 'Invitation', role, invited: true, sent: 'just now', invitedAt: at, projects: 0 }))));
    setEmails([]); setSending(false);
    setStatus([fresh.length && `${fresh.length} ${fresh.length === 1 ? 'invite' : 'invites'} sent`, already && `${already} ${already === 1 ? 'address is' : 'addresses are'} already in the workspace`].filter(Boolean).join(', ') + '.');
  };
  return (
    <main className="mb-page">
      <h2 id="mb-heading" tabIndex={-1} className="mb-h">Members</h2>
      <A.Card className="mb-invite" aria-labelledby="mb-invite-h">
        <h3 id="mb-invite-h" className="mb-sub">Invite people</h3>
        <A.Field label="Email addresses" hint="Separate addresses with commas, or paste a list.">
          <A.TagInput value={emails} onValueChange={setEmails} noun={['address', 'addresses']} placeholder="name@acme.dev" disabled={sending}
            normalize={(t) => t.trim().toLowerCase()} validate={(t) => (/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(t) ? undefined : `${t} isn’t an email address.`)} />
        </A.Field>
        <div className="mb-roles"><span id="mb-role-label" className="mb-legend">Role</span>
          <A.RadioGroup value={role} onValueChange={setRole} aria-labelledby="mb-role-label">{ROLES.map((r) => <A.Radio key={r.value} value={r.value} label={r.label} description={r.description} />)}</A.RadioGroup>
        </div>
        <div><A.Button variant="primary" loading={sending} disabled={!emails.length} onClick={send}>{sending ? 'Sending invites' : emails.length > 1 ? `Send ${emails.length} invites` : 'Send invite'}</A.Button></div>
      </A.Card>
      <A.Switch label="Make the next role change fail" checked={failNext} onCheckedChange={setFailNext} />
      <A.Table aria-label="Members and invitations">
        <A.TableHeader><A.TableRow><A.TableHead>Person</A.TableHead><A.TableHead>Role</A.TableHead><A.TableHead align="end"><span className="mb-vh">Actions</span></A.TableHead></A.TableRow></A.TableHeader>
        <A.TableBody>{people.map((p) => <MemberRow key={p.id} person={p} me={me} owners={owners} fail={fail}
          onRole={(id, r) => setPeople((l) => l.map((x) => (x.id === id ? { ...x, role: r } : x)))}
          onRemove={(x) => { setPeople((l) => l.filter((y) => y.id !== x.id)); setStatus(`${x.name} was removed.`); }}
          onResend={(x) => setStatus(`Invite resent to ${x.name}.`)}
          onRevoke={(x) => { setPeople((l) => l.filter((y) => y.id !== x.id)); setStatus(`Invitation to ${x.name} revoked.`); document.getElementById('mb-heading')?.focus(); }} />)}</A.TableBody>
      </A.Table>
      <A.WorkflowStatus>{status}</A.WorkflowStatus>
    </main>
  );
}

=== BillingPattern | <!-- @dsCard group="Patterns" height=1500 subtitle="Usage as readings, a plan change reviewed with its proration, invoices" -->
--- css
.bl-page{max-width:820px;margin-inline:auto;display:grid;gap:24px;overflow-wrap:break-word}.bl-h{font:600 21px/28px var(--font-sans);margin:0}.bl-sub{font:600 17px/24px var(--font-sans);margin:0}.bl-section{display:grid;gap:16px}.bl-meters{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(200px,1fr));gap:16px 24px}.bl-note,.bl-proration{margin:0;font:400 14px/21px var(--font-sans);color:var(--ink-muted)}.bl-proration{color:var(--ink);font-size:15px}.bl-losses{font:400 14px/21px var(--font-sans)}.bl-losses ul{margin:6px 0 0;padding-inline-start:20px}.bl-actions{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:12px}.bl-page [role="status"]:empty{display:none}.bl-page .alt-table{min-width:520px}
