const wait = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
const PLANS = [
  { id: 'free', name: 'Free', price: 0, currency: 'EUR', unit: 'per seat', features: ['3 projects', 'Community support'] },
  { id: 'pro', name: 'Pro', price: { month: 2400, year: 2000 }, currency: 'EUR', unit: 'per seat', features: ['Unlimited projects', 'SAML SSO', 'Audit log'] },
  { id: 'business', name: 'Business', price: { month: 4800, year: 4000 }, currency: 'EUR', unit: 'per seat', features: ['Everything in Pro', 'Custom roles', '99.9% uptime SLA'] },
];
const LOSSES = { free: ['SAML SSO, which 8 members sign in with', 'The audit log and its 90 days of history', '9 of your 12 projects become read-only'], pro: ['Custom roles: 2 members on the “Finance” role move to Member'] };
const eur = (minor) => A.formatNumber(minor, { format: 'currency', currency: 'EUR', locale: 'en-IE' });
const usage = [
  { week: '2026-10-05', calls: 210000 }, { week: '2026-10-12', calls: 260000 }, { week: '2026-10-19', calls: 310000 },
  { week: '2026-10-26', calls: 290000 }, { week: '2026-11-02', calls: 300000 }, { week: '2026-11-09', calls: 320000 },
];
function PlanChange({ current, period, onPeriod, onChanged, onAgain }) {
  const rank = (id) => PLANS.findIndex((p) => p.id === id);
  const flow = A.useReviewSubmit({
    initialValue: { plan: current }, validate: (d) => (d.plan === current ? { plan: 'Choose a plan you’re not on.' } : {}),
    submit: async (d) => { await wait(900); return { ok: true, receipt: { plan: d.plan } }; },
    messages: { submitting: 'Changing plan…', complete: 'Plan changed', failed: 'We couldn’t change your plan. Nothing was charged. Try again.' },
  });
  React.useEffect(() => { if (flow.phase === 'review' || flow.phase === 'complete') document.getElementById('bl-plan-h')?.focus(); if (flow.phase === 'complete' && flow.receipt) onChanged(flow.receipt.plan); }, [flow.phase]);
  const next = PLANS.find((p) => p.id === flow.draft.plan) || PLANS[0], down = rank(flow.draft.plan) < rank(current);
  const per = (p) => (typeof p.price === 'number' ? p.price : p.price[period]) * 10;
  const today = Math.max(0, Math.round((per(next) - per(PLANS[rank(current)])) * 0.35));
  return (
    <section className="bl-section" aria-labelledby="bl-plan-h">
      <h3 id="bl-plan-h" tabIndex={-1} className="bl-sub">{flow.phase === 'complete' ? `You’re on ${next.name}` : flow.phase === 'editing' ? 'Change plan' : down ? `Downgrade to ${next.name}?` : `Upgrade to ${next.name}?`}</h3>
      {flow.phase === 'editing' && <>
        <A.PlanPicker plans={PLANS} value={flow.draft.plan} onValueChange={(v) => flow.setField('plan', v)} current={current} recommended="business" period={period} onPeriodChange={onPeriod} locale="en-IE" aria-label="Plans" />
        {flow.errors.plan && <p className="bl-note" role="alert">{flow.errors.plan}</p>}
        <div><A.Button variant="primary" onClick={flow.review}>Review change</A.Button></div>
      </>}
      {(flow.phase === 'review' || flow.phase === 'submitting') && <>
        <p className="bl-proration">{down ? `Your plan changes to ${next.name} at the end of this cycle, on 30 November. Then you’ll pay ${eur(per(next))}/${period}.` : `You’ll pay ${eur(today)} today for the rest of this cycle, then ${eur(per(next))}/${period} for 10 seats.`}</p>
        {down && <div className="bl-losses"><strong>You’ll lose</strong><ul>{(LOSSES[next.id] || []).map((l) => <li key={l}>{l}</li>)}</ul></div>}
        <div className="bl-actions"><A.Button disabled={flow.phase === 'submitting'} onClick={flow.correct}>Choose another plan</A.Button>
          <A.Button variant={down ? 'danger' : 'primary'} loading={flow.phase === 'submitting'} onClick={() => void flow.submit()}>{flow.phase === 'submitting' ? 'Changing plan' : down ? `Downgrade to ${next.name}` : `Upgrade to ${next.name}`}</A.Button></div>
      </>}
      {flow.phase === 'complete' && <p className="bl-note">{down ? `${next.name} starts on 30 November. Until then everything on your current plan keeps working.` : `${next.name} features are on now. A receipt is on its way to finance@acme.dev.`}</p>}
      {flow.phase === 'complete' && <div><A.Button variant="ghost" onClick={onAgain}>Change plan again</A.Button></div>}
      <A.WorkflowStatus>{flow.status}</A.WorkflowStatus>
    </section>
  );
}
function App() {
  const [current, setCurrent] = React.useState('pro');
  const [period, setPeriod] = React.useState('month');
  const [attempt, setAttempt] = React.useState(0);
  const [failed, setFailed] = React.useState(false);
  return (
    <main className="bl-page">
      {failed && <A.Alert placement="page" tone="critical" variant="raised" title="Your last payment failed"
        action={<A.Button size="sm" onClick={() => setFailed(false)}>Update payment method</A.Button>}>We couldn’t charge the card ending 4242 on 1 November. Update it by 8 November to keep your workspace active.</A.Alert>}
      <h2 className="bl-h">Billing</h2>
      <A.Switch label="Simulate a failed payment" checked={failed} onCheckedChange={setFailed} />
      <section className="bl-section" aria-labelledby="bl-usage-h">
        <h3 id="bl-usage-h" className="bl-sub">Usage this cycle</h3>
        <div className="bl-meters">
          <A.Meter label="Seats" value={8} max={10} high={8} format={(v) => String(v)} />
          <A.Meter label="Storage" value={62} max={100} high={80} format={(v) => `${v} GB`} />
          <A.Meter label="API calls" value={1.2} max={1} high={0.8} format={(v) => `${v}M`} />
        </div>
        <A.Stat label="Estimated next bill" value={28800} format={eur} help="10 seats × €24, plus €48 for 0.2M API calls over the 1M included. Updated hourly; the final amount is on your invoice." />
        <A.ChartContainer aria-labelledby="bl-calls-h" config={{ calls: { label: 'API calls', format: (v) => A.formatNumber(v, { format: 'compact', locale: 'en-IE' }) } }} height={180}>
          <A.BarChart data={usage} x="week" status={(d) => (String(d.week) > '2026-10-30' ? 'projected' : undefined)} />
        </A.ChartContainer>
        <p id="bl-calls-h" className="bl-note">API calls per week. The last two weeks of the cycle are projected.</p>
      </section>
      <PlanChange key={attempt} current={current} period={period} onPeriod={setPeriod} onChanged={setCurrent} onAgain={() => setAttempt((n) => n + 1)} />
      <section className="bl-section" aria-labelledby="bl-roles-h">
        <h3 id="bl-roles-h" className="bl-sub">Custom roles</h3>
        <A.FeatureGate requires="Business" description="Create roles with exactly the permissions a team needs." onUpgrade={() => document.getElementById('bl-plan-h')?.focus()}>
          <A.Button>Create a role</A.Button>
        </A.FeatureGate>
      </section>
      <section className="bl-section" aria-labelledby="bl-inv-h">
        <h3 id="bl-inv-h" className="bl-sub">Invoices</h3>
        <A.Table aria-labelledby="bl-inv-h">
          <A.TableHeader><A.TableRow><A.TableHead>Date</A.TableHead><A.TableHead>Invoice</A.TableHead><A.TableHead align="end">Amount</A.TableHead><A.TableHead>Status</A.TableHead></A.TableRow></A.TableHeader>
          <A.TableBody>{[{ d: '1 Oct 2026', n: 'INV-0042', a: 26400, s: 'Paid' }, { d: '1 Sep 2026', n: 'INV-0041', a: 24000, s: 'Paid' }, { d: '1 Aug 2026', n: 'INV-0040', a: 24000, s: 'Refunded' }].map(({ d, n, a, s }) =>
            <A.TableRow key={n}><A.TableCell>{d}</A.TableCell><A.TableCell><A.Link href={'#/invoices/' + n}>{n}</A.Link></A.TableCell><A.TableCell align="end"><A.FormattedNumber value={a} format="currency" currency="EUR" locale="en-IE" /></A.TableCell><A.TableCell><A.Badge tone={s === 'Paid' ? 'positive' : 'neutral'}>{s}</A.Badge></A.TableCell></A.TableRow>)}</A.TableBody>
        </A.Table>
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}

=== AuthPattern | <!-- @dsCard group="Patterns" height=760 subtitle="Sign in, sign up, two-factor and reset on a raised card, with copy that reveals nothing" -->
--- css
.au-page{display:grid;place-items:start center;padding-block:24px}.au-card{width:min(420px,100%);padding:28px;gap:16px}.au-card p{margin:0;font:400 15px/23px var(--font-sans)}.au-h{font:600 21px/28px var(--font-sans);margin:0}.au-form{display:grid;gap:16px}.au-wide{width:100%}.au-links{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;justify-content:space-between;gap:8px}.au-card .au-note{font-size:14px;color:var(--ink-muted)}
--- jsx
const wait = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
const isEmail = (v) => /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(v);
const RULES = [{ label: 'At least 12 characters', test: (v) => v.length >= 12 }, { label: 'Not just letters', test: (v) => /[^a-z]/i.test(v) }];
function App() {
  const [step, setStep] = React.useState('sign-in');
  const [email, setEmail] = React.useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = React.useState('');
  const [name, setName] = React.useState('');
  const [code, setCode] = React.useState('');
  const [recovery, setRecovery] = React.useState('');
  const [trust, setTrust] = React.useState(false);
  const [busy, setBusy] = React.useState('');
  const [errors, setErrors] = React.useState(/** @type {{ id: string, label: string, message: string }[]} */ ([]));
  const [tries, setTries] = React.useState(0);
  const [submits, setSubmits] = React.useState(0);
  const [resendIn, setResendIn] = React.useState(0);
  const heading = React.useRef(/** @type {HTMLHeadingElement | null} */ (null));
  React.useEffect(() => { if (resendIn <= 0) return; const t = setTimeout(() => setResendIn((s) => s - 1), 1000); return () => clearTimeout(t); }, [resendIn]);
  const go = (next) => { setStep(next); setErrors([]); setBusy(''); requestAnimationFrame(() => heading.current?.focus()); };
  const fail = (list) => { setErrors(list); setSubmits((n) => n + 1); };
  const signIn = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const list = [];
    if (!isEmail(email)) list.push({ id: 'au-email', label: 'Email', message: 'Enter your email address, like name@example.com.' });
    if (!password) list.push({ id: 'au-password', label: 'Password', message: 'Enter your password.' });
    if (list.length) return fail(list);
    if (tries >= 3) return fail([{ id: 'au-password', label: 'Password', message: 'Too many attempts. Wait 5 minutes, then try again, or reset your password.' }]);
    setBusy('password'); await wait(800); setBusy('');
    if (password !== 'altitude') { setTries((n) => n + 1); setPassword(''); return fail([{ id: 'au-password', label: 'Password', message: 'That email and password don’t match. Try again or reset your password.' }]); }
    go('two-factor');
  };
  const sendLink = async (next) => {
    if (!isEmail(email)) return fail([{ id: 'au-email', label: 'Email', message: 'Enter your email address, like name@example.com.' }]);
    setBusy('link'); await wait(700); setResendIn(30); go(next);
  };
  const signUp = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const list = [];
    if (!name.trim()) list.push({ id: 'au-name', label: 'Name', message: 'Enter your name.' });
    if (!isEmail(email)) list.push({ id: 'au-email', label: 'Email', message: 'Enter your email address, like name@example.com.' });
    if (!RULES.every((r) => r.test(password))) list.push({ id: 'au-password', label: 'Password', message: 'Choose a password that meets both requirements.' });
    if (list.length) return fail(list);
    setBusy('password'); await wait(800); setCode(''); setResendIn(30); go('verify');
  };
  const verify = async (value) => {
    setBusy('code'); await wait(700);
    if (value !== '123456') { setBusy(''); setCode(''); return fail([{ id: 'au-code', label: 'Code', message: 'That code didn’t work. Check your authenticator app and enter the newest code.' }]); }
    go('done');
  };
  const field = (id) => errors.find((x) => x.id === id)?.message;
  const resend = <p className="au-note">{resendIn > 0 ? `You can resend the email in ${resendIn} ${resendIn === 1 ? 'second' : 'seconds'}.` : <A.Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => sendLink(step)}>Resend the email</A.Button>}</p>;
  return (
    <main className="au-page">
      <A.Card className="au-card">
        {step === 'sign-in' && <>
          <h2 ref={heading} tabIndex={-1} className="au-h">Sign in to Acme</h2>
          <A.Button className="au-wide" onClick={() => go('sso')}>Continue with single sign-on (SSO)</A.Button>
          <A.Separator label="or" />
          <form className="au-form" noValidate onSubmit={signIn} aria-labelledby="au-pw-h">
            <span id="au-pw-h" hidden>Sign in with a password</span>
            <A.ErrorSummary errors={errors} focusKey={submits} title="We couldn’t sign you in" />
            <A.Field label="Email" error={field('au-email')}><A.Input id="au-email" type="email" autoComplete="username" value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} /></A.Field>
            <A.Field label="Password" error={field('au-password')}><A.PasswordInput id="au-password" autoComplete="current-password" value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} /></A.Field>
            <A.Button type="submit" variant="primary" className="au-wide" loading={busy === 'password'}>{busy === 'password' ? 'Signing in' : 'Sign in'}</A.Button>
          </form>
          <div className="au-links"><A.Button variant="ghost" size="sm" loading={busy === 'link'} onClick={() => sendLink('link-sent')}>Email me a sign-in link</A.Button><A.Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => go('reset')}>Forgot your password?</A.Button></div>
          <p className="au-note">New to Acme? <A.Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => { setPassword(''); go('sign-up'); }}>Create an account</A.Button></p>
        </>}
        {step === 'sign-up' && <>
          <h2 ref={heading} tabIndex={-1} className="au-h">Create your Acme account</h2>
          <form className="au-form" noValidate onSubmit={signUp} aria-label="Create an account">
            <A.ErrorSummary errors={errors} focusKey={submits} />
            <A.Field label="Name" error={field('au-name')}><A.Input id="au-name" autoComplete="name" value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} /></A.Field>
            <A.Field label="Email" error={field('au-email')}><A.Input id="au-email" type="email" autoComplete="email" value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} /></A.Field>
            <A.Field label="Password" error={field('au-password')}><A.PasswordInput id="au-password" autoComplete="new-password" requirements={RULES} value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} /></A.Field>
            <A.Button type="submit" variant="primary" className="au-wide" loading={busy === 'password'}>{busy === 'password' ? 'Creating account' : 'Create account'}</A.Button>
          </form>
          <A.Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => go('sign-in')}>I already have an account</A.Button>
        </>}
        {step === 'verify' && <>
          <h2 ref={heading} tabIndex={-1} className="au-h">Verify your email</h2>
          <p>We sent a 6-digit code to <strong>{email}</strong>. It works for 10 minutes.</p>
          <A.ErrorSummary errors={errors} focusKey={submits} title="We couldn’t verify that code" />
          <A.Field label="Verification code" error={field('au-code')}><A.InputOTP id="au-code" maxLength={6} value={code} onChange={setCode} verifying={busy === 'code'} autoComplete="one-time-code" /></A.Field>
          <A.Button variant="primary" className="au-wide" loading={busy === 'code'} disabled={code.length < 6} onClick={() => verify(code)}>{busy === 'code' ? 'Verifying' : 'Verify email'}</A.Button>
          {resend}
          <A.Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => go('sign-up')}>Change email address</A.Button>
        </>}
        {step === 'link-sent' && <>
          <h2 ref={heading} tabIndex={-1} className="au-h">Check your email</h2>
          <p>If <strong>{email}</strong> has an Acme account, we’ve sent a sign-in link to it. The link works once, for 15 minutes.</p>
          {resend}
          <A.Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => go('sign-in')}>Use a different email address</A.Button>
        </>}
        {step === 'sso' && <>
          <h2 ref={heading} tabIndex={-1} className="au-h">Sign in with SSO</h2>
          <A.Field label="Work email" hint="We’ll send you to your company’s sign-in page."><A.Input type="email" autoComplete="username" value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} /></A.Field>
          <A.Button variant="primary" className="au-wide" onClick={() => go('sign-in')}>Continue</A.Button>
          <A.Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => go('sign-in')}>Back to sign in</A.Button>
        </>}
        {step === 'two-factor' && <>
          <h2 ref={heading} tabIndex={-1} className="au-h">Enter your code</h2>
          <p>Open your authenticator app and enter the 6-digit code for Acme.</p>
          <A.ErrorSummary errors={errors} focusKey={submits} title="We couldn’t verify that code" />
          <A.Field label="Authentication code" error={field('au-code')}>
            <A.InputOTP id="au-code" maxLength={6} value={code} onChange={setCode} verifying={busy === 'code'} autoComplete="one-time-code" />
          </A.Field>
          <A.Checkbox label="Trust this device for 30 days" checked={trust} onCheckedChange={setTrust} />
          <A.Button variant="primary" className="au-wide" loading={busy === 'code'} disabled={code.length < 6} onClick={() => verify(code)}>{busy === 'code' ? 'Verifying' : 'Verify'}</A.Button>
          <A.Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => go('recovery')}>Use a recovery code instead</A.Button>
        </>}
        {step === 'recovery' && <>
          <h2 ref={heading} tabIndex={-1} className="au-h">Use a recovery code</h2>
          <A.Field label="Recovery code" hint="One of the codes you saved when you set up two-factor. Each works once."><A.Input autoComplete="off" value={recovery} onChange={(e) => setRecovery(e.target.value)} /></A.Field>
          <A.Button variant="primary" className="au-wide" onClick={() => go('done')}>Verify</A.Button>
          <A.Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => go('two-factor')}>Use your authenticator app</A.Button>
        </>}
        {step === 'reset' && <>
          <h2 ref={heading} tabIndex={-1} className="au-h">Reset your password</h2>
          <A.ErrorSummary errors={errors} focusKey={submits} />
          <A.Field label="Email" error={field('au-email')}><A.Input id="au-email" type="email" autoComplete="username" value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} /></A.Field>
          <A.Button variant="primary" className="au-wide" loading={busy === 'link'} onClick={() => sendLink('reset-sent')}>Send reset link</A.Button>
          <A.Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => go('sign-in')}>Back to sign in</A.Button>
        </>}
        {step === 'reset-sent' && <>
          <h2 ref={heading} tabIndex={-1} className="au-h">Check your email</h2>
          <p>If <strong>{email}</strong> has an Acme account, we’ve sent a link to reset its password. The link works once, for 1 hour.</p>
          {resend}
          <A.Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => go('sign-in')}>Back to sign in</A.Button>
        </>}
        {step === 'done' && <>
          <h2 ref={heading} tabIndex={-1} className="au-h">You’re signed in</h2>
          <p>{trust ? 'This device won’t ask for a code for 30 days.' : 'We’ll ask for a code next time you sign in on this device.'}</p>
          <A.Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => { setPassword(''); setCode(''); setTries(0); go('sign-in'); }}>Sign out</A.Button>
        </>}
      </A.Card>
    </main>
  );
}

=== AuditLogPattern | <!-- @dsCard group="Patterns" height=860 subtitle="Entries are the record: flush sentences, filtered, paged, expanded to what changed" -->
--- css
.al-page{max-width:900px;margin-inline:auto;display:grid;gap:18px;overflow-wrap:break-word}.al-h{font:600 21px/28px var(--font-sans);margin:0}.al-sub{font:600 17px/24px var(--font-sans);margin:0}.al-note{margin:0;font:400 14px/21px var(--font-sans);color:var(--ink-muted)}.al-tools{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:12px;align-items:flex-start;justify-content:space-between}.al-tools>:first-child{flex:1 1 320px;min-width:0}.al-export{display:grid;gap:6px;font:500 13px/18px var(--font-sans);color:var(--ink-muted)}.al-record{display:grid;gap:12px}.al-page .alt-table{min-width:640px}
--- jsx
const wait = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
const ACTORS = [{ value: 'Maya Chen', label: 'Maya Chen' }, { value: 'Theo Marsh', label: 'Theo Marsh' }, { value: 'Ines Duarte', label: 'Ines Duarte' }];
const ACTIONS = [{ value: 'role', label: 'Changed a role' }, { value: 'invite', label: 'Invited someone' }, { value: 'key', label: 'Rotated an API key' }, { value: 'export', label: 'Exported data' }];
/** @typedef {{ id: string, actor: string, action: string, resource: string, sentence: string, at: string, ip: string, before: Record<string, string>, after: Record<string, string> }} AuditEvent */
const SEED = Array.from({ length: 22 }, /** @returns {AuditEvent} */ (_, i) => {
  const actor = ACTORS[i % 3].value, kind = ACTIONS[i % 3].value, day = String(28 - Math.floor(i / 3)).padStart(2, '0');
  const at = `2026-10-${day}T${String(17 - (i % 3) * 3).padStart(2, '0')}:${String((i * 7) % 60).padStart(2, '0')}:00Z`;
  if (kind === 'role') return { id: 'e' + i, actor, action: kind, resource: 'Ines Duarte', sentence: `${actor} changed Ines Duarte’s role from Member to Admin`, at, ip: '81.2.69.' + (10 + i), before: { role: 'Member' }, after: { role: 'Admin' } };
  if (kind === 'invite') return { id: 'e' + i, actor, action: kind, resource: `guest${i}@acme.dev`, sentence: `${actor} invited guest${i}@acme.dev as a Viewer`, at, ip: '81.2.69.' + (10 + i), before: { invited: 'No' }, after: { invited: 'Yes, as Viewer' } };
  return { id: 'e' + i, actor, action: kind, resource: 'Production key', sentence: `${actor} rotated the Production API key`, at, ip: '81.2.69.' + (10 + i), before: { key: 'ak_live_EXAMPLE…4f2a' }, after: { key: 'ak_live_EXAMPLE…9c1d' } };
});
/** @type {A.FilterField[]} */
const FIELDS = [{ key: 'actor', label: 'Actor', type: 'enum', options: ACTORS }, { key: 'action', label: 'Action', type: 'enum', options: ACTIONS }, { key: 'at', label: 'Date', type: 'date' }];
const test = (row, f) => {
  if (f.value === undefined || f.value === '') return true;
  const v = f.field === 'at' ? row.at.slice(0, 10) : row[f.field];
  if (f.operator === 'is') return v === f.value;
  if (f.operator === 'is not') return v !== f.value;
  if (f.operator === 'before') return v < f.value;
  if (f.operator === 'after') return v > f.value;
  if (f.operator === 'between' && typeof f.value === 'object') return v >= f.value.start && v <= f.value.end;
  return true;
};
function App() {
  const [log, setLog] = React.useState(SEED);
  const [filters, setFilters] = React.useState(/** @type {A.Filter[]} */ ([]));
  const [page, setPage] = React.useState(1);
  const [loading, setLoading] = React.useState(false);
  const [exporting, setExporting] = React.useState(/** @type {number | null} */ (null));
  const matching = log.filter((r) => filters.every((f) => test(r, f)));
  const pageCount = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(matching.length / 5));
  const rows = matching.slice((page - 1) * 5, page * 5);
  const turn = async (p) => { setLoading(true); await wait(400); setPage(p); setLoading(false); };
  const runExport = async () => {
    for (const pct of [0, 20, 40, 60, 80, 100]) { setExporting(pct); await wait(250); }
    setExporting(null);
    setLog((l) => [{ id: 'x' + l.length, actor: 'Maya Chen', action: 'export', resource: 'Audit log', sentence: `Maya Chen exported ${matching.length} audit log events as CSV`, at: '2026-10-29T09:00:00Z', ip: '81.2.69.4', before: {}, after: { file: 'audit-log.csv' } }, ...l]);
    A.toast({ title: 'Your export is ready', description: `${matching.length} events, audit-log.csv`, tone: 'positive', duration: 0, action: { label: 'Download', onClick: () => {} } });
  };
  return (
    <main className="al-page">
      <h2 className="al-h">Audit log</h2>
      <p className="al-note">Every change in the Acme workspace, kept for 90 days on the Pro plan. Older events are deleted.</p>
      <div className="al-tools">
        <A.FilterBar aria-label="Filter events" fields={FIELDS} value={filters} onValueChange={(f) => { setFilters(f); setPage(1); }} resultCount={matching.length} />
        <A.Button onClick={runExport} disabled={exporting !== null}>Export CSV</A.Button>
      </div>
      {exporting !== null && <div className="al-export"><A.Progress value={exporting} aria-label="Preparing export" /><span role="status">Preparing export, {exporting}%</span></div>}
      <A.DataTable aria-label="Audit log events" noun="events" rows={rows} getRowId={(r) => r.id} getRowLabel={(r) => r.sentence}
        empty="No events match these filters. Remove a filter to see more."
        columns={[
          { key: 'sentence', header: 'Event', width: '46%' },
          { key: 'actor', header: 'Actor' },
          { key: 'at', header: 'Time', cell: (r) => <A.RelativeTime date={r.at} locale="en-IE" /> },
          { key: 'ip', header: 'IP address' },
        ]}
        renderExpanded={(r) => <A.DiffView mode="properties" before={r.before} after={r.after} caption={`${r.resource}: what changed`} />}
        pagination={{ page, pageCount, total: matching.length, onPageChange: turn, loading, noun: 'events' }} />
      <section className="al-record" aria-labelledby="al-activity-h">
        <h3 id="al-activity-h" className="al-sub">Activity on Q4 plan</h3>
        <A.Timeline aria-label="Activity on Q4 plan" groupBy="none" locale="en-IE">
          <A.TimelineItem key="a1" time="2026-10-28T15:10:00Z">Theo Marsh changed the due date to 15 December</A.TimelineItem>
          <A.TimelineItem key="a2" time="2026-10-27T11:02:00Z" detail={<A.DiffView mode="properties" before={{ owner: 'Maya Chen' }} after={{ owner: 'Ines Duarte' }} />}>Maya Chen made Ines Duarte the owner</A.TimelineItem>
          <A.TimelineItem key="a3" time="2026-10-26T09:45:00Z" href="#/docs/q4">Ines Duarte linked the Q4 pricing doc</A.TimelineItem>
        </A.Timeline>
      </section>
      <A.Toaster />
    </main>
  );
}

=== ImportPattern | <!-- @dsCard group="Patterns" height=820 subtitle="Upload, map, fix the rows with problems, review, then import as a job with a receipt" -->
--- css
.im-page{max-width:820px;margin-inline:auto;display:grid;gap:20px;overflow-wrap:break-word}.im-h{font:600 21px/28px var(--font-sans);margin:0}.im-sub{font:600 17px/24px var(--font-sans);margin:0}.im-step{display:grid;gap:16px}.im-note{margin:0;font:400 14px/21px var(--font-sans);color:var(--ink-muted)}.im-lead{margin:0;font:400 15px/23px var(--font-sans)}.im-map{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(220px,1fr));gap:16px 24px}.im-actions{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:12px}.im-receipt{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(160px,1fr));gap:12px;margin:0}.im-receipt div{display:grid;gap:2px}.im-receipt dt{font:500 13px/18px var(--font-sans);color:var(--ink-muted)}.im-receipt dd{margin:0;font:600 17px/24px var(--font-sans)}
--- jsx
const wait = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
const STEPS = [{ title: 'Upload' }, { title: 'Map columns' }, { title: 'Fix problems' }, { title: 'Review' }, { title: 'Import' }];
const SOURCE = [{ key: 'c0', header: 'Full name', sample: 'Ada Lovelace' }, { key: 'c1', header: 'E-mail', sample: 'ada@example.com' }, { key: 'c2', header: 'Company', sample: 'Analytical Ltd' }, { key: 'c3', header: 'Notes', sample: 'Met at the conference' }];
const TARGETS = [{ value: 'name', label: 'Name (required)' }, { value: 'email', label: 'Email (required)' }, { value: 'company', label: 'Company' }, { value: 'skip', label: 'Don’t import' }];
const FILE = [
  ['Ada Lovelace', 'ada@example.com', 'Analytical Ltd', ''], ['Grace Hopper', 'grace@example', 'Navy Labs', 'Follow up'], ['Alan Turing', 'alan@example.com', 'Bletchley', ''],
  ['', 'nameless@example.com', 'Unknown', ''], ['Katherine Johnson', 'katherine@example.com', 'NASA', ''], ['Ada Lovelace', 'ada@example.com', 'Analytical Ltd', 'Duplicate'],
  ['Hedy Lamarr', 'hedy@example.com', 'MGM', ''], ['Edsger Dijkstra', 'edsger@@example.com', 'TU Eindhoven', ''],
].map((r, i) => ({ id: 'r' + i, line: i + 2, c0: r[0], c1: r[1], c2: r[2], c3: r[3] }));
const problemsIn = (row, map) => {
  const col = (t) => Object.keys(map).find((k) => map[k] === t);
  const out = [];
  const n = col('name'), e = col('email');
  if (n && !String(row[n]).trim()) out.push({ key: n, message: 'Enter a name.' });
  if (e && !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(String(row[e]))) out.push({ key: e, message: 'This isn’t an email address.' });
  return out;
};
function App() {
  const [step, setStep] = React.useState(0);
  const [queue, setQueue] = React.useState(/** @type {A.UploadItem[]} */ ([]));
  const [map, setMap] = React.useState(/** @type {Record<string, string>} */ ({}));
  const [rows, setRows] = React.useState(FILE);
  const [onlyProblems, setOnlyProblems] = React.useState(false);
  const [progress, setProgress] = React.useState(0);
  const [cancelled, setCancelled] = React.useState(false);
  const stop = React.useRef(false);
  const heading = React.useRef(/** @type {HTMLHeadingElement | null} */ (null));
  const go = (n) => { setStep(n); requestAnimationFrame(() => heading.current?.focus()); };
  const upload = async (name) => {
    setQueue([{ id: 'f', name, state: 'uploading', progress: 40 }]); await wait(300);
    setQueue([{ id: 'f', name, state: 'uploading', progress: 100 }]); await wait(300);
    setQueue([{ id: 'f', name, state: 'done' }]); await wait(300); go(1);
  };
  const missing = ['name', 'email'].filter((t) => !Object.values(map).includes(t)).map((t) => (t === 'name' ? 'Name' : 'Email'));
  const mapped = SOURCE.filter((s) => map[s.key] && map[s.key] !== 'skip');
  const bad = rows.filter((r) => problemsIn(r, map).length);
  const problemCount = rows.reduce((n, r) => n + problemsIn(r, map).length, 0);
  const emailCol = Object.keys(map).find((k) => map[k] === 'email');
  const seen = new Set(), dupes = new Set();
  rows.forEach((r) => { if (problemsIn(r, map).length || !emailCol) return; const e = String(r[emailCol]); if (seen.has(e)) dupes.add(r.id); seen.add(e); });
  const importing = rows.filter((r) => !problemsIn(r, map).length && !dupes.has(r.id));
  const run = async () => {
    go(4); stop.current = false; setCancelled(false);
    for (let i = 1; i <= importing.length; i++) { await wait(350); if (stop.current) { setCancelled(true); return; } setProgress(i); }
    go(5);
  };
  const H = (text) => <h3 ref={heading} tabIndex={-1} className="im-sub">{text}</h3>;
  return (
    <main className="im-page">
      <h2 className="im-h">Import contacts</h2>
      <A.Stepper steps={STEPS} current={step} aria-label="Import steps" onStepChange={step < 4 ? (i) => go(i) : undefined} />
      {step === 0 && <section className="im-step">{H('Upload a CSV file')}
        <A.DropZone accept=".csv,text/csv" multiple={false} onFiles={(f) => f[0] && upload(f[0].name)} queue={queue}>
          <p className="im-note">Drop a .csv file here or choose one. The first row should name the columns.</p>
        </A.DropZone>
        <div><A.Button onClick={() => upload('contacts.csv')}>Use the sample file</A.Button></div>
      </section>}
      {step === 1 && <section className="im-step">{H('Map columns to fields')}
        <p className="im-note">Choose the field each column of contacts.csv goes into.</p>
        <div className="im-map">{SOURCE.map((s) => <A.Field key={s.key} label={s.header} hint={`For example, “${s.sample}”`}>
          <A.Select value={map[s.key] || ''} placeholder="Not mapped" onValueChange={(v) => setMap((m) => ({ ...m, [s.key]: v }))} items={TARGETS} />
        </A.Field>)}</div>
        <p className="im-note" role="status">{missing.length ? `Map these required fields: ${missing.join(', ')}.` : 'Every required field is mapped.'}</p>
        <div><A.Button variant="primary" disabled={missing.length > 0} onClick={() => go(2)}>Continue</A.Button></div>
      </section>}
      {step === 2 && <section className="im-step">{H('Fix problems')}
        <p className="im-note" role="status">{problemCount ? `${problemCount} ${problemCount === 1 ? 'problem' : 'problems'} left in ${bad.length} ${bad.length === 1 ? 'row' : 'rows'}. Rows with problems are skipped.` : 'No problems left.'}</p>
        <A.Switch label="Only rows with problems" checked={onlyProblems} onCheckedChange={setOnlyProblems} />
        <A.DataGrid aria-label="Rows to import" showInvalid rows={onlyProblems ? bad : rows} getRowId={(r) => r.id} getRowLabel={(r) => `row ${r.line}`}
          columns={[{ key: 'line', header: 'Row', align: 'end', width: 64 }, ...mapped.map((s) => ({ key: s.key, header: TARGETS.find((t) => t.value === map[s.key])?.label.replace(' (required)', '') || s.header, editable: true,
            validate: (v, row) => problemsIn({ ...row, [s.key]: v }, map).find((p) => p.key === s.key)?.message }))]}
          onCellSave={({ rowId, key, draft }) => { setRows((all) => all.map((r) => (r.id === rowId ? { ...r, [key]: draft } : r))); return { ok: true }; }} />
        <div className="im-actions"><A.Button onClick={() => go(1)}>Back to mapping</A.Button><A.Button variant="primary" onClick={() => go(3)}>Continue</A.Button></div>
      </section>}
      {step === 3 && <section className="im-step">{H('Review the import')}
        <p className="im-lead">{importing.length} contacts will be imported. {dupes.size + bad.length} rows will be skipped: {dupes.size} {dupes.size === 1 ? 'duplicate' : 'duplicates'} and {bad.length} with problems.</p>
        <div className="im-actions"><A.Button onClick={() => go(2)}>Fix more rows</A.Button><A.Button variant="primary" onClick={run}>Import {importing.length} contacts</A.Button></div>
      </section>}
      {step === 4 && <section className="im-step">{H(cancelled ? 'Import cancelled' : 'Importing contacts')}
        {cancelled ? <><p className="im-lead">{progress} of {importing.length} contacts were imported before you cancelled; the rest weren’t. Importing again skips the ones already imported.</p><div><A.Button onClick={() => { setProgress(0); go(3); }}>Back to review</A.Button></div></> : <>
          <A.Progress value={progress} max={importing.length} aria-label="Importing contacts" />
          <p className="im-note" role="status">Importing {progress} of {importing.length}</p>
          <div><A.Button onClick={() => { stop.current = true; }}>Cancel import</A.Button></div></>}
      </section>}
      {step === 5 && <section className="im-step">{H('Import finished')}
        <dl className="im-receipt"><div><dt>Imported</dt><dd>{importing.length} contacts</dd></div><div><dt>Skipped</dt><dd>{dupes.size} {dupes.size === 1 ? 'duplicate' : 'duplicates'}</dd></div><div><dt>Failed</dt><dd>{bad.length} {bad.length === 1 ? 'row' : 'rows'} with problems</dd></div></dl>
        <div className="im-actions"><A.Button>Download skipped rows (CSV)</A.Button><A.Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => { setQueue([]); setMap({}); setRows(FILE); setProgress(0); go(0); }}>Import another file</A.Button></div>
      </section>}
    </main>
  );
}

=== SyncPattern | <!-- @dsCard group="Patterns" height=720 subtitle="Pending until the server says so, offline queues, conflicts kept, sessions that don't lose drafts" -->
--- css
.sy-page{max-width:720px;margin-inline:auto;display:grid;gap:20px;overflow-wrap:break-word}.sy-head{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:12px;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between}.sy-h{font:600 21px/28px var(--font-sans);margin:0}.sy-sub{font:600 17px/24px var(--font-sans);margin:0}.sy-ch{font:600 15px/22px var(--font-sans);margin:0}.sy-demo{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:12px;align-items:center}.sy-section{display:grid;gap:12px}.sy-tasks{list-style:none;margin:0;padding:0;display:grid;gap:8px}.sy-task{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:12px;padding:10px 14px;border-radius:var(--radius-md)}.sy-state,.sy-note{font:500 13px/18px var(--font-sans);color:var(--ink-muted)}.sy-note{margin:0}.sy-note:empty{display:none}.sy-brief{margin:0;white-space:pre-line;font:400 15px/23px var(--font-sans)}.sy-conflict{padding:16px;display:grid;gap:12px;border-radius:var(--radius-lg)}.sy-versions{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(220px,1fr));gap:12px}.sy-version{padding:12px;border-radius:var(--radius-md);box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 1px var(--line-control)}.sy-version[data-presence="2"]{box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 2px var(--presence-2)}.sy-version p{margin:6px 0 0;font:400 15px/23px var(--font-sans)}.sy-who{display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:center;font:500 13px/18px var(--font-sans);color:var(--ink-muted)}.sy-actions{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px}
--- jsx
const wait = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
function App() {
  const [online, setOnline] = React.useState(true);
  const live = React.useRef(true); live.current = online;
  const [tasks, setTasks] = React.useState([
    { id: 't1', title: 'Draft the launch email', done: true }, { id: 't2', title: 'Book the venue', done: false }, { id: 't3', title: 'Send the press kit', done: false },
  ]);
  const [writes, setWrites] = React.useState(/** @type {Record<string, 'pending' | 'committed' | 'failed'>} */ ({}));
  const [syncedAt, setSyncedAt] = React.useState(new Date());
  const [conflict, setConflict] = React.useState(/** @type {{ mine: string, theirs: string } | null} */ (null));
  const [brief, setBrief] = React.useState('Launch on 4 December, in Lisbon.');
  const [message, setMessage] = React.useState('');
  const [expiring, setExpiring] = React.useState(false);
  const [left, setLeft] = React.useState(120);
  React.useEffect(() => { if (!expiring) return; const t = setInterval(() => setLeft((s) => Math.max(0, s - 1)), 1000); return () => clearInterval(t); }, [expiring]);
  const queue = Object.keys(writes).filter((k) => writes[k] === 'pending');
  const send = async (ids) => {
    await wait(700);
    if (!live.current) return;
    setWrites((w) => { const n = { ...w }; ids.forEach((id) => { n[id] = 'committed'; }); return n; });
    setSyncedAt(new Date());
  };
  const toggle = (id, done) => { setTasks((l) => l.map((t) => (t.id === id ? { ...t, done } : t))); setWrites((w) => ({ ...w, [id]: 'pending' })); void send([id]); };
  const goOnline = (v) => { setOnline(v); live.current = v; if (v && queue.length) void send(queue); };
  const resolve = (choice) => {
    if (!conflict) return;
    setBrief(choice === 'mine' ? conflict.mine : choice === 'theirs' ? conflict.theirs : `${conflict.mine}\n${conflict.theirs}`);
    setMessage(choice === 'mine' ? 'Kept your version. Jane Park’s version is in the history.' : choice === 'theirs' ? 'Kept Jane Park’s version. Yours is in the history.' : 'Kept both versions, one after the other.');
    setConflict(null); setSyncedAt(new Date());
    requestAnimationFrame(() => document.getElementById('sy-brief-h')?.focus());
  };
  const minutes = Math.ceil(left / 60);
  return (
    <main className="sy-page">
      <header className="sy-head"><h2 className="sy-h">Launch plan</h2>
        <A.SyncIndicator state={conflict ? 'conflict' : !online ? 'offline' : queue.length ? 'saving' : 'saved'} pending={queue.length} conflicts={conflict ? 1 : 0} lastSyncedAt={syncedAt}
          onOpenConflicts={() => document.getElementById('sy-conflict-h')?.focus()} />
      </header>
      <div className="sy-demo">
        <A.Switch label="Online" checked={online} onCheckedChange={goOnline} />
        <A.Button size="sm" disabled={!!conflict} onClick={() => setConflict({ mine: 'Launch on 4 December, in Lisbon.', theirs: 'Launch on 11 December, in Porto.' })}>Simulate a conflict</A.Button>
        <A.Button size="sm" onClick={() => { setLeft(120); setExpiring(true); }}>Simulate session expiry</A.Button>
      </div>
      <section aria-labelledby="sy-tasks-h" className="sy-section"><h3 id="sy-tasks-h" className="sy-sub">Tasks</h3>
        <ul className="sy-tasks">{tasks.map((t) => <li key={t.id}><A.Surface level={0} status={writes[t.id]} className="sy-task">
          <A.Checkbox label={t.title} checked={t.done} onCheckedChange={(v) => toggle(t.id, v)} />
          <span className="sy-state">{writes[t.id] === 'pending' ? (online ? 'Saving…' : 'Waiting to sync') : ''}</span>
        </A.Surface></li>)}</ul>
      </section>
      <section aria-labelledby="sy-brief-h" className="sy-section"><h3 id="sy-brief-h" tabIndex={-1} className="sy-sub">Brief</h3>
        {conflict ? <A.Surface level={1} className="sy-conflict" role="group" aria-labelledby="sy-conflict-h">
          <h4 id="sy-conflict-h" tabIndex={-1} className="sy-ch">Changed by Jane Park while you were editing</h4>
          <div className="sy-versions">
            <div className="sy-version"><div className="sy-who">Your version</div><p>{conflict.mine}</p></div>
            <div className="sy-version" data-presence="2"><div className="sy-who"><A.Avatar name="Jane Park" size={20} /> Jane Park’s version</div><p>{conflict.theirs}</p></div>
          </div>
          <div className="sy-actions"><A.Button onClick={() => resolve('mine')}>Keep mine</A.Button><A.Button onClick={() => resolve('theirs')}>Keep theirs</A.Button><A.Button onClick={() => resolve('both')}>Keep both</A.Button></div>
        </A.Surface> : <p className="sy-brief">{brief}</p>}
      </section>
      <p className="sy-note" role="status">{message}</p>
      <A.Dialog open={expiring} background="stationary" dismissible={false} size="sm" onOpenChange={setExpiring}
        title={left > 0 ? `Your session ends in ${minutes} ${minutes === 1 ? 'minute' : 'minutes'}` : 'Your session ended'}
        description={left > 0 ? 'Stay signed in to keep working. Your unsaved changes are kept on this device either way.' : 'Sign in again to continue. Your unsaved changes are kept on this device.'}
        footer={<><A.Button onClick={() => setExpiring(false)}>Sign out</A.Button><A.Button variant="primary" onClick={() => setExpiring(false)}>{left > 0 ? 'Stay signed in' : 'Sign in again'}</A.Button></>} />
    </main>
  );
}

=== DeveloperSettingsPattern | <!-- @dsCard group="Patterns" height=860 subtitle="API keys shown once, rolled with an overlap, revoked with what breaks; webhooks; integrations" -->
--- css
.dv-page{max-width:820px;margin-inline:auto;display:grid;gap:24px;overflow-wrap:break-word}.dv-h{font:600 21px/28px var(--font-sans);margin:0}.dv-sub{font:600 17px/24px var(--font-sans);margin:0}.dv-section{display:grid;gap:12px}.dv-row{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px 12px;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between}.dv-mask{font:500 13px/18px var(--font-mono)}.dv-muted{margin:0;font:400 13px/19px var(--font-sans);color:var(--ink-muted)}.dv-muted:empty{display:none}.dv-actions{display:inline-flex;gap:4px}.dv-vh{position:absolute;width:1px;height:1px;overflow:hidden;clip-path:inset(50%)}.dv-hook{padding:16px;gap:14px}.dv-hook code{font:500 13px/18px var(--font-mono)}.dv-events{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px 20px}.dv-delivery{display:inline-flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap}.dv-apps{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(220px,1fr));gap:16px}.dv-app{padding:16px;gap:8px;align-items:flex-start}.dv-page .alt-table{min-width:600px}

const wait = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
const PAYLOAD = JSON.stringify({ id: 'evt_EXAMPLE_1042', type: 'invoice.paid', created: '2026-10-29T09:12:00Z', data: { invoice: 'INV-0042', amount: 26400, currency: 'eur' } }, null, 2);
function App() {
  const [keys, setKeys] = React.useState([{ id: 'k1', name: 'Production', tail: '4f2a', used: 'Used 3 minutes ago by 3 services' }, { id: 'k2', name: 'CI pipeline', tail: '8d0e', used: 'Used yesterday' }]);
  const [creating, setCreating] = React.useState(/** @type {null | { name: string, secret?: string, saved?: boolean }} */ (null));
  const [confirm, setConfirm] = React.useState(/** @type {null | { kind: 'roll' | 'revoke' | 'disconnect', id: string, busy?: boolean }} */ (null));
  const [status, setStatus] = React.useState('');
  const [deliveries, setDeliveries] = React.useState(/** @type {{ id: string, event: string, at: string, result: 'Delivered' | 'Failed', status?: 'pending' | 'committed' | 'failed', attempt: number }[]} */ ([
    { id: 'd1', event: 'invoice.paid', at: '2026-10-29T09:12:00Z', result: 'Delivered', attempt: 1 },
    { id: 'd2', event: 'customer.updated', at: '2026-10-29T08:40:00Z', result: 'Failed', status: 'failed', attempt: 3 },
  ]));
  const [test, setTest] = React.useState(false);
  const [apps, setApps] = React.useState([{ id: 'chat', name: 'Team chat', about: 'Post alerts to a channel.', state: 'connected' }, { id: 'cal', name: 'Calendar sync', about: 'Add due dates to calendars.', state: 'idle' }, { id: 'store', name: 'Cloud storage', about: 'Attach files from your drive.', state: 'idle' }]);
  const heading = React.useRef(/** @type {HTMLHeadingElement | null} */ (null));
  const key = confirm && keys.find((k) => k.id === confirm.id);
  const app = confirm && apps.find((a) => a.id === confirm.id);
  const create = async () => { if (!creating) return; await wait(500); setCreating({ ...creating, secret: 'ak_live_EXAMPLE_7Hq2Xv9LmR3p' }); };
  const finishCreate = () => { if (!creating) return; setKeys((l) => [...l, { id: 'k' + (l.length + 1), name: creating.name || 'Untitled key', tail: '3p0K', used: 'Never used' }]); setStatus(`Key “${creating.name || 'Untitled key'}” created.`); setCreating(null); };
  const retry = async (id) => {
    setDeliveries((l) => l.map((d) => (d.id === id ? { ...d, status: 'pending', attempt: d.attempt + 1 } : d)));
    await wait(1000);
    setDeliveries((l) => l.map((d) => (d.id === id ? { ...d, status: 'committed', result: 'Delivered' } : d)));
  };
  const act = async () => {
    if (!confirm) return;
    setConfirm({ ...confirm, busy: true }); await wait(700);
    if (confirm.kind === 'revoke') { setKeys((l) => l.filter((k) => k.id !== confirm.id)); setStatus(`Key “${key?.name}” revoked. Requests that use it now fail.`); }
    if (confirm.kind === 'roll') { setKeys((l) => l.map((k) => (k.id === confirm.id ? { ...k, tail: 'Zx81', used: 'New key. The old one works until tomorrow, 09:12' } : k))); setStatus(`Key “${key?.name}” rolled. The old key keeps working for 24 hours.`); }
    if (confirm.kind === 'disconnect') { setApps((l) => l.map((a) => (a.id === confirm.id ? { ...a, state: 'idle' } : a))); setStatus(`${app?.name} disconnected.`); }
    setConfirm(null); requestAnimationFrame(() => heading.current?.focus());
  };
  const connect = async (id) => {
    setApps((l) => l.map((a) => (a.id === id ? { ...a, state: 'pending' } : a)));
    await wait(1200);
    setApps((l) => l.map((a) => (a.id === id ? { ...a, state: 'connected' } : a)));
  };
  return (
    <main className="dv-page">
      <h2 ref={heading} tabIndex={-1} className="dv-h">Developers</h2>
      <section className="dv-section" aria-labelledby="dv-keys-h">
        <div className="dv-row"><h3 id="dv-keys-h" className="dv-sub">API keys</h3><A.Button size="sm" onClick={() => setCreating({ name: '' })}>Create key</A.Button></div>
        <A.Table aria-labelledby="dv-keys-h">
          <A.TableHeader><A.TableRow><A.TableHead>Name</A.TableHead><A.TableHead>Key</A.TableHead><A.TableHead>Last used</A.TableHead><A.TableHead align="end"><span className="dv-vh">Actions</span></A.TableHead></A.TableRow></A.TableHeader>
          <A.TableBody>{keys.map((k) => <A.TableRow key={k.id}><A.TableCell>{k.name}</A.TableCell><A.TableCell><code className="dv-mask">ak_live_••••{k.tail}</code></A.TableCell><A.TableCell className="dv-muted">{k.used}</A.TableCell>
            <A.TableCell align="end"><div className="dv-actions"><A.Button size="sm" variant="ghost" aria-label={`Roll ${k.name} key`} onClick={() => setConfirm({ kind: 'roll', id: k.id })}>Roll</A.Button><A.Button size="sm" variant="ghost" aria-label={`Revoke ${k.name} key`} onClick={() => setConfirm({ kind: 'revoke', id: k.id })}>Revoke</A.Button></div></A.TableCell></A.TableRow>)}</A.TableBody>
        </A.Table>
      </section>
      <section className="dv-section" aria-labelledby="dv-hooks-h">
        <h3 id="dv-hooks-h" className="dv-sub">Webhooks</h3>
        <A.Card className="dv-hook">
          <div className="dv-row"><code>https://api.acme.dev/hooks/altitude</code><A.Badge tone="positive">Active</A.Badge></div>
          <div className="dv-events">{['invoice.paid', 'customer.updated', 'member.removed'].map((e) => <A.Checkbox key={e} label={e} defaultChecked={e !== 'member.removed'} />)}</div>
          <A.Timeline aria-label="Recent deliveries" groupBy="none" locale="en-IE">
            {deliveries.map((d) => <A.TimelineItem key={d.id} time={d.at} status={d.status} onRetry={d.result === 'Failed' ? () => retry(d.id) : undefined}>
              <span className="dv-delivery"><code>{d.event}</code> <A.Badge tone={d.status === 'pending' ? 'neutral' : d.result === 'Delivered' ? 'positive' : 'critical'}>{d.status === 'pending' ? `Retrying (attempt ${d.attempt} of 5)` : d.result}</A.Badge></span>
            </A.TimelineItem>)}
          </A.Timeline>
          <div><A.Button size="sm" onClick={() => setTest(true)}>Send test event</A.Button></div>
          {test && <A.CodeBlock title="Test event sent: invoice.paid" language="json" code={PAYLOAD} />}
        </A.Card>
      </section>
      <section className="dv-section" aria-labelledby="dv-apps-h">
        <h3 id="dv-apps-h" className="dv-sub">Integrations</h3>
        <div className="dv-apps">{apps.map((a) => <A.Card key={a.id} className="dv-app" status={a.state === 'pending' ? 'pending' : undefined}>
          <div className="dv-row"><strong>{a.name}</strong>{a.state === 'connected' && <A.Badge tone="positive">Connected</A.Badge>}</div>
          <p className="dv-muted">{a.about}</p>
          {a.state === 'connected' ? <A.Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => setConfirm({ kind: 'disconnect', id: a.id })}>Disconnect</A.Button>
            : <A.Button size="sm" loading={a.state === 'pending'} onClick={() => connect(a.id)}>{a.state === 'pending' ? 'Waiting for authorization' : `Connect ${a.name}`}</A.Button>}
        </A.Card>)}</div>
      </section>
      <p className="dv-muted" role="status">{status}</p>
      <A.Dialog open={!!creating} dismissible={!creating?.secret || !!creating?.saved} onOpenChange={(o) => { if (!o) setCreating(null); }} title={creating?.secret ? 'Save your new key' : 'Create an API key'}
        footer={creating?.secret ? <A.Button variant="primary" disabled={!creating.saved} onClick={finishCreate}>Done</A.Button> : <><A.Button onClick={() => setCreating(null)}>Cancel</A.Button><A.Button variant="primary" onClick={create}>Create key</A.Button></>}>
        {creating?.secret ? <A.SecretField label="Secret key" value={creating.secret} once warning="You won’t see this key again. Store it somewhere safe, like your secrets manager." onAcknowledge={() => setCreating({ ...creating, saved: true })} />
          : <A.Field label="Key name" hint="Say where it’s used, like “Billing service”."><A.Input value={creating?.name || ''} onChange={(e) => creating && setCreating({ ...creating, name: e.target.value })} /></A.Field>}
      </A.Dialog>
      <A.Dialog open={!!confirm} background="stationary" size="sm" dismissible={!confirm?.busy} onOpenChange={(o) => { if (!o) setConfirm(null); }}
        title={confirm?.kind === 'roll' ? `Roll the ${key?.name} key?` : confirm?.kind === 'revoke' ? `Revoke the ${key?.name} key?` : `Disconnect ${app?.name}?`}
        description={confirm?.kind === 'roll' ? 'You’ll get a new key now. The old key keeps working for 24 hours, so you can update your services first.'
          : confirm?.kind === 'revoke' ? `Requests that use it fail immediately. ${key?.used}.` : `${app?.name} stops receiving updates, and anyone using it loses access to Acme data through it.`}
        footer={<><A.Button disabled={confirm?.busy} onClick={() => setConfirm(null)}>Cancel</A.Button><A.Button variant={confirm?.kind === 'roll' ? 'primary' : 'danger'} loading={confirm?.busy} onClick={act}>{confirm?.kind === 'roll' ? 'Roll key' : confirm?.kind === 'revoke' ? 'Revoke key' : 'Disconnect'}</A.Button></>} />
    </main>
  );
}

=== AgentActionPattern | <!-- @dsCard group="Patterns" height=900 subtitle="Propose, approve, run, recover: each tool-call state is one reading, and provenance stays" -->
--- css
.ag-page{max-width:680px;margin-inline:auto;display:grid;gap:18px;overflow-wrap:break-word}.ag-ask{justify-self:end;max-width:80%;padding:10px 14px;border-radius:var(--radius-lg);background:var(--surface-raised);box-shadow:var(--elev-1)}.ag-ask p{margin:2px 0 0;font:400 15px/23px var(--font-sans)}.ag-who,.ag-muted{font:500 12.5px/17px var(--font-sans);color:var(--ink-muted)}.ag-muted{margin:0}.ag-demo{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:12px;align-items:center}.ag-receipt{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;align-items:center;font:400 14px/20px var(--font-sans)}.ag-record{display:grid;gap:6px;padding-top:12px;border-top:1px solid var(--line)}.ag-sub{font:600 17px/24px var(--font-sans);margin:0}.ag-line{margin:0;display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;font:400 15px/23px var(--font-sans)}
--- jsx
const wait = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
function App() {
  const [failNext, setFailNext] = React.useState(false);
  const fail = React.useRef(false); fail.current = failNext;
  const [search, setSearch] = React.useState(/** @type {'running' | 'done' | 'failed'} */ ('running'));
  const [archive, setArchive] = React.useState(/** @type {'planned' | 'running' | 'done'} */ ('planned'));
  const [undone, setUndone] = React.useState(false);
  const [email, setEmail] = React.useState(/** @type {A.ConfirmationState} */ ('requested'));
  const [purge, setPurge] = React.useState(/** @type {A.ConfirmationState} */ ('requested'));
  const runSearch = async () => { setSearch('running'); await wait(900); if (fail.current) { setFailNext(false); setSearch('failed'); return; } setSearch('done'); setArchive('running'); await wait(800); setArchive('done'); };
  const restart = () => { setArchive('planned'); setUndone(false); setEmail('requested'); setPurge('requested'); void runSearch(); };
  React.useEffect(() => { void runSearch(); }, []);
  const resolve = async (set, ok) => { set('running'); await wait(900); set(ok ? 'done' : 'failed'); };
  const stepFor = (c) => (c === 'requested' ? 'awaiting-approval' : c === 'running' || c === 'approved' ? 'running' : c === 'failed' ? 'failed' : 'done');
  const ready = archive === 'done';
  return (
    <main className="ag-page">
      <div className="ag-ask"><span className="ag-who">You asked</span><p>Clean up leads nobody has touched since July, and tell their owners.</p></div>
      <div className="ag-demo"><A.Switch label="Make the next step fail" checked={failNext} onCheckedChange={setFailNext} /><A.Button size="sm" onClick={restart}>Run again</A.Button></div>
      <A.Trace aria-label="Assistant run: clean up stale leads">
        <A.TraceStep title="Find leads untouched since July" status={search} duration={search === 'done' ? 900 : undefined}
          error={search === 'failed' ? 'The CRM didn’t answer in time. Nothing was changed.' : undefined} onRetry={search === 'failed' ? runSearch : undefined}>
          <A.TraceToolCall name="search_leads" input={{ untouchedSince: '2026-07-01', limit: 50 }} output={search === 'done' ? { found: 4 } : undefined} />
        </A.TraceStep>
        <A.TraceStep title="Archive 4 stale leads" status={archive}>
          {archive === 'done' && <div className="ag-receipt"><span role="status">{undone ? 'Archive undone. The 4 leads are back.' : '4 leads archived. You can undo this for 30 days.'}</span>
            {!undone && <A.Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => setUndone(true)}>Undo archive</A.Button>}</div>}
        </A.TraceStep>
        <A.TraceStep title={email === 'rejected' ? 'Didn’t email the lead owners' : 'Email 3 lead owners'} status={ready ? stepFor(email) : 'planned'}>
          {ready && <A.Confirmation state={email} title="Email 3 lead owners" requestedBy="The assistant" resolvedBy="Maya Chen"
            consequence="Theo Marsh, Ines Duarte and Sam Okafor will get an email listing the leads that were archived."
            approveLabel="Send 3 emails" rejectLabel="Don’t send" runningLabel="Sending…" doneLabel="3 emails sent" failedLabel="The emails weren’t sent."
            onApprove={() => resolve(setEmail, !fail.current)} onReject={() => setEmail('rejected')} onRetry={() => resolve(setEmail, true)} />}
        </A.TraceStep>
        <A.TraceStep title="Delete the “Summer import” list for good" status={ready && email !== 'requested' ? stepFor(purge) : 'planned'}>
          {ready && email !== 'requested' && <A.Confirmation state={purge} escalate="dialog" title="Delete the “Summer import” list" resolvedBy="Maya Chen"
            consequence="The list and its 212 rows are deleted permanently. Leads in it stay; only the list goes."
            approveLabel="Delete list" rejectLabel="Keep the list" runningLabel="Deleting…" doneLabel="List deleted"
            onApprove={() => resolve(setPurge, true)} onReject={() => setPurge('rejected')} />}
        </A.TraceStep>
      </A.Trace>
      {email === 'done' && <section className="ag-record" aria-labelledby="ag-rec-h"><h3 id="ag-rec-h" className="ag-sub">Northwind Traders</h3>
        <p className="ag-line">Status: Archived <A.AILabel subject="Change" model="Opus 5.5" reviewedBy="Maya Chen" generatedAt="2026-10-30T09:00:00Z" /></p>
        <p className="ag-muted">Archived by the assistant, approved by Maya Chen.</p>
      </section>}
    </main>
  );
}

=== OnboardingPattern | <!-- @dsCard group="Patterns" height=980 subtitle="A setup checklist the app stamps, empty states before tours, and what's new" -->
--- css

const TASKS = [
  { id: 'project', title: 'Create a project', about: 'Somewhere to keep your work.' },
  { id: 'invite', title: 'Invite your team', about: 'Work goes faster together.' },
  { id: 'connect', title: 'Connect a data source', about: 'Bring in what you already have.', action: 'Connect a data source' },
  { id: 'billing', title: 'Add billing details', about: 'Needed before your trial ends on 14 November.', action: 'Add billing details' },
];
function App() {
  const [done, setDone] = React.useState(/** @type {Record<string, boolean>} */ ({ project: true, invite: true }));
  const [guide, setGuide] = React.useState(true);
  const [sources, setSources] = React.useState(/** @type {string[]} */ ([]));
  const [tour, setTour] = React.useState(false);
  const [seen, setSeen] = React.useState(false);
  const count = TASKS.filter((t) => done[t.id]).length;
  const happen = (id) => setDone((d) => ({ ...d, [id]: true }));
  return (
    <main className="ob-page">
      <header className="ob-head"><h2 className="ob-h">Acme workspace</h2>
        <div className="ob-tools">
          <A.Popover aria-label="What’s new" align="end" onOpenChange={(o) => { if (o) setSeen(true); }}
            trigger={<A.Button size="sm" variant="ghost" id="ob-new">What’s new{!seen && <A.Badge variant="count" aria-label="2 unseen">2</A.Badge>}</A.Button>}>
            <div className="ob-news"><A.Timeline aria-label="Recent changes" groupBy="none" locale="en-IE">
              <A.TimelineItem key="n1" time="2026-10-28T09:00:00Z" isNew={!seen}>Saved views: keep a filter and come back to it</A.TimelineItem>
              <A.TimelineItem key="n2" time="2026-10-21T09:00:00Z" isNew={!seen}>The sidebar now groups projects by team</A.TimelineItem>
              <A.TimelineItem key="n3" time="2026-10-07T09:00:00Z">Export any table as CSV</A.TimelineItem>
            </A.Timeline></div>
          </A.Popover>
          <A.Button size="sm" variant="ghost" id="ob-help" onClick={() => setGuide(true)}>Help: setup guide</A.Button>
        </div>
      </header>
      {guide && <A.Card className="ob-guide" aria-labelledby="ob-guide-h">
        <div className="ob-row"><h3 id="ob-guide-h" className="ob-sub">Set up your workspace</h3>
          <A.Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => setGuide(false)}>{count === TASKS.length ? 'Hide setup guide' : 'Skip for now'}</A.Button></div>
        <A.Meter label="Setup" value={count} max={TASKS.length} format={(v) => `${v}`} />
        <p className="ob-note">Tasks are checked off when you do them, wherever you do them.</p>
        <ol className="ob-tasks">{TASKS.map((t) => <li key={t.id} className="ob-task" data-done={done[t.id] ? '' : undefined}>
          <div><div className="ob-title">{t.title}</div><div className="ob-note">{t.about}</div></div>
          {done[t.id] ? <A.Badge tone="positive">Done</A.Badge> : t.action ? <A.Button size="sm" onClick={() => document.getElementById('ob-' + t.id)?.scrollIntoView({ block: 'center' })}>{t.action}</A.Button> : null}
        </li>)}</ol>
      </A.Card>}
      {!guide && <p className="ob-note" role="status">Setup guide hidden. Open it again any time from Help.</p>}
      <section className="ob-section" aria-labelledby="ob-sources-h" id="ob-connect">
        <h3 id="ob-sources-h" className="ob-sub">Data sources</h3>
        {sources.length ? <ul className="ob-list">{sources.map((s) => <li key={s}>{s}</li>)}</ul>
          : <A.EmptyState size="sm" title="No data sources yet" description="Connect a database or a spreadsheet and its tables appear here."
              action={<A.Button variant="primary" onClick={() => { setSources(['Sales database']); happen('connect'); }}>Connect a data source</A.Button>} />}
      </section>
      <section className="ob-section" aria-labelledby="ob-billing-h" id="ob-billing">
        <h3 id="ob-billing-h" className="ob-sub">Billing</h3>
        {done.billing ? <p className="ob-note">Card ending 4242 on file.</p> : <div><A.Button onClick={() => happen('billing')}>Add billing details</A.Button></div>}
      </section>
      <nav aria-label="Projects" className="ob-nav" id="ob-sidebar"><span className="ob-note">Design team</span><A.Link href="#/p/1">Website refresh</A.Link><A.Link href="#/p/2">Brand kit</A.Link></nav>
      <div><A.Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => setTour(true)}>Show me what changed in the sidebar</A.Button></div>
      <A.Tour open={tour} onOpenChange={setTour} steps={[
        { target: '#ob-sidebar', title: 'Projects are grouped by team', body: 'Each team’s projects sit under its name. Drag a project to move it.' },
        { target: '#ob-new', title: 'Find changes here', body: 'What’s new lists every change, so you can come back to this later.' },
      ]} />
    </main>
  );
}

=== AppSkeleton | <!-- @dsCard group="Patterns" height=720 subtitle="A starting point: shell, page header, a filtered and paged table, a record in a sheet, settings" -->

.sk-frame{height:680px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:var(--radius-lg);overflow:hidden}.sk-ws{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:6px;font:600 14px/20px var(--font-sans)}.sk-search{flex:1;max-width:360px;justify-content:flex-start;color:var(--ink-muted)}.sk-body{display:grid;gap:16px;padding-bottom:96px;min-width:0}.sk-body .alt-table{min-width:560px}

const I = ({ d }) => <svg width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.75" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d={d} /></svg>;
const ICONS = { customers: 'M16 19v-1a4 4 0 0 0-8 0v1M12 11a3 3 0 1 0 0-6 3 3 0 0 0 0 6', reports: 'M5 20V10M12 20V4M19 20v-7', settings: 'M4 7h10M18 7h2M4 17h4M12 17h8M14 5v4M8 15v4' };
const PLANS = [{ value: 'Free', label: 'Free' }, { value: 'Pro', label: 'Pro' }, { value: 'Business', label: 'Business' }];
const PEOPLE = ['Ada Lovelace', 'Grace Hopper', 'Alan Turing', 'Katherine Johnson', 'Hedy Lamarr', 'Edsger Dijkstra', 'Barbara Liskov', 'Donald Knuth', 'Margaret Hamilton', 'Ken Thompson', 'Radia Perlman', 'Tim Berners-Lee'];
const SEED = PEOPLE.map((name, i) => ({ id: 'c' + i, name, email: name.split(' ')[0].toLowerCase() + '@example.com', plan: PLANS[i % 3].value, seats: 3 + ((i * 7) % 40) }));
const FIELDS = /** @type {A.FilterField[]} */ ([{ key: 'plan', label: 'Plan', type: 'enum', options: PLANS }, { key: 'name', label: 'Name', type: 'text' }]);
const matches = (row, f) => f.value === undefined || f.value === '' || (f.operator === 'is' ? row[f.field] === f.value : f.operator === 'is not' ? row[f.field] !== f.value
  : f.operator === 'contains' ? String(row[f.field]).toLowerCase().includes(String(f.value).toLowerCase()) : !String(row[f.field]).toLowerCase().includes(String(f.value).toLowerCase()));
function Customers() {
  const [rows, setRows] = React.useState(SEED);
  const [filters, setFilters] = React.useState(/** @type {A.Filter[]} */ ([]));
  const [page, setPage] = React.useState(1);
  const [selected, setSelected] = React.useState(/** @type {string[]} */ ([]));
  const [open, setOpen] = React.useState(/** @type {string | null} */ (null));
  const shown = rows.filter((r) => filters.every((f) => matches(r, f)));
  const pageCount = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(shown.length / 5));
  const record = rows.find((r) => r.id === open);
  const save = (key) => async (value) => { await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 400)); setRows((l) => l.map((r) => (r.id === open ? { ...r, [key]: value } : r))); return /** @type {const} */ ({ ok: true, value }); };
  return <>
    <A.PageHeader primary={<A.Button variant="primary">Add customer</A.Button>} actions={<A.Button variant="secondary">Export</A.Button>}>
      <A.PageHeaderTitle>Customers</A.PageHeaderTitle><A.PageHeaderDescription>{rows.length} customers on every plan.</A.PageHeaderDescription>
    </A.PageHeader>
    <div className="sk-body">
      <A.FilterBar aria-label="Filter customers" fields={FIELDS} value={filters} onValueChange={(f) => { setFilters(f); setPage(1); }} resultCount={shown.length} />
      <A.DataTable aria-label="Customers" noun="customers" rows={shown.slice((page - 1) * 5, page * 5)} getRowId={(r) => r.id} getRowLabel={(r) => r.name}
        selectable selected={selected} onSelectedChange={setSelected} matchingTotal={shown.length}
        actions={(ids) => <A.Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => setSelected([])}>Email {ids.length}</A.Button>}
        onRowClick={(r) => setOpen(r.id)} empty="No customers match these filters."
        columns={[{ key: 'name', header: 'Name' }, { key: 'email', header: 'Email' }, { key: 'plan', header: 'Plan', cell: (r) => <A.Badge>{r.plan}</A.Badge> }, { key: 'seats', header: 'Seats', align: 'end' }]}
        pagination={{ page, pageCount, total: shown.length, onPageChange: setPage, noun: 'customers' }} />
    </div>
    <A.Sheet open={!!record} onOpenChange={(o) => { if (!o) setOpen(null); }} size="md">
      {record && <>
        <A.SheetHeader><A.SheetTitle>{record.name}</A.SheetTitle><A.SheetDescription>{record.email}</A.SheetDescription></A.SheetHeader>
        <A.PropertyList aria-label={`${record.name}’s details`}>
          <A.PropertyListItem label="Plan" value={record.plan} commit="on-choose" onSave={save('plan')} editor={(c) => <A.Select {...c} items={PLANS} />} />
          <A.PropertyListItem label="Seats" value={String(record.seats)} onSave={save('seats')} validate={(v) => (/^\d+$/.test(v) ? undefined : 'Enter a whole number.')} />
          <A.PropertyListItem label="Email" value={record.email} />
        </A.PropertyList>
      </>}
    </A.Sheet>
  </>;
}
function Settings() {
  const general = A.useEditRecord({ initialValue: { name: 'Acme', url: 'acme' }, validationMode: 'submit',
    validate: (d) => (d.name.trim() ? {} : { name: 'Enter a workspace name.' }), save: async (d) => { await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 600)); return { ok: true, value: d }; } });
  const [digest, setDigest] = React.useState(true);
  return <>
    <A.PageHeader><A.PageHeaderTitle>Settings</A.PageHeaderTitle><A.PageHeaderDescription>Changes to the workspace are saved together.</A.PageHeaderDescription></A.PageHeader>
    <div className="sk-body">
      <A.SettingsSection title="Workspace">
        <A.SettingsRow label="Workspace name" error={general.errors.name}><A.Input value={general.draft.name} onChange={(e) => general.setField('name', e.target.value)} /></A.SettingsRow>
        <A.SettingsRow label="Workspace URL" description={'altitude.app/' + general.draft.url}><A.Input value={general.draft.url} onChange={(e) => general.setField('url', e.target.value)} /></A.SettingsRow>
      </A.SettingsSection>
      <A.SettingsSection title="Notifications" description="Applies as soon as you change it.">
        <A.SettingsRow label="Weekly digest" description="A summary every Monday."><A.Switch checked={digest} onCheckedChange={setDigest} /></A.SettingsRow>
      </A.SettingsSection>
    </div>
    <A.SaveBar record={general} />
  </>;
}
function App() {
  const [at, setAt] = React.useState('customers');
  const item = (key, label) => <A.SidebarMenuItem key={key}>
    <A.SidebarMenuButton href={'#/' + key} current={at === key} icon={<I d={ICONS[key]} />} onClick={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); setAt(key); }}>{label}</A.SidebarMenuButton>
  </A.SidebarMenuItem>;
  return (
    <div className="sk-frame">
      <A.AppShell
        sidebar={<A.Sidebar>
          <A.SidebarHeader><span className="sk-ws"><A.Avatar name="Acme" size={24} /><span className="alt-sidebar-text">Acme</span></span></A.SidebarHeader>
          <A.SidebarContent><A.SidebarGroup label="Workspace"><A.SidebarMenu>{item('customers', 'Customers')}{item('reports', 'Reports')}{item('settings', 'Settings')}</A.SidebarMenu></A.SidebarGroup></A.SidebarContent>
          <A.SidebarFooter><A.SidebarMenu><A.SidebarMenuItem><A.SidebarMenuButton href="#/me" icon={<A.Avatar name="Maya Chen" size={20} />}>Maya Chen</A.SidebarMenuButton></A.SidebarMenuItem></A.SidebarMenu></A.SidebarFooter>
        </A.Sidebar>}
        topBar={<A.Button variant="secondary" size="sm" className="sk-search">Search… <A.Kbd>⌘K</A.Kbd></A.Button>}>
        {at === 'customers' ? <Customers /> : at === 'settings' ? <Settings /> : <>
          <A.PageHeader><A.PageHeaderTitle>Reports</A.PageHeaderTitle></A.PageHeader>
          <div className="sk-body"><A.EmptyState title="No reports yet" description="Reports you save appear here." action={<A.Button variant="primary">Create a report</A.Button>} /></div>
        </>}
      </A.AppShell>
    </div>
  );
}

=== EditRegion | <!-- @dsCard group="Inputs" height=900 subtitle="Edit several fields of one record in place, saved together as one write" -->
