const ER_ROLES = [{ value: 'owner', label: 'Owner' }, { value: 'editor', label: 'Editor' }, { value: 'viewer', label: 'Viewer' }];
const ER_PEOPLE = Object.freeze([
  Object.freeze({ id: 'p-mara', name: 'Mara Quist', email: 'mara@northwind.example', role: 'owner', notify: true, revision: 1 }),
  Object.freeze({ id: 'p-ines', name: 'Inés Ortega', email: 'ines@northwind.example', role: 'editor', notify: false, revision: 1 }),
  Object.freeze({ id: 'p-tobi', name: 'Tobi Adeyemi', email: 'tobi@northwind.example', role: 'viewer', notify: true, revision: 1 }),
]);
const ER_FIXTURES = ['success', 'slow', 'fail-once', 'conflict-during-save'];
const erWait = ms => new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
const erFields = ({ revision, id, ...fields }) => fields;

// A fictional people service with revisions: a stale write is rejected, an accepted one returns the canonical record.
function EditRegionExample({ fixture }) {
  const server = React.useRef(new Map(ER_PEOPLE.map(p => [p.id, { ...p }])));
  const [people, setPeople] = React.useState(() => ER_PEOPLE.map(p => ({ ...p })));
  const [log, setLog] = React.useState([]);
  const failed = React.useRef(false);
  const trace = event => setLog(log => [...log, event]);
  const reconcile = id => setPeople(people => people.map(p => p.id === id ? { ...server.current.get(id) } : p));
  const externalUpdate = () => {
    const current = server.current.get('p-mara');
    server.current.set('p-mara', { ...current, email: 'm.quist@northwind.example', revision: current.revision + 1 });
    trace({ event: 'external-update', id: 'p-mara', revision: current.revision + 1 }); reconcile('p-mara');
  };
  const save = person => async (draft, { changed }) => {
    trace({ event: 'requested', id: person.id, expectedRevision: person.revision, changed });
    await erWait(fixture === 'slow' ? 1400 : 250);
    if (fixture === 'fail-once' && !failed.current) { failed.current = true; trace({ event: 'rejected', id: person.id }); return { ok: false, error: 'We couldn’t reach the people service. Your changes are still here. Try again.' }; }
    if (fixture === 'conflict-during-save') { externalUpdate(); await erWait(300); }
    const current = server.current.get(person.id);
    if (current.revision !== person.revision) { trace({ event: 'rejected-stale', id: person.id }); return { ok: false, error: 'Someone else changed this person. Review the current details.' }; }
    if ([...server.current.values()].some(p => p.id !== person.id && p.email === draft.email.trim())) return { ok: false, error: 'Check the highlighted field.', fieldErrors: { email: 'Someone else already uses this email.' } };
    const next = { ...current, ...draft, name: draft.name.trim(), email: draft.email.trim(), revision: current.revision + 1 };
    server.current.set(person.id, next);
    trace({ event: 'committed', id: person.id, revision: next.revision, changed });
    return { ok: true, value: erFields(next) };
  };
  const validate = draft => {
    const errors = {};
    if (!draft.name.trim()) errors.name = 'Enter a name.';
    if (!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(draft.email.trim())) errors.email = 'Enter an email address, like name@example.com.';
    return Object.keys(errors).length ? errors : undefined;
  };
  return <>
    <section aria-labelledby="er-title">
      <h2 id="er-title">People with access</h2>
      <ul className="er-list">
        {people.map(person => <li key={person.id}>
          <A.EditRegion layout="in-place" label={person.name} editLabel={`Edit ${person.name}`} value={erFields(person)} validate={validate}
            onValueChange={canonical => setPeople(people => people.map(p => p.id === person.id ? { ...server.current.get(person.id), ...canonical } : p))}
            onSave={save(person)}>
            <A.EditRegionView>
              <span className="er-name"><A.EditRegionValue field="name">{person.name}</A.EditRegionValue></span>
              <span className="er-meta"><A.EditRegionValue field="email">{person.email}</A.EditRegionValue> · <A.EditRegionValue field="role">{ER_ROLES.find(r => r.value === person.role).label}</A.EditRegionValue>{person.notify ? <> · <A.EditRegionValue field="notify">Gets updates</A.EditRegionValue></> : ''}</span>
            </A.EditRegionView>
            <A.EditRegionForm>
              {field => <>
                <div className="er-name-edit"><A.Field label="Name" required error={field.error('name')}><A.Input {...field('name')} autoComplete="off" /></A.Field></div>
                <div className="er-meta-edit">
                  <A.Field label="Email" required error={field.error('email')}><A.Input {...field('email')} type="email" autoComplete="off" /></A.Field>
                  <A.Field label="Role"><A.Select {...field.value('role')} items={ER_ROLES} /></A.Field>
                  <A.Switch {...field.checked('notify')} label="Gets updates" />
                </div>
              </>}
            </A.EditRegionForm>
          </A.EditRegion>
        </li>)}
      </ul>
    </section>
    <TripSheet />
    <details className="er-harness">
      <summary>Fixture controls</summary>
      <p>Documentation harness only: change Mara's record on the “server” while it's being edited.</p>
      <div className="er-harness-controls"><A.Button onClick={externalUpdate}>Apply external update to Mara</A.Button></div>
      <output className="er-log" data-er-log="" aria-label="Fixture operation log">{JSON.stringify(log, null, 2)}</output>
    </details>
  </>;
}

// Labelled rows whose values already have their controls' height (EditRegionValue reserve): Edit all moves nothing.
const ER_SEATS = [{ value: 'aisle', label: 'Aisle' }, { value: 'window', label: 'Window' }, { value: 'any', label: 'No preference' }];
function TripSheet() {
  const [trip, setTrip] = React.useState({ traveller: 'Mara Quist', email: 'mara@northwind.example', phone: '', seat: 'aisle' });
  const validate = draft => /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(draft.email.trim()) ? undefined : { email: 'Enter an email address, like name@example.com.' };
  const rows = [['traveller', 'Traveller'], ['email', 'Email'], ['phone', 'Phone'], ['seat', 'Seat']];
  return <section className="er-sheet" aria-labelledby="er-sheet-title">
    <A.EditRegion layout="in-place" label="Trip details" editLabel="Edit all trip details" value={trip} validate={validate} onValueChange={setTrip}
      onSave={async draft => { await erWait(250); return { ok: true, value: { ...draft, traveller: draft.traveller.trim(), email: draft.email.trim(), phone: draft.phone.trim() } }; }}>
      <A.EditRegionView trigger={false}>
        <div className="er-sheet-head"><h2 id="er-sheet-title">Trip details</h2><A.EditRegionTrigger>Edit all</A.EditRegionTrigger></div>
        <dl className="er-sheet-grid">{rows.map(([key, label]) => <React.Fragment key={key}><dt>{label}</dt><dd>
          <A.EditRegionValue field={key} reserve>{key === 'seat' ? ER_SEATS.find(s => s.value === trip.seat).label : trip[key] || <span className="er-meta">Not set</span>}</A.EditRegionValue>
        </dd></React.Fragment>)}</dl>
      </A.EditRegionView>
      <A.EditRegionForm>
        {field => <>
          <div className="er-sheet-head"><h2>Trip details</h2></div>
          <dl className="er-sheet-grid">
            <dt>Traveller</dt><dd><A.Field label="Traveller" required error={field.error('traveller')}><A.Input {...field('traveller')} autoComplete="off" /></A.Field></dd>
            <dt>Email</dt><dd><A.Field label="Email" required error={field.error('email')}><A.Input {...field('email')} type="email" autoComplete="off" /></A.Field></dd>
            <dt>Phone</dt><dd><A.Field label="Phone"><A.Input {...field('phone')} type="tel" placeholder="Add a phone number" autoComplete="off" /></A.Field></dd>
            <dt>Seat</dt><dd><A.Field label="Seat"><A.Select {...field.value('seat')} items={ER_SEATS} /></A.Field></dd>
          </dl>
        </>}
      </A.EditRegionForm>
    </A.EditRegion>
  </section>;
}

function App() {
  const [fixture, setFixture] = React.useState('success');
  const [mounted, setMounted] = React.useState({ fixture: 'success', epoch: 0 });
  return <div className="er-demo">
    <EditRegionExample key={mounted.epoch} fixture={mounted.fixture} />
    <details className="er-harness">
      <summary>Choose the next fixture</summary>
      <div className="er-harness-controls">
        <label>Save fixture<select value={fixture} onChange={event => setFixture(event.target.value)}>{ER_FIXTURES.map(name => <option key={name}>{name}</option>)}</select></label>
        <A.Button onClick={() => setMounted({ fixture, epoch: mounted.epoch + 1 })}>Reset example</A.Button>
      </div>
    </details>
  </div>;
}

=== RemovalReceipt | <!-- @dsCard group="Display" height=520 subtitle="An item that leaves its list: the result in its own slot, held, then the gap glides closed" -->
--- css
.rr { max-width: 640px; }
.rr h3 { font: 600 17px/24px var(--font-sans); margin: 0 0 var(--space-2); color: var(--ink); }
.rr p.rr-hint { font: 400 13px/18px var(--font-sans); color: var(--ink-muted); margin: 0 0 var(--space-3); }
.rr ul { list-style: none; margin: 0; padding: 0; border-top: 1px solid var(--line); }
.rr li { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: var(--space-3); padding: var(--space-3) 0; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line); }
.rr .rr-body { flex: 1; min-width: 0; }
.rr .rr-name { font: 500 14.5px/21px var(--font-sans); color: var(--ink); }
.rr .rr-url { font: 400 12.5px/17px var(--font-mono); color: var(--ink-muted); overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
.rr .rr-empty { padding: var(--space-5) var(--space-3); color: var(--ink-muted); font: 400 13.5px/20px var(--font-sans); }
--- jsx
const HOOKS = [
  { id: 'h1', name: 'Deploy notifications', url: 'https://hooks.example.com/deploys' },
  { id: 'h2', name: 'Billing events', url: 'https://hooks.example.com/billing' },
  { id: 'h3', name: 'New sign-ups', url: 'https://hooks.example.com/signups' },
  { id: 'h4', name: 'Error alerts', url: 'https://hooks.example.com/errors' },
];
// A fictional service: removing and restoring a webhook each take 400ms.
const later = (value) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(() => resolve(value), 400));
function App() {
  const [hooks, setHooks] = React.useState(HOOKS);
  const [receipts, setReceipts] = React.useState({});
  const [back, setBack] = React.useState(null);
  const put = (id, value) => setReceipts((r) => { const next = { ...r }; if (value) next[id] = value; else delete next[id]; return next; });
  const remove = async (hook) => { put(hook.id, { pending: true }); await later(); put(hook.id, { pending: false }); };
  const undo = async (hook) => { put(hook.id, { pending: true, undoing: true }); await later(); put(hook.id, null); };
  const restore = async (hook) => {
    await later();
    setHooks((list) => HOOKS.filter((h) => h.id === hook.id || list.some((x) => x.id === h.id)));
    setBack(hook.id);
  };
  return (
    <section className="rr" aria-labelledby="rr-title">
      <h3 id="rr-title">Webhooks</h3>
      <p className="rr-hint">Remove one: its result stays in its place, then the list closes up. Point at the result to keep it.</p>
      <ul>
        {hooks.map((hook) => {
          const receipt = receipts[hook.id];
          return (
            <A.RemovalReceipt as="li" key={hook.id} removed={!!receipt} pending={receipt?.pending} arrive={back === hook.id}
              title={receipt?.undoing ? `Restoring ${hook.name}…` : receipt?.pending ? `Removing ${hook.name}…` : `${hook.name} removed.`}
              action={receipt && (!receipt.pending || receipt.undoing) &&
                <A.Button size="sm" loading={!!receipt.undoing} aria-label={`Undo removing ${hook.name}`} onClick={() => undo(hook)}>Undo</A.Button>}
              onClosed={() => {
                put(hook.id, null); setHooks((list) => list.filter((h) => h.id !== hook.id));
                A.toast({ title: `${hook.name} removed.`, action: { label: 'Undo', onClick: () => restore(hook) } });
              }}>
              <div className="rr-body"><div className="rr-name">{hook.name}</div><div className="rr-url">{hook.url}</div></div>
              <A.Button size="sm" variant="ghost" aria-label={`Remove ${hook.name}`} onClick={() => remove(hook)}>Remove</A.Button>
            </A.RemovalReceipt>
          );
        })}
      </ul>
      {!hooks.length && <p className="rr-empty">No webhooks.</p>}
      <A.Toaster />
    </section>
  );
}

=== ItemGroup | <!-- @dsCard group="Display" height=760 subtitle="A list changed in the list: edit a slat in place, add, reorder, remove with Undo where it was" -->
--- css
.ig-demo { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(min(300px, 100%), 1fr)); gap: 32px; max-width: 900px; }
.ig-demo h3 { margin: 0 0 8px; font: 600 15px/22px var(--font-sans); color: var(--ink); }
.ig-url { font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 12px; }
--- jsx
const IG_HOOKS = [
  { id: 'h1', name: 'Production', url: 'https://api.northwind.example/hooks/prod', revision: 1 },
  { id: 'h2', name: 'Staging', url: 'https://staging.northwind.example/hooks', revision: 1 },
  { id: 'h3', name: 'Billing events', url: 'https://billing.northwind.example/in', revision: 1 },
];
const igWait = ms => new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
const HookIcon = () => <svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.5" strokeLinecap="round"><path d="M6 4.5a2.5 2.5 0 1 1 3 2.4L7 11" /><path d="M4 12a2.5 2.5 0 1 1 2-4" /><path d="M12 12.5H8" /><circle cx="12" cy="12.5" r="1.5" /></svg>;
function App() {
  const [hooks, setHooks] = React.useState(IG_HOOKS);
  const [options, setOptions] = React.useState([]);
  const trash = React.useRef(new Map());
  const seq = React.useRef(0);
  const validate = draft => ({ ...(draft.name.trim() ? {} : { name: 'Enter a name.' }), ...(/^https:\/\/[^\s/]+\.[^\s]+$/.test(draft.url) ? {} : { url: 'Use an https:// address.' }) });
  return <div className="ig-demo">
    <section>
      <h3 id="ig-hooks">Webhooks</h3>
      <A.ItemGroup aria-labelledby="ig-hooks" items={hooks} getKey={h => h.id} getLabel={h => h.name}
        renderItem={(h, { editProps, removeProps }) => <A.Item>
          <A.ItemMedia><HookIcon /></A.ItemMedia>
          <A.ItemContent><A.ItemTitle>{h.name}</A.ItemTitle><A.ItemDescription className="ig-url">{h.url}</A.ItemDescription></A.ItemContent>
          <A.ItemActions><A.Button size="sm" variant="ghost" {...editProps}>Edit</A.Button><A.Button size="sm" variant="ghost" {...removeProps}>Remove</A.Button></A.ItemActions>
        </A.Item>}
        renderEditor={field => <>
          <A.Field label="Name" required error={field.error('name')}><A.Input {...field('name')} /></A.Field>
          <A.Field label="Endpoint URL" required error={field.error('url')}><A.Input {...field('url')} type="url" /></A.Field>
        </>}
        onSave={async (draft, { previousValue }) => {
          const errors = validate(draft); if (Object.keys(errors).length) return { ok: false, error: 'Check the highlighted fields.', fieldErrors: errors };
          await igWait(300); return { ok: true, value: { ...draft, name: draft.name.trim(), revision: previousValue.revision + 1 } };
        }}
        onItemChange={h => setHooks(list => list.map(x => x.id === h.id ? h : x))}
        newItem={() => ({ id: '', name: '', url: 'https://', revision: 0 })}
        onCreate={async draft => {
          const errors = validate(draft); if (Object.keys(errors).length) return { ok: false, error: 'Check the highlighted fields.', fieldErrors: errors };
          await igWait(300); const hook = { ...draft, id: 'n' + ++seq.current, name: draft.name.trim(), revision: 1 }; setHooks(list => [...list, hook]); return { ok: true, value: hook };
        }}
        addLabel="Add webhook"
        onReorder={async next => { await igWait(200); setHooks(next); }}
        onRemove={async h => { await igWait(200); trash.current.set(h.id, hooks.indexOf(h)); setHooks(list => list.filter(x => x.id !== h.id)); }}
        removal={{ kind: 'recoverable', undo: h => setHooks(list => { const next = [...list]; next.splice(trash.current.get(h.id) ?? next.length, 0, h); return next; }) }} />
      <A.Toaster />
    </section>
    <section>
      <h3 id="ig-options">Answer options</h3>
      <A.ItemGroup aria-labelledby="ig-options" items={options} getKey={o => o.id} getLabel={o => o.label}
        renderItem={(o, { editProps, removeProps }) => <A.Item><A.ItemContent><A.ItemTitle>{o.label}</A.ItemTitle></A.ItemContent>
          <A.ItemActions><A.Button size="sm" variant="ghost" {...editProps}>Edit</A.Button><A.Button size="sm" variant="ghost" {...removeProps}>Delete</A.Button></A.ItemActions></A.Item>}
        renderEditor={field => <A.Field label="Option" required error={field.error('label')}><A.Input {...field('label')} /></A.Field>}
        onItemChange={o => setOptions(list => list.map(x => x.id === o.id ? o : x))}
        newItem={() => ({ id: '', label: '' })}
        onCreate={draft => { if (!draft.label.trim()) return { ok: false, error: 'Enter an option.', fieldErrors: { label: 'Enter an option.' } }; const o = { id: 'o' + ++seq.current, label: draft.label.trim() }; setOptions(list => [...list, o]); return { ok: true, value: o }; }}
        addLabel="Add option" onReorder={setOptions}
        onRemove={o => setOptions(list => list.filter(x => x.id !== o.id))}
        removal={{ kind: 'confirm', title: o => `Delete “${o.label}” permanently?`, description: () => 'Responses that chose it keep their answer, but it can’t be chosen again.', confirmLabel: 'Delete option' }}
        empty={<A.EmptyState size="sm" title="No options yet" description="Add the answers people can choose from." />} />
    </section>
  </div>;
}

=== PropertyList | <!-- @dsCard group="Display" height=700 subtitle="A record's properties, each edited where it sits; unset values are openings" -->
--- css
.pl-demo { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(min(280px, 100%), 1fr)); gap: 40px; max-width: 860px; }
.pl-demo h3 { margin: 0 0 12px; font: 600 15px/22px var(--font-sans); color: var(--ink); }
.pl-demo .pl-side { max-width: 280px; }
--- jsx
const PL_STATUS = [{ value: 'todo', label: 'To do' }, { value: 'progress', label: 'In progress' }, { value: 'review', label: 'In review' }, { value: 'done', label: 'Done' }];
const PL_TONE = { todo: 'neutral', progress: 'accent', review: 'caution', done: 'positive' };
const PL_PEOPLE = [{ value: 'mara', label: 'Mara Quist' }, { value: 'ines', label: 'Inés Ortega' }, { value: 'tobi', label: 'Tobi Adeyemi' }];
const PL_PRIORITY = [{ value: 'low', label: 'Low' }, { value: 'medium', label: 'Medium' }, { value: 'high', label: 'High' }];
const plWait = ms => new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
const plLabel = (list, v) => list.find(o => o.value === v)?.label;
function App() {
  const [issue, setIssue] = React.useState({ status: 'review', assignee: 'mara', priority: '', estimate: 3, created: '21 Oct 2026' });
  const [trip, setTrip] = React.useState({ name: 'Mara Quist', email: 'mara@northwind.example', start: '2026-11-02', end: '2026-11-06', phone: '' });
  const set = key => v => setIssue(i => ({ ...i, [key]: v }));
  const save = key => async draft => { await plWait(250); if (key === 'status' && draft === 'done' && !issue.assignee) return { ok: false, error: 'Assign someone before marking it done.' }; return { ok: true, value: draft }; };
  const tripSet = key => v => setTrip(t => ({ ...t, [key]: v }));
  const tripSave = async draft => { await plWait(200); return { ok: true, value: draft }; };
  return <div className="pl-demo">
    <section className="pl-side">
      <h3 id="pl-issue">Issue details</h3>
      <A.PropertyList aria-labelledby="pl-issue" shortcuts>
        <A.PropertyListItem label="Status" value={issue.status} shortcut="s" commit="on-choose" onSave={save('status')} onValueChange={set('status')}
          renderValue={s => <A.Badge tone={PL_TONE[s]}>{plLabel(PL_STATUS, s)}</A.Badge>} editor={c => <A.Select {...c} items={PL_STATUS} size="sm" />} />
        <A.PropertyListItem label="Assignee" value={issue.assignee} shortcut="a" commit="on-choose" onSave={save('assignee')} onValueChange={set('assignee')}
          renderValue={p => plLabel(PL_PEOPLE, p)} editor={c => <A.Combobox {...c} items={PL_PEOPLE} size="sm" />} />
        <A.PropertyListItem label="Priority" value={issue.priority} emptyLabel="Add priority" commit="on-choose" onSave={save('priority')} onValueChange={set('priority')}
          renderValue={p => plLabel(PL_PRIORITY, p)} editor={c => <A.Select {...c} items={PL_PRIORITY} size="sm" />} />
        <A.PropertyListItem label="Estimate" value={issue.estimate} onSave={save('estimate')} onValueChange={set('estimate')} required
          validate={n => Number.isInteger(n) && n >= 0 ? undefined : 'Use a whole number, 0 or more.'} renderValue={n => `${n} points`}
          editor={({ value, onValueChange, ...c }) => <A.Input {...c} type="number" size="sm" value={value ?? ''} onChange={e => onValueChange(e.target.value === '' ? null : Number(e.target.value))} />} />
        <A.PropertyListItem label="Created" value={issue.created} />
      </A.PropertyList>
    </section>
    <section>
      <h3 id="pl-trip">Check your answers</h3>
      <A.PropertyList aria-labelledby="pl-trip" layout="inline"
        editAll={{ label: 'trip details', value: trip, onValueChange: setTrip,
          validate: d => d.end < d.start ? { end: 'The last day must be on or after the first.' } : undefined,
          onSave: async d => { await plWait(250); return { ok: true, value: d }; },
          renderEditor: field => <>
            <A.Field label="Name" error={field.error('name')}><A.Input {...field('name')} /></A.Field>
            <A.Field label="Email" error={field.error('email')}><A.Input {...field('email')} type="email" /></A.Field>
            <A.Field label="First day" error={field.error('start')}><A.Input {...field('start')} type="date" /></A.Field>
            <A.Field label="Last day" error={field.error('end')}><A.Input {...field('end')} type="date" /></A.Field>
            <A.Field label="Phone"><A.Input {...field('phone')} type="tel" /></A.Field>
          </> }}>
        <A.PropertyListItem label="Name" value={trip.name} onSave={tripSave} onValueChange={tripSet('name')} required />
        <A.PropertyListItem label="Email" value={trip.email} onSave={tripSave} onValueChange={tripSet('email')} required />
        <A.PropertyListItem label="Dates" value={`${trip.start} to ${trip.end}`} />
        <A.PropertyListItem label="Phone" value={trip.phone} emptyLabel="Add phone number" onSave={tripSave} onValueChange={tripSet('phone')} />
      </A.PropertyList>
    </section>
  </div>;
}
